patippurimi aṟṟatu

inta ākkattuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭiyavarkaḷ, ulakaḷaḷāviya potup payaṉpāṭṭukku eṉa patippurimic caṭṭattukku uṭpaṭṭu, taṅkaḷ aṉittup patippurimikaḷiyum viṭuvittuḷḷaṉar.

nīṅkaḷ ivvākkattip paṭiyeṭukkalām; mēmpaṭuttalām; pakiralām; vēṟu vaṭivamāka māṟṟalām; vaṇikap payaṉkaḷum aṭiyalām. ivaṟṟukku nīṅkaḷ opputal ētum kōrat tēviyilli.

﻿muṉṉuri

uṭalik kākkum kaliyil, uṭaṟkalviyāṉatu ulakil muṉṉiliyil niṟkiṟatu.

‘eṅku cuṟṟiṉālum araṅkaṉi cēvikka varavēṇṭum'. eṉpatu oru paḻamoḻi

atupōlavē, āyiram kalikaḷ tōṉṟiṉālum, aṟputamāka viññāṉa muṉṉēṟṟatti nikaḻttiṉālum, attaṉi cukattiyum aṉupavikka, ātāramāka uṭaltāṉē amintirukkiṟatu. ataṟkāka uṭaṟkalviyiṭam ellōrumē vantutāṉ āka vēṇṭum.

uṭal nalintāl, ulaka vāḻvē kulintu pōkum. ūṟukiṉṟa ñāṉaṅkaḷum, ñāyaṅkaḷum aḻintē pōkum.

appaṭi nērntu viṭṭāl, akilattu makkaḷi aṉucaraṉiyuṭaṉ kākka, aṉpuṭaṉ vaḻikāṭṭum ariya kalviyākavē uṭaṟkalvi tikaḻkiṟatu; makiḻkiṟatu.

kuḻantikaḷi, māṇavarkaḷi, vāliparkaḷi, vayōtikarkaḷi eṉṟu vayatu vāriyākap pirittu; iṉam vāriyākat tokuttu, ēṟṟavarkaḷukku ēṟṟa muṟiyil pōṟṟum vakiyil uṭaṟ kalvi utavi uṟutuṇiyāka niṟkiṟatu.

uṭalukku vaḷamum valimiyum vēṇṭumā? vāḻvukkut tempum tiṭamaṉatum vēṇṭumā? vantu tontaravu tarum nōykaḷi viraṭṭiyaṭikka vēṇṭumā? varumuṉṉarē oḻikka vēṇṭumā?

uṭaṟkalvi pala upāyaṅkaḷik kāṭṭukiṟatu. uṇmiyāṉa ārōkkiyatti aḷittu āṉantatti vaḷarttu, aticayamāka vāḻa vaḻi kāṭṭukiṟatu.

appaṭippaṭṭa uṭaṟkalviyit tāṉ. evvāṟu kaṟpikka ﻿vēṇṭum? eṉṉeṉṉa muṟikaḷ? eṉṉeṉṉa vaḻikaḷ eṉṟu teḷivāka, tiṭṭavaṭṭamāka, innūlil kūṟiyuḷḷēṉ.

eḷiya muṟikaḷ kaṟpikkum iṉiya vaḻikaḷ payaṉ vantu viḷiyum pāṅkukaḷ etirnōkkum ilaṭciyap pātikaḷ ellāvaṟṟiyum eḷiya iṉiya tamiḻil tantirukkiṟēṉ.

inta nūli acciṭṭa kirēs piriṇṭars, ākka pūrvamāṉa paṇikaḷ āṟṟiya tiruvāḷarkaḷ ār. āṭam cākraṭṭīs, eṉ. ci. rāj mōkaṉ aṉivarukkum eṉ pārāṭṭukkaḷ.

arumiyāṉa inta nūli, aṟivōṭu payaṉpaṭutti, varuṅkāla camutāyatti, vaḷamākka muṉiyum uṭaṟkalvi āciriyarkaḷukkum, uṭaṟkalviyil īṭupāṭuḷḷa aṉittup perumakkaḷukkum, eṉ aṉpāṉa vāḻttukkaḷiyum vaṇakkattiyum terivittuk koḷkiṟēṉ.

'lilli pavaṉam'

ceṉṉi-600 017





aṉpuṭaṉ

ṭākṭar es. navarāj celliyā

﻿1. pāṭap poruḷum kaṟpikkum muṟiyum

(MATTER AND METHODS)



* * *





kaṟpikkum kali

kalviyik kaṟpippatu eṉpatu oru kali.

kaṟpikkum kāriyatti mēṟkoḷkiṉṟa āciriyar, ataṉi puṉitamāṉa paṇiyākavē, karuti perumitam poṅkap, paṇiyāṟṟukiṟār.

kalviyik kaliyākak kaṟpikkum āciriyari, kaliñaṉ eṉavum, pukaḻntu pēcukiṉṟārkaḷ.

kallic cetukki, taṉ kaṟpaṉiyuṭaṉ ciliyāka vaṭittuk kāṭṭukiṟa ciṟpiyippōla, maṉita uḷḷaṅkaḷi kalviyiṉ mūlam, ceppaṉiṭṭu. cīrmipaṭutti, ciṟappu nilikku uyarttukiṟār āciriyar.

ākavē tāṉ, nalla āciriyarkaḷ māṇavarkaḷukku nilik kaṇṇāṭiyāka viḷaṅkukiṉṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ maṉatilē āḻamāy patintu, kōpuramāy uyarntu, kuṉṟiliṭṭa tīpam pōla vaḻi kāṭṭi vāḻvikkiṉṟārkaḷ.

eṉavē tāṉ, kuruvāka tiruvāka, māṇavarkaḷ maṉatilē āciriyarkaḷ māṟāta ōriṭatti vakittu, perumiyaṭikiṉṟārkaḷ. pūrippaṭikiṉṟārkaḷ.

ivvāṟu ciṟappiṭattip peṟṟiṭa, āciriyarkaḷ taṅkaḷukkuḷḷāka, pala vaḻi muṟikaḷi vakuttukkoṇṭu, kuṟivaṟak kaṟṟut tantu, tāṅkaḷ etirpārkkum ciṟanta kuṇaṅkaḷi, nalaṉkaḷi māṇavarkaḷiṭam vaḷarttu viṭukiṉṟaṉar.

ippaṭiyāka mēṟkoḷkiṉṟa vaḻimuṟikaḷittāṉ kaṟpikkum muṟikaḷ eṉṟu kuṟittuk kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉar vallunarkaḷ.

kaṟpikkum kaliyum niliyum

kaṟpikkum muṟi eṉpatu acintu koṭukkāta kuṉṟu pōṉṟatu alla. nilimikkēṟpa nekiḻntu koṭuttu, tēvikkēṟpa vaḷintu tantu, viḷivukkēṟpa māṟik koṇṭum, māṟṟik koṇṭum, utavukiṟa uṉṉata muṟiyākum.

vakuppaṟik kalviyāṉa potukkalvi, col viḷakkattiṉ mūlam kaṟpikkappaṭukiṟatu. viḷiyāṭṭut tiṭalil kaṟpikkappaṭum uṭaṟkalviyāṉatu, colviḷakkam, ceyalviḷakkam, paṅku peṟum uṭal iyakkam eṉpatākak kaṟpikkappaṭukiṟatu.

inta muṟikaḷil īṭupaṭum āciriyariṉ nōkkam. eppaṭi amiyavēṇṭum eṉṟu mutalil kāṇpōm.

āciriyariṉ kaṟpikkum nōkkam

1. māṇavarkaḷukkuk kaṟkum cūḻniliyi uruvākkit tarutal.

2. māṇavarkaḷ kaṟka uṟcākappaṭuttutal.

3. māṇavarkaḷiṉ uṭalum maṉamum uṟutiyuṭaṉ vaḷara payiṟci tantu utavutal.

4. māṇavarkaḷiṉ ārvatti aṟintu, ataṟkēṟpa kaṟpittal.

5. māṇavarkaḷ kaṟpaṉic ceṟivuṭaṉ paṇiyāṟṟat tuṇṭutal.

6. naṟkuṇaṅkaḷ, nalluṇarvukaḷ, naṟceyalkaḷ āṟṟa uṟcākappaṭuttutal.

7. taṉṉampikkiyuṭaṉ ceyalpaṭum tuṇivi ūkkuvittal.

8. taṅkaḷāl eṉṉa ceyya muṭiyum eṉpati uṇarac ceytu, avarkaḷi antat tuṟiyil celutti, utavutal.

ittakiya ciṟappukkaḷi vaḷarkkum muṉippuṭaṉ paṇiyāṟṟum āciriyarkaḷ, tāṅkaḷ kaṟpikkiṉṟa pāṭap poruḷi, evvitam kaṟpikka vēṇṭum eṉpataṟku, cila vaḻimuṟikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. appaṭippaṭṭa kaṟpikkum muṟikaḷ paṟṟi iṅkē kāṇpōm.

1. pāṭapporuḷum pāṭamuṟiyum

āciriyar tāṉ kaṟpikkappōkiṉṟa pāṭattiṟku ēṟpavē, pāṭamuṟiyip pakuttuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

﻿varalāṟṟup pāṭam eṉkiṟa pōtu vāyc col viḷakkam. kaṇakkup pāṭam eṉkiṟa pōtu, karumpalaki eḻuttu iyakkam. uṭaṟpayiṟci eṉkiṟa pōtu, naṭimuṟi ceyal iyakkam.

ippaṭiyāka, eṭuttuk koḷkiṟa pāṭattiṟku ēṟpa, mēṟkoḷkiṟa muṟiyi, muṟippaṭuttik koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

2. māṇavarkaḷ taram aṟital

vakuppaṟikkuc celvataṟku muṉṉatākavē, āciriyariṉ poṟuppu ārampamākiviṭukiṟatu

māṇavarkaḷiṉ kaṟkum tiṟaṉ, ēṟkaṉavē kaṟṟirukkum aḷavu; antak kuṟippiṭṭap pāṭattil avarkaḷiṉ aṉupava aṟivu ārvam potuvāka anta vakuppiṉ naṭatti, kalviyil mēmpāṭu, nikaḻttiya cātaṉi; ētāvatu, piracciṉi uṇṭā eṉpaṉavaṟṟi aṟintu, ataṟkēṟpa taṉatu kaṟpikkum kāriyatti āciriyar toṭaṅka vēṇṭum. toṭara vēṇṭum.

3. paṭippikkum cūḻnili

māṇavarkaḷukkuk kaṟpikkiṉṟa cūḻnili, eppoḻutum oṉṟu pōlavēamintu viṭuvatilli.

iṭattiṟku iṭam, nērattiṟku nēram, māṇavarkku māṇavar cūḻnili māṟiyē amintuviṭum.

kaṟpikka utavum utavip poruṭkaḷ camayattil illāmal pōkalām. vakuppukku māṇavarkaḷ, etirpārāta eṇṇikkiyil atikamāka varalām. paḷḷic ﻿cūḻniliyil pāṭam naṭattak kūṭiya amiti kūṭa. illāmal irukkalām.

ivaṟṟiyellām aṉucarittuk koṇṭu, kaṟpittalit taḷarāmal, iṉimi cūḻa toṭara vēṇṭum.

4. pāṭamum nēramum

pāṭatti naṭattuvataṟkēṟṟavāṟu niṟiya kuṟippukkaḷ eṭuttu vittiruntālum; tayārittatik kaṟpikka, atika ārvam koṇṭiruntālum, pōtiya nēram kiṭikkāmal pōkum cūḻnili ēṟpaṭum.

ataṉāl atiriyamō āttiramō aṭiyāmal, pakkuvamāka nēram aṟintu, niṉittatik kaṟpikkum. cāturyam vēṇṭum.

5. māṇavarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ

taṅkaḷ peyarkaḷi āciriyar kūṟi aḻikkumpoḻutu, māṇavarkaḷukku makiḻcciyum. perumiyum ēṟpaṭum. appoḻutu avarkaḷ āciriyar mēl matippum mariyātiyum perukiṭa, avar kūṟukiṟa karuttukkaḷik kūrntu kēṭṭu kaṟṟiṭuvārkaḷ. atuvē āciriyariṉ veṟṟiyāka amintu viṭukiṟatu.

6. poṟumiyum tiṟamiyum

āciriyar aṭikkaṭi kōpappaṭuvatu, vakuppuc cūḻnili yiyē māṟṟi viṭum ōr acātāraṇa cūḻniliyiyum ēṟpaṭuttiviṭum. kōpappaṭukiṟapōtu, putticālit taṉamāka, karuttōṭu pōtikkiṟa kavaṉam kaṭṭuk kulintu pōkiṟatu. eṉavē, poṟumiyik kaṭipiṭikkumpōtu. tiṟamiyum kūṭum. tērcciyum perukum.

7. māṇavarkaḷiṉ taṉmiyaṟintu!

uṭaṟkalviyi pōtikkiṟapōtu, māṇavarkaḷiṉ kūṭṭatti maṭṭum matippiṭṭu viṭakkūṭātu. avarkaḷ tōṟṟattiyum, uṭal amippiyum, uḷḷattiṉ iyalpukaḷiyum uṇarntu, avaṟṟiṟku ēṟpa, kaṟpittu viṭavēṇṭum.

(a) uṭalamippu karuttukkaḷ (Anatomy & Physiology)

1. vayatukkēṟpa payiṟcikaḷi aḷikka vēṇṭum.

2. āṇ peṇ vēṟupāṭukku ēṟpa, payiṟciyiṉ aḷavu vēṇṭum.

3. entap payiṟcikkum muṉṉatāka, uṭalip patappaṭuttum payiṟcikaḷi ceytiṭa, kaṭṭāyappaṭutta vēṇṭum.

4. kaḷippu uṇṭāvataṟku muṉ, payiṟcikaḷi muṭittu viṭavēṇṭum.

(ā) uḷaviyal karuttukkaḷ (Psychology)

1. payiṟciyil īṭupaṭa, ārvatti mutalil ūṭṭa vēṇṭum.

2. oru kāriyattik kaṟṟuk koḷḷa, ceyalpaṭukiṟa pōtu tāṉ ciṟappākap peṟa muṭiyum.

3. tirumpat tirumpa payiṟci ceykiṟa pōtu, atil tērcciyum vaḷarcciyum niṟiya ēṟpaṭum.

4. māṇavarkaḷ virumpātati vaṟpuṟuttāmal, ēṟṟuk koḷḷac ceytiṭa muyaṉṟu, veṟṟi peṟṟa piṟaku, kaṭiṉap payiṟcikaḷi ceyya vikkavum.

5. kāriyamāṟṟum ārvam, viruppam, muyaṟci, muṉippu ellām vayatukkēṟpa māṟupaṭum eṉpatāl, ataṉiyum naṉṟāka aṟintu kaṟpikkavum.

camūkaviyal karuttukkaḷ (Sociology)

1. maṉitaṉ kūṭi vāḻum camūka amippic cērntavaṉ.

2. camūkattil avaṉ vāḻntālum, taṉatu taṉit taṉmiyi iḻakkāmal vāḻkiṟāṉ.

3. taṉatu urimiyi iḻakkāmal, maṟṟavarkaḷ urimiyiyum paṟikkāmal, taṉakkum uḷḷa poṟuppukkaḷi uṇarntu, paṅkiṭṭuk koṇṭu, pakkuvamāka vāḻkiṟāṉ.

4. camūkattil oṟṟumiyum uṇṭu. pōṭṭikaḷum uṇṭu. iraṇṭumē muṉṉēṟṟattiṟku iru kaṇkaḷ pōṉṟaviyākum.

5. ēṟṟattāḻvaṟṟa camutāyatti amikka, viḷiyāṭṭum āṭukaḷaṅkaḷum utavukiṉṟaṉa.

ippaṭippaṭṭa karuttukkaḷi purintu koṇṭu, āciriyarkaḷ kaṟpikkum pōtu, etirpārtta naṉmikaḷ ellāmē kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa.





﻿2. kaṟpittalil vaḷarcciyum eḻucciyum

(TEACHING PROCESS)



* * *



kalviyiṉ taramum perumiyum, taramuḷḷa āciriyarkaḷiṉ kaṟpikkum tiṟamiyil tāṉ amintirukkiṟatu.

kaṟpittal eṉpatu cātāraṇa kāriyamalla

ētō vakuppukku muṉṉē vantu niṉṟu, māṇavarkaḷiṭam puttakattil uḷḷa pāṭattik kūṟi, pariṭci vittu matippeṇkaḷ aḷikkiṉṟa, aṉṟāṭa naṭimuṟi ceyalkaḷum alla kaṟpittal eṉpatu.

kaṟpittal eṉpatu ētō entiram pōl iyaṅki ceyalpaṭuvatum alla, atu tantira nuṇukkam niṟinta, cāturyam mikunta, etir nīccal pōṭac ceykiṉṟa, ki vanta kaliyākum.

māṇavarkaḷ kūṭṭattiṉ muṉṉē vāyppantal pōṭum vēliyalla kaṟpittal eṉpatu. māṇavarkaḷiṉ aṟivu pūrvamāṉa, (Intellectual), uṇarvu pūrvamāṉa (Emotional) camūkat toṭarpāṉa (Social): ātmārtta māṉa (Spiritual) maṟṟum uṭal vaḷarccikkāka utavum vakiyil tāṉ, kaṟpikkum kāriyam naṭi peṟukiṟatu.



putiya kalvik koḷkiyiṉpaṭi, kaṟpikkum paṇi mūṉṟu mukkiya nōkkattuṭaṉ piṉpaṟṟappaṭukiṟatu.

1. kaṟpavari valiyuṟuttal (Emphasising the learner)

2. kaṟpavarukku vaḻi kāṭṭutal (Guiding the learner)

3. kaṟpavari muṉṉēṟṟutal (promoting the learner)

1. kaṟpavari valiyuṟuttal

kaṟpikkum poḻutu, aṅku mukkiya iṭatti vakippavarkaḷ māṇavarkaḷākavē irukkiṉṟārkaḷ.

vakuppilē pāṭṭup pāṭuvatu, vāy viḷakkam taruvatu; maṉappāṭam ceytu oppuvippatu pōṉṟu naṭantatu ellām, iṉṟu maliyēṟip pōy viṭṭatu.

māṟāka, pōtikkum poruḷ toṭarpāka, vaḻi muṟikaḷ mūlamāka, māṇavarkaḷiṉ tiṟamikaḷi vaḷarttu viṭukiṟa ilaṭciyap pāṅkē, iṉṟu tali tūkki niṟkiṟatu

māṇavarkaḷiṉ uṇmiyāṉa tiṟaṉkaḷi uṇarntu koṇṭu, avaṟṟi vaḷarttu viṭukiṟa muṟikaḷik kiyāṇṭu; avarkaḷukku aṟivārnta aṉupavaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭa vaḻivakuttut tantu; atikamākak kaṟṟuk koḷḷa valiyuṟuttum ēṟpāṭukaḷ tām iṉṟiya kaṟpikkum muṟiyiṉ taliyāya nōkkamākat tikaḻkiṟatu.

2. kaṟpavarkku vaḻi kāṭṭutal

kaṟpippavarukku oru nōkkam maṟṟum kaṭami iruppatu. pōla, kaṟpavarukkum irukkiṟatu.

āciriyar kūṟukiṟa karuttukkaḷiyum viḷakkaṅkaḷiyum kēṭkiṟa pōtu maṭṭum, māṇavarkaḷiṉ tiṟami vaḷarnatu viṭuvatilli.

kēṭṭa col viḷakkattiṟkēṟpa, ceyal iyakkattiṟku uṭpaṭuttiṉāl tāṉ, avarkaḷiṉ tiṟami vaḷarkiṟatu. aṟivu mikuti peṟukiṟatu.

pommiyi āṭṭuvikkum pōtu atu aḻakāka irukkiṟatu. māṇavarkaḷi ceyal iyakkattil īṭupaṭuttum poḻutu, avarkaḷiṉ eṇṇam teḷivāka irukkiṟatu. tiṟami valivāka māṟukiṟatu. tērcciyum pala tavaṟukaḷik kaṭantu, eḻucci peṟukiṟatu.

ākavē, āciriyar, taṉatu tiṭṭaṅkaḷuṭaṉ, vaṭivamittuḷḷa ceyalamippukaḷuṭaṉ, māṇavarkaḷi ceyalpaṭa uṟcākappaṭutti, vaḻikāṭṭum paṇiyil kaṭamiyic ceykiṟavarākiṟār.

anta vaḻikāṭṭum paṇiyil, māṇavarkaḷatu ceyaltiṟaṉ, toḻil muṉṉēṟṟam, tiṟamiyiṉ nīrōṭṭam aṉittiyum aṟintu koḷḷa, āciriyarāl muṭiyum. āmām niccayamāka muṭikiṟatu.

ākavē, āciriyarkaḷ eṉpavarkaḷ, kaṟpikkiṟa pōtu. vēli vāṅkum mēstiriyāka illāmal, vaḻi kāṭṭum tuṇiyākavē viḷaṅkukiṟār. vaḷam kūṭṭukiṟār eṉpatāka viḷaṅka vēṇṭum.

3. kaṟpavari muṉṉēṟṟutal

ovvoru kuḻantiyum ataṟkeṉṟu taṉiyāṉa kuṇa nalaṉkaḷ, kūṭivarum tiṟaṉ vaḷaṅkaḷ koṇṭaviyākavē tikaḻkiṟatu.

antat tiṟaṉum kuṇamum, antak kuḻantiyiṉ vaḷarum cūḻnilikku ēṟpavē peruki vaḷarkiṟatu.

antak kuḻantiyiṉ kaṟkum ārvam: ārvam ceyal vaṭivam peṟa āciriyariṉ ālōcaṉi: aṭuttut toṭarkiṉṟa ceyalākkam. ippaṭittāṉ māṇavak kuḻantikaḷiṉ muṉṉēṟṟattip paṭippaṭiyāka vaḷarttuk kāṭṭa muṭiyum.

māṇavarkaḷukku iyaṟkiyākavē vaḷarcci uṇṭu. anta vaḷarcciyi aṉupavaṅkaḷ mūlamāka, vaḷarttu viṭuvatē āciriyariṉ kaṟpikkum paṇiyāka amintirukkiṟatu.

muṭiyāta aḷavukkuk kaṟpaṉiyik kūṭṭi vittuk koṇṭu kāriyamāṟṟac colvatum; tiṭṭavaṭṭamāṉa muṭivu illāmal, kuḻappattuṭaṉ ceyalpaṭac colvatum. vaḷarcci taruvataṟkup patilāka, vaḷarcciyit taṭuttu viṭukiṟatu. cila camayaṅkaḷil keṭuttum viṭukiṟatu.

ākavē, māṇavarkaḷukku etu tēvi, etu eḷitu. etu iṉimiyāṉa aṉupavaṅkaḷik koṭukkum, etu ēṟṟa muṉṉēṟṟatti aḷikkum eṉpaṉavaṟṟi āyntu, aṟintu, teḷintu, tērntu, tiṭṭavaṭṭamākat tarukiṟapōtu tāṉ, tiraṇṭa palaṉkaḷip peṟa muṭiyum eṉpati āciriyarkaḷ eṇṇippārttu ceyalpaṭa vēṇṭum.

āciriyariṉ ciṟappāṉa intap paṇikku utavum cila kuṟippukkaḷik kāṇpōm.

1. vakuppi ciṟappāka naṭattutal.



2. kaṟkum māṇavarkaḷukku kaṟṟalil ārvam ūṭṭi uṟcākappaṭuttutal.

3. māṇavarkaḷ virumpum vaṇṇam ceymuṟi kāriyaṅkaḷit tērnteṭuttal.

4. avaṟṟi naṭi muṟippaṭuttiṭa culapamākak kaṟpikkum muṟikaḷit tērnkeṭuttal.

5. inta ceyalāl, iṉṉa viḷivukaḷ nikaḻum eṉṟu etirpārttu, tiṭṭamiṭṭuk kaṟṟuttarutal.

6. iṟutiyil ēṟpaṭṭa muṭivukaḷ paṟṟi, matippīṭu ceytal.

ippaṭit tiṭṭamiṭukiṟapōta, kaṟpikkum muṟiyil, āciriyarkaḷ iraṇṭu karuttukkaḷi maṉatil koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

1. taṉippaṭṭa māṇavarkaḷiṉ uṭal, vayatu, iṉam, paṟṟiya vēṟupāṭukaḷi aṟintu, ataṟkēṟpa kaṟpittali valiyuṟuttal.

2. taṉippaṭṭa aṉivariyum oṉṟu kūṭṭi; ōr amippāka iyaṅka; anta amippu camutāya, amippāka piṉṉāḷil uruvāvatu pōla, ceyalpaṭa vittal.

inta iraṇṭuk karuttukkaḷiṉ piṉṉaṇiyil, cutantaramāka vāḻum tattuvamum; cukam tarum vāḻvut tattuvamum aṭaṅkik kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa.

ākavē, māṇavarkaḷukkuk kaṭiṉamāṉa karuttukkaḷiyum kaṉipōla cuvikkumāṟu eḷitākki, iṉitākkit tantu; māṇavarkaḷiṉ tiṟaṉkaḷukku ēṟpa ceyalkaḷit tuvakki, nāḷukku nāḷ vaḷarttu; niṟinta palaṉkaḷi viḷittut taruvatē kaṟpikkum kaliyāka viḷaṅkukiṟatu.

antak kaṟpikkum vaḻiyiltāṉ vaḷarcciyum eḻucciyum mikuntu, merukēṟiya muṉṉēṟṟatti vaḻaṅkukiṟatu eṉpati āciriyarkaḷ, eṉṟum niṉivil koṇṭu, aṟpikka vēṇṭum.





﻿3. kaṟṟalil vaḷarcciyum eḻucciyum

(PSYCHOLOGY OF LEARNING)



* * *



kalviyiṉ nōkkam

kalviyiṉ nōkkamāṉatu, kuḻantikaḷ naṭattiyil cila vicēṣamāṉa, viruppamāṉa māṟ ṟaṅkaḷi ēṟpaṭuttuvatu tāṉ.

anta māṟṟaṅkaḷ nikaḻvatu, avarkaḷ peṟum aṉupavaṅkaḷāl tāṉ.

aṉupavaṅkaḷ eṉpatu, avarkaḷ tēvikaḷit tīrttuk koḷḷa muyalukiṟapōtum; taṅkaḷ ārvaṅkaḷi niṟivēṟṟik koḷkiṟapōtum, ēṟpaṭukiṟa nikaḻccikaḷiṉ tokuppākum

oruvaratu naṭattiyil, eṇṇaṅkaḷil, naṭimuṟiyil, uṇarccikaḷil ēṟpaṭukiṟa māṟṟaṅkaḷik koṇṭē, avar kaṟṟuk koṇṭirukkiṟār eṉṟu aṟiya muṭiyum eṉkiṟār oru mēlnāṭṭaṟiñar.

kaṟkum toṭarnili

kaṟṟal eṉpatu ovvoruvar vāḻkkiyilum, ēṟpaṭukiṉṟa toṭar nikaḻcciyākum.



ovvoruvarum taṅkaḷukku ēṟpaṭukiṉṟa cūḻnilik kēṟpa, ceyalpaṭumpoḻutē, kaṟṟuk koḷḷavum ceykiṉṟār.

appaṭik kaṟṟuk koḷkiṟapōtu, aṟivāl vaḷarkiṟār. ātmārttamākavum uyarntu koḷkiṟār.

ivvāṟu kaṟkum toṭar nili eṉpatu, pala kāraṇaṅkaḷāl nikaḻkiṟatu. niṟivu kāṇukiṟatu.

1. kaṟpavariṉ tēvikaḷ (Needs of the Learner)

ovvoruvarum taṅkaḷ tēvikaḷukku ēṟpa, ceyalpaṭukiṟa etirviṉiyiṉ kāraṇamāka, kaṟṟuk koḷpavarākiṟār.

oruvariṉ tēvi atikamākumpōtu, allatu cūḻnili kaṭiṉamākavō. koṭumiyākavō nērkiṟa pōtu, avariṉ ceyalum cintaṉiyum mēlum virivaṭikiṟatu. avarukkuk kaṟkum tiṟami mikuti peṟukiṟatu.

ākavē, 'tēvikaḷē kaṇṭupiṭippiṉ tāy' eṉpatu pōla, tēvikaḷum, ārvamum, āciyum, oruvari muṉippuṭaṉ īṭupaṭac ceykiṉṟaṉa. pāṭupaṭa vikkiṉṟaṉa. tēṭit tirintu tīrttuk koḷḷum aṟivi virivupaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. ceṟivupaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa.

2. kaṟpataṟkut tayār nili (Readiness to learn)

kaṟka vēṇṭum eṉṟu ēṟpaṭukiṉṟa ārvamē, ciṟappākak kaṟka utavukiṟatu. utvēkam aḷikkiṟatu. uṟcākam ūṭṭukiṟatu.

kaṟpavar kaṟkat tayār niliyil illi eṉṟāl, aṅkē kaṟṟal naṭipeṟātu. kacappāṉa uṇarvukaḷē kāl parappik kiṭakkum.

nōy vanta pōtum, uṭal uṟuppukkaḷ paḻutaṭikiṟa pōtum; vīṭṭil, camutāyattil avarukku cumuka nili illāta pōtum, oruvāta kaṟkum maṉaniliyil tēkkam piṟakkiṟatu. ūkkattiyum iḻakkiṟatu.

ākavē, kaṟpavar maṉanili, uṭalnili, uṟcāka nili, ilaṭciyanili ivikaḷē kaṟkat tūṇṭum.

3. vāyppukaḷ vaḻaṅkum cūḻnili (situation)

cuṟṟuppuṟa cūḻnili pōla, kaṟṟalukkum uriya cariyāṉa cantarppaṅkaḷ, kaṟṟalil vēkattiyum, nalla tarattiyum vaḻaṅkukiṟatu.

ākavē, āciriyarkaḷ, māṇavarkaḷ kaṟpataṟkuriya nalla cūḻniliyi mutalil uruvākkit taravēṇṭum.

vīṭṭil, camutāya amippil, paḷḷikaḷiṉ cuṟṟup puṟattil, putitu putitākak kaṟṟuk koḷḷak kūṭiya vāyppukaḷ niṟiya uṇṭu. eṉṟālum, ciṟappākak kaṟṟuk koḷḷum tiṟaṉ, māṇavarkaḷukku ēṟpaṭuttik koṭukkiṟa cūḻniliyip poṟuttum, atu vaḻaṅkukiṟa vāyppip poṟuttumē amikiṟatu.

4. ceyalpaṭum vitamum vēkamum (Interaction)

māṇavarkaḷiṉ tēvikaḷum, avarkaḷ koṇṭirukkum ilaṭciya nōkkum ēṟpaṭuttukiṟa tūṇṭutalkaḷukku ēṟpa, avarkaḷiṉ ceyalpaṭum vitamum, vēkamum amintuviṭum.

tēvikaḷit taṭukkum taṭikaḷum, etirppukaḷum, avarkaḷiṉ aṟivi nuṇukkamākap payaṉpaṭuttuvataṉ mūlam, putitu putitākak kaṟṟuk koḷḷum vitatti, vēkappaṭuttukiṟatu. vivēkappaṭuttukiṟatu. tiruptipaṭuttu kiṟatu. tīrkkamāka cintikkat tūṇṭukiṟatu.

ittakiya ceyalpaṭum tūṇṭutalum, ceyalpāṭum, kaṟpaṉiyi, aṟivucceṟivi, kaṭṭuppaṭutti, vaḷarttu, kaṇakkākak kāriyamāṟṟat tuṇipurikiṟatu.

ippaṭippaṭṭa iṉiya kaṟkum viḷivukaḷi ēṟpaṭuttum vēṟu cila tūṇṭutalkaḷum uṇṭu. avi piṉvarumāṟu.

1. cuṟkum viruppatti ūkkuvittal (Motivation).

2. maṉatukkuḷḷē pātukāppāṉa oru eṇṇam vaḷarum vakiyil uṟcākam aḷittal.(Psychological Safety)

3. paṅku peṟac ceytu, āyvu ceyya utavi, avarkaḷiṉ ārvatti niṟivēṟṟi vittal. (Experimentation)

4. kaṟṟuk koṇṭē varukiṟapōtu, muṉṉar kaṟṟati niṉivupaṭutti, mēlum kaṟka, putiyatik kaṟka tuṇipurital. (Feedback)

5. payiṟciyē pakkuvatti uṇṭākkuvatāl tirumpat tirumpa, payiṟci ceyya utavutal. (Practice).

6. kaṟṟaliṉ taṉmiyaṟintu pārāṭṭi kuravittal.

ivvāṟu ellā vaḻikaḷilum kaṟkiṟapōtu, ēṟpaṭukiṟa iṉpam, eḻuttāl varuṇikka muṭiyāta oṉṟākum.

āṉālum, kaṟṟaliṉāl uṇṭākiṉṟa vaḷarcciyum eḻucciyum, kaṟpavarkku eppaṭi amikiṟatu eṉpatiyum nām kāṇpōm.

1. kaṟpatu nalla vaḷarcciyi nalkukiṟatu. atāvatu uṭalālum, uḷḷattālum, ēṟpaṭukiṟa vaḷarcciyē atu.

2. putitu putitākat tōṉṟum cuṟṟuppuṟa cūḻnilikkēṟpa nekiḻntum, vaḷintum, oṉṟiyum, otuṅkiyum, māṟṟiyum māṟiyum koḷkiṟa maṉappakkuvatti kaṟṟal koṭukkiṟatu.

3. kaṟṟal, nalla nirvākat tiṟaṉi vaḷarttu viṭukiṟatu. avvappōtu ēṟpaṭukiṟa putiya aṉupavam, paḻiya aṉupavam ivi tarum vaḷamē atu.

4. kaṟṟal nalla ilaṭciyattiyum, nalvāḻvu nōkkattiyum aḷikkiṟatu.

5. kaṟṟal aṟivuk kūrmiyi vaḷarttu viṭukiṟatu.

6. kaṟṟal oruvariṉ cuya tēviyi pūrtti ceytu koḷḷum tuṇiviyum, nampikkiyiyum - aḷikkiṟatu.

7. kaṟṟal nalla muṉṉuṇarum aṟivi viḷivittut tarukiṟatu.

ākavē, kaṟpatiṉāl, kaṟpavarukku nalla vaḷarcciyum, ēṟṟam tarum eḻucciyum eṭuppākak kiṭikkiṟatu. eṉkiṟa uṇmi nili ellām, ellōrukkum uḷḷaṅki nellikkaṉi pōl, teḷivākavē terikiṟatu.





4. kaṟpikka utavum tuṇip poruṭkaḷ

(TEACHING AIDS)



* * *



kaṟpikkum vaḻikaḷil, ceyaṟki muṟi, iyaṟki mūṟi eṉṟu muṉṉa tākak kūṟiyiruntōm.

ceyaṟkiyāṉa pōtikkum muṟi eṉpatu, vāy moḻi mūlamākavē viḷakkam aḷippatu. ceyaṟki mūṟiyāl kaṟkiṟa māṇavarkaḷukku maṟimukamāṉa aṉupavaṅkaḷē (Indirect Experience) kiṭikkiṟatu.

iyaṟkiyāṉa pōtaṉi muṟi eṉpatu, māṇavarkaḷ tāṅkaḷē nēraṭiyākap paṅku peṟṟu, kaṇṭu kēṭṭu, uṟṟu aṟivatākum māṇavarkaḷ cuṟṟulā celvatu pōṉṟa aṉupava muṟi.

ceyaṟki muṟiyil, ōr āciriyar kaṟpaṉiyil uruvākki, kaṉivārnta coṟkaḷip payaṉpaṭutti, kavarcciyāṉa vaḻiyil, kēṭpavar purintu koḷḷumāṟu kaṟpikkum paṇiyāṟṟukiṟār.

iyaṟki muṟi eṉpatu, ōr āciriyar iyaṟkiyāṉa piratēcaṅkaḷiyum, iṭam peṟṟirukkum cūḻnilip poruḷ kaḷiyum kāṭcip poruḷākak kāṭṭip pōtippatākum.

tāṉ kaṟpikkap pōkiṉṟa pāṭap poruḷukku ēṟpa, ōr āciriyar uṇmiyāṉa poruṭkaḷi nēril kāṭṭi, neñcil paṭumpaṭi niṉivil niṟutti, taṉ kaṟpittali mēmpaṭuttik kāṭṭa utavukiṉṟa poruṭkaḷiyē tuṇip poruṭkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟukiṟōm.

ippaṭippaṭṭa tuṇip poruṭkaḷi nām 3 pirivākap pirittu aṟiyalām.

1. camutāya cūḻnili (Community)

2. kiḷip pāṭat tiṭṭa ceyalkaḷ : (Co-curricular Activities)

3. kaṇṭum kēṭṭaṟiyum utavip poruṭkaḷ (Audio - visual aids)

1. camutāya cūḻnili

maṉitaṉi kūṭi vāḻum mirukam eṉpārkaḷ. maṉitarkaḷ kūṭi vāḻvatit tāṉ camutāya amippu eṉkiṉṟaṉar. taṉippaṭṭavarkaḷ taṅkaḷ urimikaḷik kāttuk koṇṭu, potumikaḷukkuk kaṭṭuppaṭṭu, oṉṟupaṭṭu, uṇarvālum ceyalālum kaṭṭuk kōppuṭaṉ vāḻvatiyē camutāyam eṉkiṟōm.

camutāyattiṉ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkāka cakalavitamāṉa ākka vēlikaḷ makkaḷāl mēṟkoḷḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. anta vēlikaḷē kaṭṭiṭamāka, kalā cāliyāka, kāṭciyakamāka amintu viṭukiṉṟaṉa.

attakiya iṭaṅkaḷi māṇavarkaḷ, nēril pārkkiṟa pōtu, avarkaḷāl uṇmiyi uṇara muṭikiṟatu.

avaciyatti aṟiya nērkiṟatu. aṟivārnta laṭciyaṅkaḷi purintu koḷḷa muṭikiṟatu.

ākavē, iyaṟkiyōṭu iyinta aṟivip peṟṟiṭa utavum camutāyattil, kāṇappaṭum cila tuṇip poruṭkaḷik kāṇpōm.

1. aṇikkaṭṭukaḷ 2. pālaṅkaḷ

3. poruṭ kāṭci cāli 4. toḻiṟ cāli

5. kalik kāṭciyakam 6. mīṉ kāṭciyakam

7. mirukak kāṭci cāli 8. kalaṅkari viḷakkam

9. tuṟi mukam 10. uṭaṟpayiṟci kūṭaṅkaḷ

11. viḷiyāṭṭup pōṭṭikaḷ 12. pūṅkākkaḷ

13. varalāṟṟuk kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ 14. vimāṉa niliyaṅkaḷ

15. aracu potu niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷ 16. miṉ niliyaṅkaḷ

ivaṟṟi nēril kāṇukiṟa pōtu, ērāḷamāṉa ceytikaḷi māṇavarkaḷāl tārāḷamākap peṟṟuk koḷḷa muṭikiṟatu.



2. kiḷip pāṭat tiṭṭa ceyalkaḷ

paḷḷikaḷil maṟṟum pāṭa niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷil, payilum māṇavarkaḷukku, palvēṟu vitamāṉa viruppukaḷ, ācikaḷ uṇṭu.

avarkaḷ āci niṟivēṟavum, ārvamuṭaṉ paṅku koḷaḷavum, pāṭat tiṭṭattiṟkēṟpa, pala kiḷikaḷ aṭaṅkiya iyakkaṅkaḷ iṭam peṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa.

cāṉṟāka, tēciya māṇavar paṭi; cāraṇar iyakkam; mukām vāḻkki iyakkam (Camping); naṭantu ceṉṟu iṭam pārttu makiḻum iyakkam (Hiking); viḷiyāṭṭu viḻākkaḷ; kuṭiyurimip payiṟci (Citizenship trining). cukātārak kaṇkāṭci iyakkam pōṉṟavaṟṟik kūṟalām.

māṇava māṇaviyar, tāṅkaḷē nērāka ittakiya iyakkaṅkaḷil paṅku peṟum pōtu, pal vēṟu vitamāṉa karuttukkaḷiyum, pāṅkāṉa aṟiviṉiyum peṟum vāyppu mikuti peṟukiṟatu.



3. kēṭṭu kaṇṭaṟiya utavum poruṭkaḷ.

ulaki aṟintiṭa utavuvatu nam aimpulaṉkaḷē. ariya cakti paṭitta aimpulaṉkaḷē, aṟivi vaḷarttiṭa tuṇipurikiṉṟaṉa.

kēṭṭu kaṇṭaṟiya utavum tuṇip poruṭkaḷ, kaṟpatil virivākat tērccipeṟa, mikutiyāka utavukiṉṟaṉa.

intat tuṇip poruṭkaḷ, aṟivu niṟiya peṟa utavumē aṉṟi, āciriyarē tēviyilli eṉṟa aḷavukku māṟṟiviṭa muṭiyātu.

māṇavarkku intat tuṇip poruṭkaḷ, nallatik kaṟṟut taravē amintiṭavēṇṭum. kāṇpavarkkuk kaḷippūṭṭukiṉṟa intat tuṇip poruṭkaḷ, māṇavarkaḷ maṉati vaḻimāṟṟiṭa alla eṉpati nām purintu koṇṭu, takunta muṟiyil payaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

namakku cātakamāka irukkavum, purintu koḷḷavum, kēṭṭuk kaṇṭaṟiya utavum tuṇip poruṭkaḷi 5 vakiyākap pirikkalām.

1. kātāl kēṭpataṟku utavum poruṭkaḷ

(AUDITORY)



1. rēṭiyō . 2. pōṉō kirāp (Phonograph) 3. icikkaruvikaḷ.

2 kaṇṇāl kāṇpataṟku utavum poruṭkaḷ

(VISUAL)



1. tolikkāṭcik karuvi.

2. kēmarā.

3. ciṉimā paṭam.

4. viḷakkappaṭaṅkaḷ: ōviyaṅkaḷ

5. cuvaroṭṭikaḷ: pukippaṭaṅkaḷ

6. tēcappaṭaṅkaḷ : kuḷōp

7. karumpalaki

3. kaṇṇukkum kātukkum utavum poruṭkaḷ (AUDIO - (VISUAL)

1. pēcum (ciṉimā) paṭam

2. tolikkāṭci.

4. ceyalpaṭa utavum tuṇip poruḷ (AIDES THROUGH ACTIVITY)

1. ullācap payaṇam:

2. naṭippayaṇam.

3. mātirip poruṭkaḷ, tuṇip poruṭkaḷik

kaṇṭaṟintu, poṟukkic cērttal. (Collection)

5. potuvāṉa (niliyāṉa) tuṇip poruṭkaḷ

1. nāṭakam naṭittal, (pāvaṉi ceytal)

2. puttakaṅkaḷ ; tiṉa, vāra, mātāntira pattirikkikaḷ; tuṇṭu veḷiyīṭukaḷ; pattiram pōṉṟa mūla āvaṇaṅkaḷ.

kēṭṭu aṟivati viṭa, pārttu peṟum aṟivu perum payaṉ payakkum. oru cittiratti āyiram vārttikaḷik koṇṭu viḷakkuvati viṭa, nēril oru muṟi kāṭṭiṉālē pōtum, niṟiya viṣayaṅkaḷi aṟintu koḷḷa muṭiyum.

intak karutti maṉatiṟ koṇṭu, āciriyar mēṟkūṟiya tuṇip poruṭkaḷi, mikutiyākap payaṉpaṭutta vēṇṭum. appoḻutu, āciriyar etirpārkkum kaṟṟal viḷivukaḷi, mika eḷitāka māṇavarkaḷiṭam peṟa muṭiyum oru cātaṉiyiyē nikaḻtta muṭiyum.





5. kaṟpittalum tayārikkum tiṟaṉ nuṇukkamum

(PERSONAL AND TECHNICAL PREPARATIONS)



* * *



kaliyum kaliñaṉum

kaṟpikkum tiṟaṉi kaliyamcam niṟintatu eṉṟu nām kūṟuvōmāṉāl, kaṟpikkum tiṟaṉ niṟintavari, nām. kaliñaṉ eṉṟē aḻittu makiḻalām.

oruvar evvaḷavu kaṟṟirukkiṟār eṉpatil perumiyilli. avar piṟarukkup puriyumpaṭi eppaṭi kaṟpikkiṟār eṉpatil tāṉ avaratu āṟṟalum perumiyum aṭaṅkiyirukkiṟatu.

appaṭippaṭṭa āṟṟaliyum ciṟappiyum peṟa, oruvar tammit takutiyuḷḷavarāka ākkik koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

piṟar pōṟṟa, virumpi ēṟṟuk kaṟṟuk koḷḷum eḻucci mikutiyākak kaṟpikkum tiṟaṉiyē kaṟpikkum tiṟaṉ muṟukkam eṉṟu kūṟukiṟōm. (Presentation Technique)

intat tiṟaṉ nuṇukka muṟiyi, ōr āciriyar iraṇṭu vakiyil ēṟpaṭuttik koḷḷalām.

1. tiṭṭa miṭutal (Planning)

2. teḷivāka vaḻaṅkutal (Presentation).

tayār nili

ōr āciriyar, tāṉ kaṟpikkap pōkiṉṟa pāṭattip paṟṟit teḷivākat terintu koḷḷa vēṇṭum. ataṟkēṟṟavāṟu aṟivārnta muṟiyil, antap pāṭap poruḷ paṟṟi, alaci ārāyntu, avaṟṟiṉ cārāmcattiyum, atu cārnta karuttukkaḷ paṟṟiyum, tayārittu, tayār niliyuṭaṉ tāṉ māṇavarkaḷiṭam cella vēṇṭum:

poruḷ purintu pōtikkum poḻututāṉ, kēṭpavarkaḷum kiḷarcciyuṭaṉum, makiḻcciyuṭaṉum kēṭka muṭiyum,

ivvāṟu tayār nilikku varakkūṭiya nuṇukkatti. iraṇṭu pirivākap pirittuk kāṭṭuvar. 4 1. tōṟṟattil tayār nili (Personal Preparation)

2. poruḷil nuṇukka nili (Technical Preparation)

tōṟṟattil tayār nili

vakuppukkuc celvataṟku muṉṉatāka, āciriyar taṉṉip pāṭaṅkaḷil, maṭṭum tayār ceytu koṇṭāl pōtātu. naṭi, uṭi, tōṟṟam, pāvaṉi, āḷumi, maṟṟum poḻivu pōṉṟavaṟṟil taṉikkavaṉam celuttuvatiyē, taṉ nilit tayār nili eṉṟu kūṟukiṟōm:

kāṇpavarkaḷukku makiḻcciyum, mariyātiyum ūṭṭak kūṭiya taṉmiyil aminta tūya nallāṭi, nimirnta naṭi taḷarāta āḷumi; piṟarik kavarntiḻukkum collāṟṟal; kaṉivāṉa kaṇīreṉṟa pēccu: nakiccuvi iḻiyōṭum pāṅku; ivi niṟiya vēṇṭum.

aṭuttavarkku aṟuveṟuppūṭṭukiṉṟa aṅka acivu; tēviyaṟṟa cikikaḷ, paṇpāṭillāta paḻakka vaḻakkam pōṉṟavaṟṟit tavirttirukka vēṇṭum.

ittakiya veḷittōṟṟamum, eṭuppāṉa naṭi muṟiyum koṇṭiruppatuṭaṉ, paṟpala uṭaliyakka ceyal muṟikaḷiyellām ciṟanta muṟiyil ceytu kāṭṭum tiṟaṉmikkavarākavum āciriyar viḷaṅka vēṇṭum.

itil oru ciṟappuk kuṟippu eṉṉaveṉṟāl, āciriyar oruvariṉ naṭimuṟi, maṟṟa māṇavarkaḷukku muṉ mātiriyākavum; (Example) avarē oru muṉṉōṭiyākavum (Model) amintiruppatu pōl iruppatutāṉ.

2. nuṇukka poruḷil nuṇukka nili

māṇavarkaḷukkuk kaṟpikka vēṇṭiya pāṭap poruḷi mutalil varici muṟiyāka vakuttu, niralpaṭuttik koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

payiṟṟuvikkum pāṭatti, oru nōṭputtakattil kuṟippeṭuttuk koḷḷalām. allatu maṉatil patiya vittuk koḷḷalām.

payiṟṟuvippataṟkēṟṟa tuṇip poruṭkaḷ, utavi cātaṉaṅkaḷ, mukkiyamāṉa karuvikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟit tayār ceytu vittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

āṭukaḷaṅkaḷ, ōṭukaḷaṅkaḷ mutaliya viḷiyāṭṭiṭaṅkakaḷi tēviyāṉa muṟiyil tayār ceytu, kuṟittu vittiṭa vēṇṭum.

vakuppiluḷḷa māṇavarkaḷi eppaṭi pirippatu, pōtippatu payiṟciyaḷippatu eṉpaṉavaṟṟi, tiṭṭamiṭṭu, oru teḷivāṉa maṉaniliyil muṭiveṭuppatu mikavum mukkiyam.

tāṉum uṭal niliyil tayārāki, maṉaniliyil, tayārittuk koṇṭu, celkiṉṟa pāṅku, kaṟpikkum vallamiyi vaḻaṅki vaḷappaṭutti, vaḻikāṭṭukiṟatu.

evvāṟu pōtikka vēṇṭum?

māṇavarkaḷukkup pōtikkum tiṟamiyi, vaḻaṅkum nili (Presentation) eṉṟu kūṟuvārkaḷ. atāvatu, tāṉ kaṟpikkum karuttik kavaṉamuṭaṉ muṉvippatu eṉṟum kūṟalām.

ākavē, tiṭṭamiṭṭut teḷivākat tayāritta pāṭak kuṟippukaḷi vittuk koṇṭu, pūrippu mika eppaṭi pōtippatu? ataṟkāṉa naṭimuṟikaḷik kaṭipiṭikkum cila vaḻikaḷik kāṇpōm.

1. terinta karutti mutalil kūṟi teriyāta karutti, viḷakka vēṇṭum. (Known to unknown)



2. ciṟiya eḷiya karutti mutalil viḷakki, piṟaku cikkalāṉa ceytikaḷit teḷivupaṭutta vēṇṭum. (Simple to Complex)



3. viṣayaṅkaḷi viḷakkukiṟa pōtu pēcuvatō, virivākap ākkuvatō allatu āmi poṟumiyi iḻakkac ceyvatō kūṭātu.



māṇavarkaḷ purintu koḷḷum aṟivu niliyi aṟintu, ataṟkē pakkuvamākap pōtikka vēṇṭum.



atu pōlavē, uḷaviyal uriyāṭal muṟiyiliruntu vivāta muṟi vari naṭattiṭa vēṇṭum.



4. kaṟṟut tarukiṟa pōtē, māṇavarkaḷiṉ karuttil patiyumāṟum, cintaṉikaḷ viriyumāṟum, ceyalpaṭuttat tuṇivu ēṟpaṭumāṟum, teḷivu uṇṭākumāṟum kaṟṟut taral vēṇṭum.



5. mēmpāṭuḷḷa tuṇik karuvikaḷiyum, utavip poruṭkaḷiyum payaṉpaṭuttukiṟapōtu, māṇavarkaḷiṉ kaṟkum tiṟaṉ eḻucci peṟukiṟatu eṉpati āciriyar eṉṟum niṉivil iruttik koḷḷa vēṇṭum.



6. ovvoru karuttum, antap pāṭattiṉ muḻumiyiyum purintu koḷḷumāṟu, toṭarpupaṭutti, pōtikka vēṇṭum.



7. col viḷakkatti viṭa, nēril poruṭkaḷik kāṭṭi viḷakkiṭa vēṇṭum.



mēṟkūṟiya karuttukkaḷi maṉatil koṇṭōmāṉāl, avaṟṟit toṭarntu, aṟivārnta aṇukumuṟikaḷ cila uṇṭu. anta vaḻaṅkum kaṟpikkum muṟikaḷil uḷḷa vakikaḷiyum terintu koḷvōm.

ciṟappāka pōtikkum varicikaḷ

(Steps in Presentation)



ciṟappāka pōtikkum niliyiṉi iraṇṭu pirivākap pārkkalām.

1. ceyaṟki muṟi (Artificial Device)

2. iyaṟki muṟi (Natural Device).

ceyaṟki muṟi pōtaṉiyiṉ pirivukaḷik, kāṇpōm.

1. pāṭatti curukkamāka aṟimukappaṭuttutal (Orientation)

tāṉ kaṟpikkum pāṭattip paṟṟiyō, allatu payiṟciyip paṟṟiyō; ataṉ tōṟṟam, vaḷarcci, taṉmi, iyalpu, perumi, payaṉ mutaliyavaṟṟit teḷivāka viḷakkum muṟi.

inta viḷakkatti iṉṉum nuṇmiyākkuvārkaḷ.

pāṭap poruḷ paṟṟiya varalāṟu, ilakkiyam, amippu paṟṟi kati pōlak kūṟum muṟi. (Narration).

pāṭap poruḷ paṟṟiya uṭkarutti, uṇmiyāṉa poruḷi uṇarntu koḷḷumāṟu viḷakkik kūṟum muṟi. (Exposition).

pāṭap poruḷ paṟṟiya cintaṉiyi, maṉatil paṭam: piṭittuk koṇṭu aṟiyumāṟu viḷakkam tarum muṟi (Description).

etaṟkākak kaṟṟuk koḷkiṟōm eṉpati māṇavarkaḷ uṇarntu, atil ārvam koṇṭu, eppaṭi ceyal paṭuvatu, mēmpaṭuttuvatu eṉpatākap pōtikkum muṟi. (Explanation).

iti col viḷakka muṟi - eṉṟum (Verbal Explanation) eṉṟum kūṟuvārkaḷ.

2. ceyal viḷakkam tarutal (Demonstration)

uṭaṟkalvi eṉpatu ceytu koṇṭē paḻakutal ākum ākavē, entap pāṭattiyum payiṟciyiyum ceyal mūkām kāṭṭi, ceyyac ceytu, tērcci peṟumāṟu ceyvatu tāṉ ceyal viḷakka muṟiyākum.

3. ārāyntaṟiya utavutal (Exploration)

māṇavarkaḷ tāṅkaḷ aṟintavaṟṟi, kaṇṭu kēṭṭavaṟṟi ceyalpaṭuttip pārtta piṟakum, mēlum atu paṟṟi ārāyntu, turuvit turuvi ārāyum cūḻniliyi uṇṭākkum muṟi itu iti āyvukkūṭa muṟi eṉṟum kūṟalām

4. kalanturiyāṭal (Discussion)

taṉippaṭṭa muṟiyil tāṅkaḷ purintu koṇṭati maṟṟa māṇavarkaḷuṭaṉ kalanturiyāṭac ceytu, mēlum terintu koḷḷa utavutal. inta uriyāṭalil āciriyar mukkiya paṅki vakikkiṟār. cikkalkaḷi tīrttu. aiyatti viḷakki, uriya pāṭattil cīriya karuttut teḷivu kāṇum muṟi itu.

5. mēṟpārvi kāṇal (Supervision)

tāṉ kaṟṟut tantati. kaṟṟuk koṇṭavāṟu, māṇavarkaḷ payiṟci ceykiṟārkaḷā eṉpati, āciriyar mēṟpārviyiṭutal. inta muṟi, māṇavarkaḷ ceykiṉṟa tavaṟukaḷi cuṭṭik kāṭṭavum, tiriyattuṭaṉ payiṟcikaḷit toṭaravum utavum.

6. matippīṭṭu muṟi (Evaluation)

māṇavarkaḷ kaṟṟuk koṇṭatil, peṟṟa muṉṉēṟṟattip pērārvattuṭaṉ terintu koḷḷa utavum muṟi.

enta aḷavu, evvaḷavu teḷivu, ettaṉic ceṟivu, avarkaḷiṉ kaṟṟal tiṟaṉ iruntatu; āciriyariṉ kaṟpikkum tiṟaṉ iruntatu eṉpati, pala tērvukaḷ mūlam kaṇṭaṟintu, matippīṭu ceyvataṉ mūlam, kaṟpikkum yukti, cakti peṟṟuk koḷkiṟatu.





﻿6. vakuppaṟi nirvākam

(CLASS MANAGEMENT)



* * *



vakuppum tokuppum

vakuppaṟi eṉpatu veṟumaṉē oru veṟṟiṭamalla. atil uḷḷavi ellām uyiraṟṟa poruṭkaḷumalla.

vakuppaṟi eṉpatu jīvaṉuḷḷa iṭam. tuṟu tuṟuppum, tuṭippum, muṉippum niṟinta māṇavarkaḷ cūḻnta kuḻām atu.

attakiya aṟivārnta māṇavarkaḷi, aṟputat tiṟamiyuṭaṉ kiyāṇṭu, kalviyiṉ nōkkaṅkaḷiyum, ilaṭciyaṅkaḷiyum māṇavarkaḷiṭam veḷippaṭuttum vakiyil ceyalpaṭuvatiyē vakuppaṟi nirvākam eṉṟu aḻikkiṉṟaṉar.

iti vakuppaṟi ceyal āṭci eṉṟum cilar kūṟuvārkaḷ.

nirvāka aṉukumuṟi.

āciriyariṉ vakuppaṟi nirvākattiṟku utavukiṉṟa, potuvāṉa ukanta cila kuṟippukkaḷi nām iṅkē kāṇpōm.



1. āciriyariṉ āḷumi. (Personality)

2. ceyalkaḷi iyakkum muṟikaḷ. (Activities)

3. uṭaṟkalviyi naṭattum cūḻnilikaḷ (Environment)

4. pātukāppu muṟikaḷ (Safety)

5. utavip poruṭkaḷik kiyāḷutal (Materials) 6. nērak kaṭṭuppāṭu (Time)

7. māṇavarkaḷip kuḻuvākap pirittal (Grouping)

8. pāṭat tiṭṭaṅkaḷi vakuttut tarutal (Schedule)

9. pōṭṭikaḷi ēṟpaṭuttit tarutal (Tournaments)

10. matippeṇkaḷ mūlam matippiṭutal (Assignment of Marks)

mēlē kāṇum kuṟippukkaḷi viḷakkuvataṟku muṉ, āciriyar pāṭam naṭattuvataṟkēṟṟa pakkuva niliyuṭaṉ cella vēṇṭum eṉpati, valiyuṟuttum uyarnta taṉmiyi cuṭṭik kāṭṭukiṟōm.

enta vakuppukkuc celkiṟōm, eṉṉa pāṭam, enta pōtaṉi muṟi, enta utavip poruṭkaḷ, ataṟkāṉa āṭukaḷam tayārā? aḷaviṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉavā, eṉpatiyellām tayār ceytu koṇṭu ceṉṟāl tāṉ, vakuppi taṭiyillāmal, taṭumāṟṟamillāmal, kuḻappam nērāmal nirvakittuc cella muṭiyum.

iviyellām āciriyariṉ āḷumit taṉmiyākum.

āḷumi niṟinta āciriyar, vakuppaṟikkuc celkiṟa pōtu, etirkoḷkiṟa piracciṉikaḷ pala.

ivaṟṟip piraṭciṉi eṉpati viṭa, naṭi muṟi nikaḻccikaḷ eṉṟu koṇṭōmāṉāl, cikkaliṉṟi cellavum, ciṟappākac collavum, māṇavarkaḷ maṉati vellavum muṭiyum.

ivaṟṟiyum caṟṟu virivākak kāṇpōm.

1. vakuppil māṇavarkaḷ

māṇavarkaḷiṉ eṇṇikki oru vakuppiṟku 40 mutal 45 eṉpatu potuvāṉa viti. oru vakuppukku 25 pērkaḷ eṉpatu uttamamāṉatu eṉpatu niyati. eṉṟālum, cūḻ tilikkēṟpa, varukiṉṟa māṇavarkaḷiṉ eṇṇikkiyik āṇṭu vetumpāmal, tarukiṉṟa pāṭaṅkaḷik koṇṭē, āciriyariṉ takutiyum, tiṟamiyum, nirvākac ciṟappum veṉippaṭukiṉṟatu.

2. iṭamum nēramum

paḷḷiyiṉ vakuppukkum, āṭukaḷattiṉ iṭamum uḷḷa ṭiveḷiyi aṉucarittē, vakuppi ārampikkavum, viṭuvikkavum muṭiyum. ākavē, māṇavarkaḷ vantu cēr vēṇṭiya kuṟippiṭṭa iṭam: kuṟippiṭṭa nēram; mutaliyavaṟṟi āciriyar kūṟiṭa vēṇṭum.

tēviyāṉa cīruṭi aṇiyavum, variciyāka vantu cēravum kūṭiya nērattiyum kuṟittuk kāṭṭa vēṇṭum.

āciriyarum muṉ kūṭṭiyē anta iṭattiṟku vantu, kāttuk koṇṭiruntu, muṉṉēṟpāṭṭuṭaṉ, tayārāka irukka vēṇṭum.

kuṟitta nērattil pāṭatti ārampippatu pōlavē, naṭatti muṭittu, kuṟitta nērattil, vakuppik kalittu. cellumāṟu paṇikka vēṇṭum.

ivvāṟu iṭam, kāla nēram, aṇivakuttu varac ceytal pōṉṟavaṟṟil, āciriyar kaṭṭuppāṭṭu naṭavaṭik vakiyi mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭum.

3. cīruṭi

uṭaṟkalvip payiṟci, viḷiyāṭṭiṟkeṉṟu, kuṟippiṭṭa cīruṭikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. antantap paḷḷikkeṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭa cīruṭikaḷ tarappaṭṭirukkum.

māṇavarkaḷukku kāl caṭṭi, paṉiyaṉ, kāluṟiyum kālaṇiyum.

māṇavikaḷukkum vakukkappaṭṭa pāvāṭi, caṭṭi mutaliyaṉa uṇṭu.

orē cīrāṉa uṭiyil vantu payiṟci ceytāl, paṅku peṟṟāl, pārppataṟku aḻakāka irukkum. payiṟciyum naṉṟāka naṭakkum. eṉavē, cīruṭi viṣayattil, āciriyarkaḷ atika ciratti eṭuttuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum. atu āciriyariṉ ciṟappi uyarttum.

4. vakuppi aṇivakukkum muṟi

cūriya oḷi mukattil paṭāmal, māṇavarkaḷi variciyāka niṟutta vēṇṭum.

kuḷḷamāṉa māṇavarkaḷ muṉpuṟam, piṟaku uyaramāṉavarkaḷ eṉpatāka varicip paṭutti niṟka vikka vēṇṭum.

kikaḷi nīṭṭi muṉpuṟamum, pakkavāṭṭilum acittup payiṟci ceyya, pōtiya iṭavacatiyuṭaṉ niṟkac ceyya vēṇṭum.

māṇavarkaḷ āciriyarip pārkka, āciriyar māṇavarkaḷip pārkka vacatiyāka iruppatu pōla, niṟka vittu, niṉṟu koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

potuvāka kōṭu allatu varici muṟiyil (Line or Rank) allatu aṭukku allatu patti muṟiyil (File or Column). allatu arivaṭṭa aṇiyamippil, muḻuvaṭṭa amippil māṇavarkaḷi aṇivakuttu niṟkac ceyyalām.

5. māṇavarkaḷ varukiyaṟital (Attendance)

māṇavarkaḷ vakuppukku cariyāka vantirukkiṉṟārkaḷā eṉpati, pala muṟikaḷil kēṭṭum, aḻittum aṟiyalām.

a) māṇavarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷik (Names) kūppiṭṭu cari pārttal. itu ēṟṟa muṟiyāka iruntālum, nēram atikamāki viṭum.

ā) māṇavarkaḷukkut tarappaṭṭirukkum eṇkaḷi (Roll Numbers) aḻittu cari pārttal.

i) cuvaṟṟil eṇkaḷi eḻuti, avaravar eṇṇukku muṉpāka, māṇavarkaḷi niṟutti kāliyāka irukkum eṇṇip pārttu, varāta māṇavarik kaṇṭaṟital.

pala paḷḷikaḷil viḷiyāṭum iṭamē illāta pōtu, itu pōla amippu iruppatum kiṭippatum eḷitalla.

ivvāṟu māṇavar varukiyip pativu ceytu koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

6. kuḻu amippu

vakuppil atika eṇṇikkiyil varum māṇavarkaḷi, ciṟu ciṟu kuḻukkaḷākap (Squad) pirittu, ovvoru kuḻuvukkum oru kuḻut talivari niyamittu, kuḻuviṉ muṉṉē talivari niṟka vittu, avarkaḷ mūlam varukiyip pativu ceyvatum ciṟappāṉa muṟiyākum.

vakuppu toṭaṅkuvataṟku muṉṉatākavum, vakuppu muṭintu kalivataṟku muṉṉatākavum, peyarp paṭṭiyali caripārppatu nallatu, ēṉeṉṟāl, iṭippaṭṭa nērattil, cilar naḻuvip pōvatum uṇṭu.

enta muṟiyip piṉpaṟṟiṉālum, nēram vīṇākip pōkāmal pārttuk koḷvatu nallatu.

7. pātukāppu naṭavaṭikkikaḷ

uṭaṟpayiṟcikaḷ , viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷ eṉkiṟapōtu, avaṟṟiṟkuḷḷēcila āpattukkaḷ ēṟpaṭuvataṟkum ētukkaḷ uṇṭu.

jimṉāsṭik payiṟcikaḷ eṉpatil, kīḻē tavaṟiviḻa vāyppu uṇṭu. mettikaḷ vittu, kaṟpikkum pōtu, pātukāppu uṇṭu.

kapāṭi pōṉṟu uṭalāl mōtik koḷḷum āṭṭaṅkaḷum uṇṭu. avaṟṟil vitikaḷi cemmiyāka collikkoṭuttu viṭṭāl, vipattukkaḷ nērāmal pātukāttuk koḷḷalām.

āṭukaḷaṅkaḷ, ōṭukaḷaṅkaḷil, kaṟkaḷ, muṟkaḷ, kaṇṇāṭit tuṇṭukaḷ illāmal, mēṭu paḷḷam illāmal, cariceytu vittup pāṭam kaṟpippatu, pātukāppāṉa cūḻniliyi ēṟpaṭuttum vaḻikaḷākum.

avvāṟu etirpārātu ētāvatu vipattu nērntāl, mutalutavi ceyvataṟkāṉa, mutalutavip peṭṭiyi vittiruppatu, putticālittaṉamākum.

8. kaṭṭuppāṭu kāttal.

kaṭṭuppāṭṭuṭaṉ ceykiṟa enta kāriyamum, kaḷippiyum, kavarcciyiyum, veṟṟiyiyum maṉattiruptiyiyum aḷikkum.

viṟuviṟuppum vivaram iṉmiyum uḷḷa ciṟuvarkaḷukku, ārvattiṉ kāraṇamāka avacaramum āvēcamum vantu viṭuvatu iyaṟkitāṉ. ākavē, avarkaḷi aṉpuṭaṉ, kaṭṭukkōppākak kāttirukka, ceyalpaṭa, māṇavarkaḷip paḻakkuvatu, māṇpumiku ceyalākum.

1. āciriyariṉ tōṟṟam, āḷumi, pēccu, naṭatti, pāṭam kaṟpikkummuṟi, aṉpāṉa kaṇṭippu, ātaravāṉa vitimuṟi kaṭipiṭippu ellām māṇavarkaḷik kaṭṭuppāṭṭuṭaṉ irukka vikkum.

2, āciriyar kaṟṟuttarukiṟa payiṟcikaḷ, māṇavarkaḷukku viruppam aḷippatāka amikiṟapōtu, avarkaḷum mika iṇakkattuṭaṉ ceyalpaṭa uṟcākam aḷikkum. āciriyariṉ pāṭattērvum, kaṭṭuppāṭṭi vaḷarttiṭa utavum.

3. āciriyariṉ cariyāṉa nērattil varutal. māṇavarkaḷ cīruṭiyik kavaṉittal, kaṇkāṇittal, tiruttutal, kuṟitta nērattil pāṭam kaṟṟut tarutal, vakuppik kalittal, maṉakkaṭṭuppāṭṭiyum māṇavarkaḷiṉ uṭal kaṭṭuppāṭṭiyum mikutiyākavē vaḷarttuviṭum vēkam koṇṭaviyākum

mēlum, kuṟumpum, kutarkkamum, kuṟukkuvaḻikaḷil cantōṣappaṭum māṇavarkaḷum irukkattāṉ ceyvārkaḷ. ivarkaḷukku oru poṟuppi aḷikkiṟapōtu, avarkaḷiṉ vaḻimuṟi māṟṟam kāṇum. vakuppil amitiyum ceyal tiṟamum atikamākiviṭum.

iṟutiyāka, māṇavarkaḷiṭiyē pōṭṭikaḷ, viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷ naṭakkiṟapōtu, caricamamāṉa tiṟami. eṭi eṉṟu cīrākappārttu, pōṭṭi naṭattuvatu, ellōrukkumē iṉpam payakkak kūṭiyatāka amiyum.

iṉi, vakuppaṟi nirvākatti, ciṟappāka naṭattiṭa mukkiyamāṉa kuṟippukaḷit tokuttuttantirukkiṟōm.

1. āciriyar payiṟcikku, poruttamāṉa uṭi aṇintirukka vēṇṭum.

2. atupōlavē, māṇavarkaḷiyum cīruṭiaṇintu varacceyya vēṇṭum.

3. māṇavarkaḷukkum nirvākap poṟuppiṉi vaḷarppatu pōla, vāyppukaḷi vaḻaṅka vēṇṭum.

4. āciriyar, tāṉ kaṟṟuttarap pōkiṟa pāṭatti, ciṟappākat tayārittuc cella vēṇṭum.

5. vakuppil uḷḷa māṇavarkaḷiṉ vayatu, iṉam, tēvikaḷ, ārvaṅkaḷ, etirpārppukaḷ, tiṟami kaḷ ivikaḷukkēṟpa pāṭaṅkaḷit tērvu ceytu, kaṟpikkum muṟiyik kiyāḷa vēṇṭum.

6. pāṭaṅkaḷik kaṟpikkiṟa pōtu, teḷivāṉa kaṟpikkum muṟi maṭṭum pōtātu. kaṇireṉṟa kuralum, cariyāṉa uccarippum. teḷivāṉa pēccum vēṇṭum.

7. cariyāṉa nērattiṟku vakuppiṟku vara, kalintu cella eṉpatil kaṭṭuppāṭāṉa kavaṉam celuttiṭa vēṇṭum.

8. māṇavarkaḷiṉ apiprāyattiyum aṟintu, cevicāyttu, nallateṉṟupaṭṭāl, naṭi muṟippaṭutta vēṇṭum.

9. māṇavarkaḷukkuk kīḻppaṭitaliyum, cuyakaṭṭup pāṭṭiyum, paṇpāṉa ceyalmuṟikaḷiyum vaḷarkka āvaṉa ceyya vēṇṭum.

10. māṇavarkaḷ maṉam puṇpaṭumpaṭi pēcuvati, muṭintavari tavirkka vēṇṭum.

11. māṇavarkaḷiṉ kuṟikaḷi, nācūkkāka viḷakki

nivarttippatum tiṟamikaḷi maṟṟavarkaḷum aṟiyumpaṭi, pārāṭṭa vēṇṭum.

12. payiṟci vakuppil atikam pēcāmal, ceyalpaṭac ceytu: muṭintāl vicili atikam payaṉpaṭutti, naṭattiṭa vēṇṭum.

13. pātukāppu muṟikaḷil tīvirak kavaṉam celuttuvatuṭaṉ, taṉṉāl muṭinta aḷavu tiṟamiyip payaṉ paṭutti, veḷippaṭutti, vakuppi naṭattukiṟapōtu, niccayam nalla veṟṟiyip peṟa muṭiyum. niṟinta tiruptiyiyum aṭiya muṭiyum.





﻿7. pāṭam tayār ceytal

(LESSON PLAN)



* * *



āciriyar. nipuṇar

āciriyari, oru kaṭṭiṭa varikali nipuṇarukkum (Architect), kaṭṭiṭam kaṭṭuvōrukkum (Builder) oppiṭalām.

kaṭṭiṭa varikali nipuṇar eṉpavar, cariyākak kaṭṭiṭam kaṭṭukiṉṟa tiṭṭaṅkaḷi varipaṭam mūlam teḷivākak kāṭṭukiṟār.

kaṭṭiṭam kaṭṭupavarō, teḷivāṉa varipaṭattiṉ taṉmiyip purintu koṇṭu, tiṭṭaṅkaḷi ceyalpaṭuttik kāṭṭuvār.

atupōlavē oru āciriyar tāṉē pāṭatti tayārittuk koṇṭu tāṉē ceytu muṭikkum nipuṇarāka, vallunarākat tikaḻkiṟār.

ākavē, pāṭam tayār ceytal eṉpatu, kaṟpikka vēṇṭiya pāṭap poruḷit tērnteṭuttu, poruttamāṉa muṟiyil taram pirikkappaṭṭu, aṟivārnta muṟiyil naṭatti, muṉṉēṟṟam kāṇukiṟa muḻumiyāṉa muyaṟci eṉpatāka nām karutalām.

āciriyarum pāṭam tayār ceytalum.

pāṭat tiṭṭattit tayār ceykiṟa āciriyarukku

1. koṭukkap paṭṭirukkum kuṟippiṭṭa vakuppu nērattiṟkuḷ, pāṭattip pōtittu, pakkuvamāka ilaṭciyatti aṭiyum vaḻikaḷi culapamākap peṟamuṭikiṟatu.

2. ovvoru māṇavariyum pāṭam patamākac ceṉṟaṭiyak kūṭiya pāṅkāṉa muṟiyi, pakuttup peṟa muṭikiṟatu.

3. enta mātiriyāṉa kaṟpikkum muṟi kiyāḷappaṭukiṟatu eṉpati tiṭṭavaṭṭamāka muṭiveṭuttu, ceyalpaṭa, ciṟappu peṟa utavukiṟatu.

ivvāṟu nām āciriyariṉ poṟuppi aṟintu koḷvōmāṉāl, pāṭam tayār ceykiṉṟataṉ payaṉkaḷiyum eṉṉaveṉṟu purintu koḷḷa, namakku utavakiṉṟa kuṟippukkaḷiyum terintu koḷvōm.



pāṭam tayār ceytāl ēṟpaṭum payaṉkaḷ.

1. kuṟippiṭṭa pāṭatti, ciṟappāka pōtikka utavukiṟatu.

2. naṭanta paḻiya pāṭattiṟkum, naṭakkavirukkum putiyapāṭattiṟkum toṭarpu tarum pālamāka, amintu viṭukiṟatu.

3. pāṭap poruḷik kiyāḷutal, tēviyāṉa tuṇip poruṭkaḷit tēṭit tayār ceytal. ceyalkaḷit tiṭṭamiṭutal. ivaṟṟiṟkut tuṇipurikiṟatu.

4. viruppamāṉa kaṟpikkum muṟi etu eṉpatit tērnteṭukkum ñāṉatti āciriyarukku vaḻaṅkukiṟatu.

5. pāṭattiṉ muḻumiyi, nuṇukkamākap purintu koḷḷac ceykiṟatu.

6. pōtikkumpoḻutu ēṟpaṭukiṟa viḷivukaḷi, vivēkattuṭaṉ cantikkavum, cintikkavum, mēmpaṭuttavum tūṇṭukiṟatu.

7. pāṭatti ciṟappākap pōtikka, viṉākkaḷi eḻuppavum, varipaṭaṅkaḷit tayārikkavum kūṭiya vallamiyi viḷivikkiṟatu.

8. pāṭaṅkaḷi oruṅkiṇittuk koṇṭu (unity) pōtikka utavukiṟatu.

9. kuṟippiṭṭa poṟuppāṉa pōtaṉā muṟiyi uṟutippaṭuttukiṟatu.

10. māṇavarkaḷukkuḷḷa tiṟamikaḷiṉ vēṟṟumiyi aṟintu, ataṟkēṟpa aṉucarittup pōtikkum pērāṟṟalik koṭukkiṟatu.

11. tēviyāṉapoḻutu, tēvikaḷi tiruptiyākat tīrttu vikkavum utavukiṟatu.

12. āciriyarukku kaṟpaṉā caktiyi aḷippatuṭaṉ, pōtikkumpoḻutu, oru cutantiramāṉa uṇarviyum mikutippaṭuttukiṟatu.



ivvaḷavu vacatikaḷiyum, vāyppukkaḷiyum, vari muṟikaḷiyum vakuttu vaḻaṅkukiṟa, pāṭam tayār ceyyum tiṭṭattil, mukkiyamāṉa cila kuṟippukkaḷ iṭam peṟṟāka vēṇṭum;

pāṭam tayār ceytalil cila kuṟippukkaḷ

1. naṭattappōkiṟa pāṭattiṉ, mukkiya nōkkamum, ilakkum antap pāṭat tayārippil iṭam peṟṟirukka vēṇṭum.

2. pāṭat tayārippāṉatu, cantarppa cūḻnilikkēṟpa vaḷintu koṭukkiṉṟa taṉmiyil amiya vēṇṭum.

3. ellā vayatiṉarukkum ēṟṟāṟpōla pāṭat tayārippum kuṟippum amintiruppatuṭaṉ, antap pāṭam, purikiṟa taṉmiyil, poruḷ potintatākavum viḷaṅka vēṇṭum.

4. pāṭattip pōtikkiṟa pōtu, atil muṉṉēṟṟam viḷikiṟa aḷavil, terikiṟa taṉmiyilum atu amintiṭal vēṇṭum.

5. māṇavarkaḷ pāṭattip purintu koṇṭu payiṟci peṟavum, muyaṟci ceyyavum kūṭiya aḷavil, irukka vēṇṭum.

iṉi, pāṭat tayārippu amivati, 4 pirivākaṭ pirittup pārkkalām.

pāṭat tayārippu vakikaḷ (Types of lesson plan)

1. muḻup pāṭat tiṭṭam (Unit plan)

muḻup pāṭat tiṭṭam eṉpatu oru nāḷikkākat tayārikkappaṭāmal, pala nāṭkaḷ allatu nīṇṭa kālat tiṟku allatu oru cemasṭar kālattiṟku varuvatupōla tayārikkappaṭuvatu.

2. tiṉacarip pāṭat tiṭṭam (Daily plan) oru nāḷikku, oru nēra vakuppil (class) naṭatti muṭikiṟa aḷavukkut tayārikkum pāṭak kuṟippu.

3. paṭikka utavum vaḻikāṭṭi (Study guide)

paṭikkiṉṟa nērattil ēṟpaṭukiṟa piracciṉikaḷi camāḷittu; kuḻappattit tavirkka utavukiṟa puttakaṅkaḷi kuṟippiṭṭuk kāṭṭuvatu.

4. payiṟcip puttakaṅkaḷ (Work books)

kāliyāka iṭam viṭṭut tarukiṟa nōṭ puttakaṅkaḷil, irukkiṟa viṉākkaḷukku viṭiyaḷittal; piracciṉikaḷi aṟintu tīrkkum muṟikaḷi eḻutip paḻakutal.

ippaṭi pala vakiyāṉa muṟikaḷum pirivukaḷum iruntālum, uṭaṟkalviviyip poṟuttavari, pāṭat tayārippuk kuṟippu muṟiyi iraṇṭu vakiyākap pirikkalām.

1. potup pāṭattiṟkut tayārittal



(General Lesson Plan)

2. ciṟappup pāṭattiṟkut tayārittal



(Particular Lesson Plan)





I potup pāṭattiṟkut tayārittal

1. uṭalip patappaṭuttum āyattap payiṟcikaḷ (Introductory Activities)

kaṭumiyāṉa payiṟcikaḷ, viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷil īṭupaṭu paṭuvataṟku muṉ, uṭaliluḷḷa viṟipput taṉmiyiliruntu viṭupaṭa, uṭalip patamākkum payiṟcikaḷ palavaṟṟic ceytu, āyattappaṭuttukiṉṟamuṟi itu. (warming up)

vakuppiṉ motta nērattil, ⅛ pakuti nēratti, itaṟkāka celavu ceyyalām.

metuvāka ōṭutal; noṇṭiyaṭittal, kutittuk kutittu ōṭutal; vilaṅku pāvaṉikaḷ, ūrtikaḷ pōla ōṭutal muṟiyil patappaṭuttum payiṟcikaḷit taralām. itu uṭal piṭippu, taci iḻuppu ēṟpaṭāmal taṭuttu, tārāḷamāka iyaṅka utavum.

2. oḻuṅkupaṭuttum payiṟcikaḷ (Formal Activities)

uṭal uṟuppukkaḷ tuvaḷuntaṉmi peṟa; nimirntu niṟkum tōraṇiyil uṟuti peṟa; aḻakuṟa uṭali naṭattic cella; uṭal kaṭṭuppāṭṭil tikaḻa, kīḻkkāṇum uṭaṟpayiṟcikaḷ utavukiṉṟaṉa. uṭalil kiḷarcciyi uṇṭākkukiṉṟaṉa.

eṭiyillā veṟuṅki uṭaṟpayiṟcikaḷ;

kuṟuntaṭi (Wand) ; ṭampel uṭaṟpayiṟcikaḷ

taṇṭāl paskip payiṟcikaḷ

kaṭṭaḻaku tarum kalip payiṟcikaḷ (Calisthenics)

iviyellām oḻuṅkupaṭutta utavum payiṟci “kaḷākum. motta vakuppu nērattil - paṅku nēratti ¼ itaṟkup payaṉpaṭuttalām.

3. puttuṇarvu ūṭṭum payiṟcikaḷ (Recreative Activities)

ivvakip payiṟcikaḷ, viḷiyāṭṭut tiṟamiyi, iyaṟkiyāka uḷḷa āṟṟali vaḷarppatuṭaṉ, makiḻcciyiyum, vēṭikkiyum, iṉpattiyum ūṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.

3-1 ciṟu ciṟu viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷ (Minor games)

3-2 muṉṉōṭi viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷ (Lead up games)

3-3 katip pāṭṭu viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷ (Story plays)

3-4 cākacap payiṟcikaḷ (Stunts)

3-5 potuvāṉa caṇṭip pōṭṭi payiṟcikaḷ (Combatives)

3-6 toṭarōṭṭaṅkaḷ (Relay Races)

ivi ellām pōraṭikkāmal, puttuṇarvūṭṭum payiṟcikaḷākum. ivi maṭṭum pōtāteṉṟāl, iṉṉum pala payiṟci muṟikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟiyum puttuṇarvūṭṭap payaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷalām.

a) kōpurap payiṟci muṟikaḷ (Pyramids)

ā) kuṭṭik karaṇap payiṟcikaḷ (Tumbling)

i) aṇi naṭip payiṟcikaḷ (Marching)

ī) tāḷa naṭip payiṟcikaḷ (Rhythmics)

u) karaḷā kaṭṭip payiṟcikaḷ (Club drills)

ū) peruṅkaḻip payiṟcikaḷ (Pole drill)

maṟṟum ācaṉaṅkaḷ, cūriya namaskāram pōṉṟa payiṟcikaḷiyum; taṉittiṟaṉ ōṭukaḷap pōṭṭi nikaḻccikaḷiyum; maṟpōr, kuttuc caṇṭi pōṉṟavaṟṟiyum cērttuk koḷḷalām.

ivaṟṟik kaṟpikka ciṟappup payiṟcikaḷ tēvippaṭuvatāl, potup pāṭattiṉ ciṟappup payiṟciyiṉ kīḻ koṇṭu varalām.

kuṟippu :

1. ciṟappup payiṟcikaḷiyellām oḻuṅkupaṭuttum payiṟcikaḷukkum, puttuṇarvūṭṭum payiṟcikaḷukkum iṭiyil naṭattalām.

2. ovvoru potup pāṭat tiṭṭattilum ciṟappup payiṟcikaḷ iruntu tāṉ āka vēṇṭum eṉpatilli.

3. ciṟappup payiṟcikkāka ⅛ nērattip payaṉ paṭuttalām. iṉi, potup pāṭatti naṭattak kūṭiya pakutikaḷi, ivvāṟāka nām teḷivuṟak kāṇalām.

1. māṇavar vakuppukku varukiṉṟa varukiyaṟital

2. patappaṭuttum āyattap payiṟcikaḷ

3. oḻuṅkupaṭuttum uriya payiṟcikaḷ

4. ciṟappup payiṟcikaḷ

5. puttuṇarvūṭṭum payiṟcikaḷ

6. vakuppik kalittu, pōkac ceytal

potup pāṭattit tayārikka (viḷakkamāṉa mātiri vakuppu)

vakuppu : āṟu

nēram : 45 nimiṭam

tuṇipporuṭkaḷ : tēviyāṉavaṟṟi tokuttuk koḷḷavum.

1 māṇavar varukiyum varukip pativum (2 nimiṭaṅkaḷ) (Roll Call)

māṇavarkaḷi variciyāka vantu, aṇi vakukkac ceytu, peyarkaḷi allatu eṇkaḷi aḻittu, varukiyip pativu ceytal.

2. patappaṭuttum āyattap payiṟcikaḷ (5 nimiṭaṅkaḷ) (Warming up)

irukkiṉṟa māṇavarkaḷi ciṟu ciṟu kuḻukkaḷākap pirittu niṟka vittu, ovvoru kuḻuviṟkum oru payiṟciyik kaṟṟut tantu, mutalil ceyya vikka vēṇṭum.

1 kuḻu noṇṭiyaṭippatu. 2vatu kuḻu tuḷḷi uyaramākak kutippatu. 3vatu kuḻu niṉṟa iṭattilē ōṭuvatu. 4vatu kuḻu uyarak kutittu kikaḷi talikku mēlē taṭṭuvatu.

ippaṭi vicilukkup piṟaku, ovvoru kuḻuvum ceytu, aṭutta vicilukku kuḻukkaḷ taṅkaḷ payiṟciyi māṟṟik koṇṭu, ivvitamāka 5 nimiṭaṅkaḷ ceytiṭa vēṇṭum. atāvatu ovvoru kuḻuvum, oru muṟi inta 4 payiṟcikaḷiyum ceytu muṭittiṭa vāyppaḷikka vēṇṭum.

8. oḻuṅkupaṭuttum payiṟcikaḷ (Formal)

intap payiṟcikaḷukku 10 nimiṭaṅkaḷ eṭuttuk koḷḷalām . intap payiṟci muṟiyik kaṟpikka 4 pirivukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ vaḻiyil, viḷakkamākak kāṇalām.

3-1 vakuppi aṇivakuttu niṟkac ceytal (Formation)

vantirukkum māṇavarkaḷi oṟṟi variciyil (Single line) niṟkac ceytu; eṇṇac ceytu; eṇṇikkikku ēṟpa avarkaḷ aṇiyamipput tiṟappu muṟiyil (open order formation) varac ceytu niṟutta vēṇṭum.

māṇavarkaḷiṭiyē payiṟci ceyya pōtiya iṭiveḷi iruppatu pōlavum; ellā māṇavarkaḷum āciriyarip pārttiṭa vacatiyāka niṟpatu pōlavum tiṟutti vippatu tāṉ aṇi vakuppu muṟiyākum.

8-2. ceyal viḷakkam (Demonstration)

eṉṉa payiṟci ceyyap pōkiṟōm eṉpati māṇavarkaḷukku, pōtiya viḷakkattuṭaṉ, puriyum paṭi ceytu kāṭṭal vēṇṭum.

3-3. eṇṇikkikku ēṟpa kaṟpittal (Teaching by Counts)

tāṉ ceytu kāṭṭiyavuṭaṉ, ovvoru niliyāka māṇavarkaḷukku, eṇṇikkippaṭi ceyyumāṟu kūṟa vēṇṭum. avarkaḷ ceykiṟapōtu, tavaṟukaḷi cuṭṭik kāṭṭit tirutti, niṟutti payiṟcikaḷi kaṟṟuk koḷḷac ceyya vēṇṭum.



3-4. cīrāka, toṭarntu payiṟci ceytal (Doing Continuously and Rhythmically)

tavaṟillāmal ovvoru niliyāka, māṇavarkaḷ kaṟṟuk koṇṭa piṟaku, toṭarntu, niṟuttāmal, cīrākavum tāḷalayattuṭaṉum payiṟciyi ceytiṭap paṇikkavum.

pala muṟi, ippayiṟciyit toṭarntu ceyta piṟaku, mīṇṭum māṇavarkaḷi ōraṇiyil koṇṭu varac ceytal vēṇṭum.

ivvāṟu paḻakkukiṉṟa payiṟci muṟikaḷ nāṉkiṉi, uṅkaḷ pārvikkut tarukiṟōm. itupōl niṟiya payiṟci muṟikaḷ uḷḷaṉa.

tēviyāṉa vakuppukku ēṟpa, tēviyāṉavaṟṟit tērnteṭuttuk koḷḷavum.

mātirip payiṟcikaḷ :

1. 1. kikaḷ iraṇṭiyum talikku mēṟpuṟamāki uyātati piṟaku, kikaḷi maṭakkik kuntu. (Squat)

2. eḻuntu iyalpāka nimirntu nil.

2. 1. kikaḷip pakkavāṭṭil virittu, iṭatu kāli iṭatu pakkamāka eṭuttuvi.

2. kikaḷi, talikku mēlāka virivāka uyartti, (Swing) kutikāl uyara, muṉ kālil nil.

3. muṉ mātiriyē cey.

4. iyalpāka nimirntu nil.

3. 1. kikaḷ iraṇṭiyum (iṭuppu) pakkavāṭṭil vi.

2. iṭuppi iṭatu puṟamāka vaḷi (Bend)

3. iṭuppi nimirttu.

4. iyalpāka nimirntu nil.

4. 1. (niṟkum niliyiliruntu) kālkaḷi akaṟṟik kutittu, talikku mēlē kitaṭṭu.

2. iyalpāka nimirntu nil.

4 ciṟappup payiṟcikaḷ

oḻuṅkupaṭuttum payiṟcikaḷ muṭinta piṟaku, mīṇṭum oṟṟi aṇi varicikku, māṇavarkaḷi varac ceyyavum.

aṭuttu, ciṟappup payiṟcikaḷit toṭaṅkavum. 8 nimiṭam pōtum. itaṟku oru ācaṉattik kaṟpikka, eṭuttuk koḷḷalām.

ācaṉatti eṇṇikki muṟiyil kaṟpikkalām.

ācaṉattiṉ peyar : patmācaṉam

eṇṇikki

1. kālkaḷi nīṭṭi nimirntu uṭkārutal.

2. valatu kāli iṭatu kāl toṭi mītu paṭumpaṭi vittal.

3. iṭatu kāli valatu kāl toṭi mītu paṭumpaṭi vittal.



﻿

4. kikaḷi muḻaṅkālkaḷ mītu vittu ācaṉa irukkiyil iruttal.

cariyāṉa irukki varuvataṟku, cariyākak kaṟpittapiṉ, mīṇṭum, oṟṟi aṇi varicikku māṇavarkaḷi varac ceyyavum.

5. puttuṇarvūṭṭum viḷiyāṭṭu

inta viḷiyāṭṭukku 18 nimiṭam otukkik koḷḷalām. cāṉṟukku oru āṭṭam.

cili āṭṭam

oru kuṟippiṭṭa elliyi nirṇayittuk koṇṭu, ōṭippiṭikka (it) oruvari niyamittu, maṟṟa māṇavarkaḷi ōṭac ceyya vēṇṭum.

viraṭṭukiṟa māṇavar, tammit toṭa - varumpōtu, toṭappaṭāmal ōṭa vēṇṭum. iṉi ōṭa muṭiyātu eṉkiṟa pōtu, cilipōla aciyāmal niṉṟuviṭa vēṇṭum.

avar aciyāta vari pattiramāka irukkiṟār. acintāl toṭappaṭa toṭappaṭṭavar viraṭṭupavarāka māṟa, āṭṭam toṭarum.

kuṟippu, inta, āṭṭattiṟkāṉa vitimuṟikaḷi, muṉṉatākavē māṇavarkaḷukku viḷakka vēṇṭum.

nēram iruntāl, pala āṭṭaṅkaḷiyum āṭac ceyyalām.

viḷiyāṭṭu muṭinta piṟaku, māṇavarkaḷi aṇiyāka niṟkacceytu, vakuppik kalittiṭa (Dismiss) vēṇṭum.

II kuṟippiṭṭa ciṟappup pāṭattiṟkut tayār ceytal. (Particular Lesson Plan)

ciṟappuppāṭat tiṭṭam eṉpatu mutaṉmi āṭṭaṅkaḷ, allatu ōṭukaḷap pōṭṭi nikaḻccikaḷ paṟṟik kaṟṟuttarap, payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum kaṟpikkum muṟiyākum.

inta kaṟpikkum muṟi, viḷiyāṭṭukku viḷiyāṭṭu vēṟupaṭum.

ciṟappuppāṭattiṭṭam

viḷiyāṭṭukku (Game)

1. māṇavar varukiyum varukip pativum

2. poruttamāṉa uṭalip patappaṭuttum payiṟcikaḷ

3. viḷiyāṭṭiṉ aṭippaṭit tiṟamikaḷik kaṟpittal (Skills)

4. aṭippaṭit tiṟamikaḷi paḻakiṭap payiṟciyaḷittal

5. muṉṉōṭi viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷik kaṟpittal.

6. muḻu viḷiyāṭṭiyum viḷiyāṭavittal

7. vakuppik kalittup pōkac ceytal.

ōṭukaḷap pōṭṭi nikaḻccikku (Track and Field)

1. māṇavar varukiyum, varukippativum

2. poruttamāṉa, uṭalip patappaṭuttum payiṟcikaḷ

3. ōṭṭa nikaḻcciyiṉ aṭippaṭi nuṇukkattik kaṟpittal

4. aṭippaṭi nuṇukkattip paḻakki payiṟciyaḷittal

5. māṇavarkaḷukkiṭiyē pōṭṭi vittal

6. vakuppik kalittup pōkac ceytal

1. oru viḷiyāṭṭukku mātirip pāṭam.

vakuppu. 8

nēram: 45 nimiṭaṅkaḷ,

viḷiyāṭṭu: kippantāṭṭam

tuṇipporuṭkaḷ: 6, pantukaḷ, vali, aḷaviṭṭa āṭukaḷam.

1. varukiyum varukippativum

vakuppukku vantirukkum māṇavarkaḷi ōraṇi muṟiyil niṟkavittu, varukiyip pativu ceytal (2 nimiṭam)

2. patappaṭuttum poruttamāṉa uṭaṟpayiṟcikaḷ

niṉṟa iṭattil ōṭutal; metuvāka ōṭi piṟaku virivāka ōṭutal, kikaḷi muṉṉum piṉṉum, pakka vāṭṭilum vīcutal; kālkaḷi muṉṉum piṉṉumāka pakka vāṭṭilum cuḻaṟṟi iyakkutal; iṭuppi muṉṉum, piṉṉum pakkavāṭṭilum vaḷittut tirupputal. piṟaku, kippantāṭṭattiṟkut tēviyāṉa payiṟcikaḷit tarutal.

3. kippantāṭṭattil oru tiṟaṉik kaṟṟut tarutal.

(irukiyāl (Underhand Pass) kīḻppuṟamāka panti. eṭuttal) 8 nimiṭaṅkaḷil kaṟpikkavum.

māṇavarkaḷi paravalāka niṟkac ceytu, āciriyar ceykiṉṟa ceyali, maṟṟavarkaḷ pārppataṟkēṟṟavāṟu niṟutti vittal.

kikaḷi naṉṟākak kōrttuk koṇṭu, kālkaḷi akalamāka vacatikkēṟpa virittu niṉṟu, pantu vantu kikaḷil viḻukiṟa pōtu, pāṅkākat teṟittu pōvatu pōla, āciriyar ceytu kāṭṭa vēṇṭum.

niṟkum kālkaḷ nili, kikaḷik kōrttu vit tirukkum nili, tōḷkaḷiṉ amippu, iṭuppiṉ nili, ippaṭi ellā uṭal nilikaḷiyum āciriyar ceytu kāṭṭa vēṇṭum.

4. paḻakik koḷḷa payiṟci tarutal

itaṟku 15 nimiṭaṅkaḷ tarappaṭa vēṇṭum.

māṇavarkaḷi iruvar iruvarākap pirittu, ovvoru iruvarukkum oru pantu koṭuttu, avarkaḷik kīḻpuṟamāka panti eṭuttāṭi paḻakik koḷḷac ceytal

ēṟpaṭukiṉṟa tavaṟukaḷi avvappōtu tirutti, cariyāka ceyyac collutal.

5 muṉṉōṭi viḷiyāṭṭu

(Lead up Game)



5 muṟi toṭṭāṭum āṭṭam (Five touch Game)

irukkum māṇavarkaḷi iru kuḻukkaḷākap (10 pēr) pirittu, āṭukaḷattiṉ irupakkamum niṟka vittu, āṭac ceytal. carvīs pōṭṭa oru kuḻuvukku etirāka uḷḷa kuḻuviṉ āṭṭakkārarkaḷ, carvīs mūlam pōṭṭa panti, 5 muṟi eṭuttāṭi, etirk kuḻuviṟku aṉuppa vēṇṭum

5 muṟikkuḷ pantit tavaṟa viṭṭālum, 5 muṟikkuḷḷākap panti aṉuppiviṭṭālum, pantik kīḻāka kiyāl eṭuttāṭāmal viṭṭālum, tavaṟiḻitta kuḻu, veṟṟi eṇ peṟukiṟa vāyppi iḻantu viṭum.

vitimuṟippaṭi, entak kuḻu 15 veṟṟi eṇkaḷi eṭukkiṟatō, atuvē veṉṟatāka aṟivikkappaṭum.

6. kuḻu āṭṭam (Whole Game)

nēram iruntāl, kippantāṭṭatti, vitikaḷ paṭi āṭi makiḻa, aṉumatikkalām.

7. vakuppi ōraṇiyākki, kalittal

āṭṭam muṭinta piṟaku, aṉivariyum ōraṇiyil niṟkac ceytu, vakuppi muṟiyākak kalittu, aṉuppiviṭa vēṇṭum.

aṭuttatu, ōṭukaḷap pōṭṭiyil oru mātirippāṭam pārppōm.

2. ōṭukaḷap pōṭṭikku mātirip pāṭam tayārittal

vakuppu : 6.

nēram : 45 nimiṭaṅkaḷ

nikaḻcci : nīḷam tāṇṭal.

karuvikaḷ : utit teḻumpiṭa utavum mitipalaki

1. varukiyum varukip pativum

māṇavarkaḷi oṟṟi aṇiyil niṟkac ceytu, varukiyip pativu ceytal.

2. poruttamāṉa patappaṭuttum payiṟcikaḷ

naṭattal; metu ōṭṭam ōṭutal; virivāka ōṭutal, tuḷḷik kutittuk kikaḷi irupuṟamum virivāka āṭṭik koṇṭu ōṭutal, acittal. kīḻē amarntu, kālkaḷi nīṭṭikkuṉintu pātaṅkaḷit toṭutal. oru kāli nīṭṭi, oru kāli maṭakkik, kuṉintu iru kiyālum toṭutal, mallāntu paṭuttu; carvāṅkācaṉam pōl kālkaḷi ceṅkuttāka uyartti, cikkiḷ ōṭṭuvatupōl kālkaḷi cuḻaṟṟutal.

3. nuṇtiṟaṉik kaṟpittal

nīḷat tāṇṭalukku 4 pirivukaḷ uḷḷaṉa.

1. mititteḻum palakiyi nōkki ōṭi varutal. (Approach)

2. virivāka, aḻutti palakiyi mitittu uyartal (Take off)

3. kāṟṟil naṭattal (Walking in the air)

4. maṇaṟparappil kālūṉṟutal (Landing)

kuṟippu :

1. mititteḻum kāl etu eṉṟu aṟiya vittal.

2. mititteḻum palakikku, vēkamāka ōṭivarum aṇuku muṟiyik kaṟpittal.

3. kāṟṟil naṭakkum, cāmarttiyattik kaṟṟut tarutal.

4. kutikālkaḷ maṇaṟparappil mutalil ūṉṟumāṟu kutittal.

āciriyar inta nāṉku tiṟaṉkaḷiyum, paṭippaṭiyāka māṇavarkaḷukku ceytu kāṭṭutal.

4. paḻakum payiṟciyaḷittal

ellā māṇavarkaḷiyum ciṟu ciṟu kuḻukkaḷākap pirittu, pārttut terintu koṇṭa tiṟaṉ nuṇukkaṅkaḷi, paḻakik koḷḷac ceytal.

ovvoru kuḻu ceykiṉṟa paḻakiṭum muṟiyip pārkka, āciriyar āṅkāṅkē ceṉṟu pārttu, tavaṟukaḷit tiruttik kāṭṭi, pala muṟi ceytiṭa utava vēṇṭum.

5. pōṭṭi vittal

kaṟṟuk koṇṭa tiṟaṉkaḷip piṉpaṟṟi, māṇavarkaḷil yār atika tūram tāṇṭukiṟār eṉpataṟkāka, oru pōṭṭiyi āciriyar naṭattalām.

6. aṇiyil niṟutti vakuppik kalittal

vakuppu nēram muṭintavuṭaṉ māṇavarkaḷi, oṟṟi aṇiyil niṟkac ceytu, vakuppik kalittu, aṉuppi viṭa vēṇṭum.

kuṟippu: kaṟpikkum vakuppu nērattil (instruction period) kaṟṟut tanta pāṭaṅkaḷi, maṟu vakuppukku māṇavarkaḷ varum pōtu, tirumpac ceyyumāṟu paṇittu, nērkiṉṟa tavaṟukaḷit tiruttum muyaṟciyi āciriyar mēṟkoṇṭu, piṟaku putiya pāṭattiṟkup pōkalām.

āciriyarukkuk kiṭikkum nērattiṟku ēṟpa, aṉucarittu, pāṭattik kaṟpikkum pāṅkiṉi mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭum.

mattiya araciṉ kalvittuṟi, 1965l ēṟpaṭuttittanta nēra aṭṭavaṇiyiyum, āciriyarkaḷ piṉpaṟṟalām eṉpataṟkāka kīḻē tarappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.

ceyalkaḷiṉ





tarappaṭṭirukkum vakuppu nēram





taṉmikaḷ



35 nimiṭam



40 nimiṭam





1. payiṟcikaḷukkāka 4 mutal 7 nimiṭam 7 mutal 8 nimiṭam

2. ceyal / mutaṉmippakuti 25 nimiṭam 25 nimiṭam

3. ciṟukuḻu viḷiyāṭṭu / 6mutal 3nimiṭam 8mutal 7 nimiṭam toṭarōṭṭam

cila camayam, oru pāṭavakuppukku 45 nimiṭam eṉṟu nēram eṭukkappaṭum. appōtu mutaṉmippakutiyāṉa ceyal muṟikaḷukku 30 nimiṭam eṭuttuk koḷḷalām.





8. kaṟpikka utavum muṟikaḷ

(METHODS OF TEACHING PHYSICAL ACTIVITIES)



* * *



muṟiyiṉ niṟi

oru pāṭattik kaṟpikkiṟa pōtu, ētāvatu oru . kaṟpikkum muṟiyip payaṉpaṭutta vēṇṭum.

ciṟappākak kaṟpikka, tiṭṭavaṭṭamāṉa, tīrmāṉamāṉa muṟi oṉṟik kaṭipiṭikka vēṇṭum. illiyeṉṟāl, kaṟpikkum muyaṟcikaḷ vīṇākip pōyviṭum.

cariyāṉa kaṟpikkum muṟiyik kik koṇṭu viṭṭāl, atu kaṟkum ceyalik kaṉivākkiviṭum. āciriyariṉ aṟiya muyaṟciyum palaṉaḷikkum. muḻuppāṭat tiṭṭattiyum muṭittu vikkum nēram tantu, niṉivi niṟivēṟṟi vikkum cariyāṉa pāṭattiṭṭam, muṟiyāka naṭakkavum, māṇavarkaḷi uṟcākappaṭuttavum, ceytu malarcciyi ēṟpaṭuttum.

inta aṭippaṭiyik koṇṭē, āyvaṟiñarkaḷ muyaṟci pala mēṟkoṇṭu, kaṟpikkum muṟikaḷ palavaṟṟik kaṇṭaṟintuḷḷaṉar.

ākavē, pāṭattiṭṭattiṟkēṟpa; māṇavarkaḷuk kēṟpa, mukiḻttu varum cūḻnilikaḷukkēṟpa, kaṟpikkum muṟiyi māṟṟik koḷḷalām. māṟi māṟik kaṟpikkum muṟikaḷik kaṭippiṭikka, kīḻē koṭuttirukkum talippukaḷi aṟintu piṉpaṟṟa vēṇṭum.

uṭaṟkalviyil cila mukkiyamāṉa kaṟpikkum muṟikaḷ.

1. vāy moḻi viḷakka muṟi

(Oral method)



entavita ceyaliyum ceytu kāṭṭāmal, veṟumaṉē vāyviḷakkattuṭaṉ, pāṭatti naṭattutal. viḷakkattiṟkup piṟaku, māṇavarkaḷi ceyyumāṟu tūṇṭutal,

uṭaṟkalvittuṟikku itu poruttamāṉa muṟiyalla. uṭaṟkalvi eṉpatu ceytu koṇṭē kaṟkiṉṟa kalviyākum.

eṉṟālum viḷakka muṟi eṉpatil, aṉṟāṭa aṟivuc ceytikaḷi, viḷakkamāka, teḷivākak kūṟutal: eḷiya coṟkaḷil, iṉimiyāka, puriyumpaṭi, kēṭka makiḻcci uṇṭākumpaṭi pēcutal; terintatiliruntu teriyāta karuttukkaḷik kūṟutal pōṉṟaviyum aṭaṅkum.

2. kalanturiyāṭal muṟi

(Discussion method)



āciriyar māṇavar, pāṭap poruḷ paṟṟip pēci, kalanturiyāṭik kaṟṟuk koḷkiṟamuṟi.

ituvum vāymoḻi viḷakkamuṟiyic cārntatu tāṉ.

māṇavarkaḷ taṅkaḷ cantēkattik kēṭkavum, āciriyar tīrttu vikkavum kūṭiya cantarppatti, inta kaṟpikkum muṟi koṭuttu utavukiṟatu.

inta muṟiyil ceyal vāyppu illiyeṉṟālum, māṇavarkaḷi uṟcākamākap paṅku peṟa utavukiṟatu. cuya veḷippāṭṭukku uṟcākam aḷikkiṟatu.

3. ceyal viḷakka muṟi (Demonstration method)

āciriyar curukkamāka col viḷakkattuṭaṉ, payiṟcikaḷi colliyapaṭi ceytu kāṭṭuvār. āciriyariṉ ceyal viḷakkatti aṟintu koṇṭu, māṇavarkaḷum ceytu paḻakuvar.

ceyal viḷakkamuṟitāṉ ciṟappāṉa kaṟpikkum muṟi eṉṟu ellōrālum ēṟṟukkoḷḷappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.

ceyalviḷakkamuṟi eppaṭi amiya vēṇṭum eṉṟu pārppōm.

ceyalviḷakkamāṉatu cariyākavum tavaṟillāmalum, irukka vēṇṭum colliya vaṇṇamē ceytu kāṭṭa vēṇṭum.

ceyal viḷakkam mutalil metuvāka amintu, piṟaku paṭippaṭiyāka vēkam peṟa vēṇṭum:

mukkiyamāṉa tiṟaṉ nuṇukkaṅkaḷi, tērcci peṟṟa māṇavarkaḷik koṇṭu kaṟpikkiṟa pōtu, avarkaḷum payaṉ peṟukiṉṟārkaḷ. pārkkiṉṟavarkaḷukkum aṉupavamum, teḷivum piṟakkiṟatu.

4. pārttuc ceyyum muṟi

(Imitation method)

ituvum oruvakiyil, ceyalviḷakkam pōṉṟatutāṉ.

kuṟippiṭṭap payiṟciyāṉatu, ēṟkaṉavē māṇavarkaḷukku terintatāka iruntāl; allatu eḷitil ceyyak kūṭiya payiṟciyāka iruntāl, āciriyar ceyyum pōḻutē, pārttu, māṇavarkaḷ kaṟṟuk koḷḷa muṭiyum. āciriyarip piṉpaṟṟi, pārttatu pārttapaṭiyē ceyyum pakkuvam tara, inta muṟi iṉitāka utavukiṟatu.

pārttuc ceyyum muṟi, ceyal viḷakka muṟikku utavuvatākavum irukkiṟatu.

5. nāṭaka muṟi

(Dramatization method)

itu oru puttuṇarvūṭṭum pōtikkum muṟiyākum. palavakippaṭṭa mirukaṅkaḷ, paṟavikaḷ, vākaṉaṅkaḷ pōṉṟu naṭantu kāṭṭutal. paṟavikaḷ pōl ceytu kāṭṭutal. ūrtikaḷ pōl ūrntu kāṭṭutal.

ivaṟṟip pōlavēkati viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷum, pāṭi viḷiyāṭum viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷum uṇṭu. katiyi naṭittukkāṭṭi māṇavarkaḷ viḷiyāṭuvar.

nāṭaka muṟiyāṉatu, kuḻantikaḷukku ēṟṟatu. inta muṟi, māṇavarkaḷiṉ kaṟpaṉit tiṟaṉiyumṭi puṉivāṟṟaliyum vaḷarkkum vāyppaḷikkiṟatu.

6. viruppa muṟi (At will Method)

intap pōtaṉā muṟiyil, avaravar viruppattiṟkēṟpa payiṟci ceyyumpaṭi, māṇavarkaḷ cutantiramāka aṉumatikkappaṭukiṉṟārkaḷ.

entap payiṟciyil avarkaḷukku īṭupāṭu irukkiṟatō, avaṟṟi tārāḷamāka, taṉṉicciyāka ceytu, tērcci peṟukiṉṟārkaḷ.

inta muṟi māṇavarkaḷukku makiḻcciyiyum, māṟāta taṉṉampikkiyiyum vaḷarttuk koḷkiṟa vāyppukkaḷi vaḻaṅkukiṟatu.

7. tāḷalaya payiṟci muṟi (Set drill Method)

uṭaṟkalviyil uḷḷa ciṟappāṉa payiṟci muṟikaḷi, tāḷalaya nayattuṭaṉ kaṟpikkiṉṟa muṟiyākum.

lecim, ṭampels, perum kaḻikaḷ, kuṟuntaṭikaḷ koṇṭu ceyyum payiṟcikaḷuṭaṉ, veṟuṅkiyuṭaṉ ceyyum payiṟcikaḷ aṉittum tāḷattuṭaṉ, layam māṟākat toṭarntu ceyvatu ippayiṟci muṟiyiṉ mukkiyattuvamākum.

tāḷalayattuṭaṉ ceykiṟapōtu, māṇavarkaḷukku itu pērārvattiyum, perum maṉakkiḷarcciyiyum perukkeṭuttōṭaviṭukiṟatu.

8. muḻumiyāṉa muṟi (Whole Method)

oru payiṟciyip pākam pākamākap pirittuk kaṟpikkāmal, muḻumiyākavē, atik kaṟpittuk koṭukkum muṟikkē, muḻumiyāṉa muṟi eṉṟu peyar.

4. pārttuc ceyyum muṟi

(Imitation method)

ituvum oruvakiyil, ceyalviḷakkam pōṉṟatutāṉ.

kuṟippiṭṭap payiṟciyāṉatu, ēṟkaṉavē māṇavarkaḷukku terintatāka iruntāl; allatu eḷitil ceyyak kūṭiya payiṟciyāka iruntāl, āciriyar ceyyum pōḻutē, pārttu, māṇavarkaḷ kaṟṟuk koḷḷa muṭiyum.

āciriyarip piṉpaṟṟi, pārttatu pārttapaṭiyē ceyyum pakkuvam tara, inta muṟi iṉitāka utavukiṟatu.

pārttuc ceyyum muṟi, ceyal viḷakka muṟikku utavuvatākavum irukkiṟatu.

5. nāṭaka muṟi

(Dramatization method)

itu oru puttuṇarvūṭṭum pōtikkum muṟiyākum.

palavakippaṭṭa mirukaṅkaḷ, paṟavikaḷ, vākaṉaṅkaḷ pōṉṟu naṭantu kāṭṭutal. paṟavikaḷ pōl ceytu kāṭṭutal. ūrtikaḷ pōl ūrntu kāṭṭutal.

ivaṟṟip pōlavēkati viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷum, pāṭi viḷiyāṭum viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷum uṇṭu. katiyi naṭittukkāṭṭi māṇavarkaḷ viḷiyāṭuvar.

nāṭaka muṟiyāṉatu, kuḻantikaḷukku ēṟṟatu. inta muṟi, māṇavarkaḷiṉ kaṟpaṉit tiṟaṉiyumṭi puṉivāṟṟaliyum vaḷarkkum vāyppaḷikkiṟatu.

mutaṉmi viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷi, maṟṟum ōṭukaḷap pōṭṭi nikaḻccikaḷik kaṟpikka, inta muṟi ciṟappāṉatākum.

11. kaṭṭaḷi muṟi (Command Method)

oru ceyalic ceyyumāṟu māṇavarkaḷip paṇikka, intak kaṭṭaḷi muṟi payaṉpaṭukiṟatu.

entac ceyali, eppoḻutu, eppaṭic ceyya vēṇṭum eṉpati kaṭṭaḷi mūlam āciriyar māṇavar kaḷukkuk kaṟṟut tarukiṟār.

itaṟkākap payaṉpaṭum coṟkaḷik kaṭṭaḷic coṟkaḷ eṉṟu aḻikkiṟōm.

inta kaṭṭaḷi muṟi, iraṇṭu vakippaṭukiṟatu.

1. uṇarttum kaṭṭaḷi muṟi (Response Command)

2. toṭarntu ceyyum kaṭṭaḷi muṟi

(Rhythmic Command)

uṇarttum kaṭṭaḷi muṟi eṉpatu, oru kuṟippiṭṭa ceyaliṉ ovvoru niliyiyum viḷakki, tirutti, nuṭpamākac ceyya vikkum ciṟappuṭiyatu.

itiyum mūṉṟu kūṟākak kaṟṟuttarak kūṭum.

a) āciriyar pakuti pakutiyākap pakutiyiṉi viḷakkik kūṟum muṟi. iti (Explanation Stage) piḷakkum nili eṉpar.

ā) viḷakkik kūṟiya ceyaliṉ nili, māṇavarkaḷ maṉatil patiyavum, purintu koḷḷavum kūṭiya vakiyil nēram otukkit tarum nili. (Pause Stage)

﻿i) kuṟippiṭṭa ceyalic ceytu kaṟkum nili. (Execution Stage)

iraṇṭāvatu kaṭṭaḷi muṟiyāṉatu. ovvoru niliyilum, niṟuttāmal, toṭarntu, tāḷalayattuṭaṉ payiṟciyic ceyvatākum.

12. muṉṉēṟṟam tarum pakuti muṟi

(Progressive Part method)

oru ceyalukkup pala nilikaḷ uṇṭu eṉpati nām aṟivōm. inta muṟiyil kaṭippiṭikka vēṇṭiya yukti eṉṉaveṉṟāl, ovvoru niliyiyum. ovvoṉṟākavum, tokuttum kaṟpikkum nuṭpamākum.

utāraṇamāka : oru payiṟciyil mutal niliyik kaṟṟut taruvatu, aṭuttu 2vatu niliyik kaṟpikkum muṉ mutal niliyiyum kaṟṟut tantu, piṉ toṭarvatu. piṟaku mutal iraṇṭu niliyiyum kaṟṟut tantuviṭṭu mūṉṟāvatu niliyik kaṟṟut taruvatu.

ippaṭiyāka, paṭippaṭiyāy nilikaḷit toṭarntu kaṟṟut tantu, ceyaliṉ muḻu nilikku muṉṉēṟṟik koṇṭu varum muṟiyē ituvākum.

ōṭukaḷap payiṟcikaḷukkum, tāḷalayap payiṟcikaḷukkum inta muṟi, ciṟanta kaṟpikkum pāṅkāka amikiṉṟatu.

13. māṭcimiyuḷḷa kāṭci muṟi

māṇavarkaḷukku uṟcāka muṟiyil kaṟpikka utavum muṟiyāka itik koḷḷalām.

māvaṭṭa, mānila, tēciya aḷavilāṉa viḷiyāṭṭup pōṭṭikaḷukku aḻittuc ceṉṟu, māṇavarkaḷukkuk kāṭṭi, viḷakki, tērcci peṟṟavarkaḷiṉ nuṇukkattiṟaṉ, (Tactics) nuṇ āṭci muṟi, ciṟanta aṇuku muṟikaḷ (Techniques) pōṉṟavaṟṟi nērmukamākak kāṭṭik kūṟukiṟapōtu, māṇavarkaḷukku uṟcākam perukuvatuṭaṉ, kaṟkum tiṟaṉum kūṭip perukik koḷkiṟatu.

ittakiya vāyppukkaḷ illātapōtu, mēṟkūṟiya kāṭcikaḷ niṟinta tirippaṭaṅkaḷik kāṭṭi, tirumpat tirumpak kāṇac ceykiṟapōtu, aṟivum virivākkam koḷkiṟatu.

ciyōl olimpik pantayattil, peṇkaḷ 100, 200 mīṭṭar ōṭṭaṅkaḷil veṉṟu ulaka cātaṉi purinta puḷōraṉs kiripat jāyṉar, ciṟanta oṭṭa vīrarāṉa peṉ jāṉcaṉ oṭip paḻakiya tirippaṭattip pārttup pārttu tērcci peṟṟār eṉṟa varalāṟu, inta māṭcimi miku kaṇkāṭci muṟikku ciṟanta cāṉṟāka amikiṟatu.

nammavarkaḷum inta muṟiyip piṉpaṟṟip perum payaṉaṭiyalām.





9. kaṭṭaḷikaḷ koṭukkum muṟi

(COMMANDS)



* * *



ceṉṟa pakutiyil kaṭṭaḷi muṟi eṉṟa oru kaṟpikkum muṟiyiyum, atil iru pakutiyāka uṇarttum kaṭṭaḷi muṟi. toṭarntu ceyyum kaṭṭaḷi muṟi eṉṟu uḷḷaṉa eṉpatiyum aṟintu koṇṭōm.

inta iru pakutikaḷiyum ceyalpaṭutta, evvāṟu: kaṭṭaḷikaḷ koṭuppatu eṉpati, ippakutiyil kāṇpōm.

1. uṇarttum kaṭṭaḷi muṟi (Response Command)

iti maṟu ceyal kaṭṭaḷi muṟi eṉṟum kūṟuvārkaḷ.

inta muṟiyi mūṉṟu niliyākap pirittuk kaṭṭaḷiyiṭa vēṇṭum.

1. viḷakka muṟi (Explanation)

2. nēram tarum muṟi (Pause)

3. ceyalpaṭac ceytal (Execution)

im mūṉṟiyum oṉṟākki eppaṭi kaṭṭaḷi koṭuppatu?

kikaḷi mēṟpuṟamāka uyarttu eṉpatu āciriyar kaṭṭaḷiyiṭa vēṇṭiya muṟi

1. kikaḷi mēṟpuṟamāka -cattamāka atikārattuṭaṉ kaṭṭaḷiyiṭutal.

2. piṟaku ciṟitu nēram tantuviṭṭu

3. uyarttu eṉpatik kaṭṭaḷiyiṭutal.

ceyya vēṇṭiya payiṟciyiṉi mutal niliyākak koṇṭu viḷakki, iṭiyil niṟutti nēram tantu, iṟutiyāka uratta kuralil eṉṉa ceyya vēṇṭum eṉpatik kūṟuvatutāṉ ciṟanta kaṭṭaḷi koṭukkum muṟiyākum.

2. toṭarntu ceyyum kaṭṭaḷi muṟi

(Rhythmic Command)

kaṟṟuk koṇṭa oru payiṟciyi, māṇavarkaḷ toṭarntu, tirumpat tirumpa, tāḷalayattuṭaṉ ceyya vēṇṭiyirukkiṟa poḻutu, ataṟkāka utavuvatu tāṉ. intak kaṭṭaḷi muṟi.

intak kaṭṭaḷiyiyum 5 niliyil kūṟa vēṇṭum.

1. viḷakkam (Explanation)

2. nēram tarutal (Pause)

3. ceyalit toṭaṅkutal (Execution)

4. toṭarntu tāḷalayattil eṇṇutal (Rhythmic Counting)

5. payiṟciyi niṟuttal (Halting)

utāraṇattiṟku oru payiṟciyip pārppōm.

kikaḷi pakkavāṭṭiliruntu, talikku mēlē uyartti, piṟaku pakkavāṭṭil koṇṭu vantu, toṅkaviṭu. eṉpatu tāṉ payiṟci.

intap payiṟciyi 12345678 eṉṟu eṇṇat toṭaṅki

87654321 eṉṟu niṟuttu.





ivvāṟu viḷakki, nēram tantu, ceyalit toṭaṅkic ceyyak kaṟṟuttanta piṟaku, aṭuttatāka, toṭarntu ceyyuṅkaḷ eṉṟu kaṭṭaḷi iṭavum.

ati ippaṭi āṅkilattil kūṟa vēṇṭum.

Continuously and Rhythmically, class... Begin

toṭaṅku eṉṟu colliya uṭaṉēyē, muṉ viḷakkiyatu pōla 12345678 eṉṟu mēl nōkki eṇṇi, piṟaku kīḻnōkki iṟakkik koṇṭuvaravum.

iṟakkivarukiṟa pōtu 43 eṉṟu vantavuṭaṉ, payiṟciyi māṟṟa vēṇṭumāṉāl, payiṟciyi - māṟṟu (Exercise change) eṉṟu māṟṟik kūṟavēṇṭum.

payiṟciyi niṟuttiṭa vēṇṭumāṉāl, 8liruntu iṟakkik koṇṭuvantu 4, 3 eṉṟu vantavuṭaṉ, payiṟciyi niṟuttu (Class Halt) eṉṟu kūṟi niṟutta vēṇṭum.

876543 payiṟciyi niṟuttu eṉṟu iraṇṭu eṇṇik kiyil payiṟciyi niṟuttuvatu tāṉ ciṟanta muṟi eṉṟu ellōrālum ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.

payiṟcikaḷi māṟṟa nēriṭumpōtu, Next change eṉṟum (aṭuttataṟku māṟṟu); valappuṟam vakuppit tiruppi payiṟciyic ceyyac colkiṟa pōtu (Right-change) valappuṟam māṟṟu eṉṟum.

oṉṟu māṟṟi oṉṟākap payiṟciyit toṭarumpōci (Alter-mate) māṟi māṟi eṉṟum kaṭṭaḷiyiṭalām.

ippaṭiyāka vacatikkēṟṟavāṟu, māṇavarkaḷiṉ eḷitāṉa iyakkattiṟkum; turitamāṉa ceyalkaḷukkum, kaṭṭaḷikaḷi māṟṟik koṇṭu, kampīramākak koṭukkiṟapōtu, vakuppu māṇavarkaḷukkum viṟuviṟuppu irukkum. cuṟucuṟuppu niṟintirukkum, ceykiyil putup polivum teḷivum cūḻntirukkum.

ākavē, kaṭṭaḷi koṭukkum muṟikaṉi, kaṇakkāka kaccitamāka āciriyarkaḷ kaṟṟut tarukiṟa pōtu. kaṟpikkum kaliyil kavarcciyum eḻucciyum karipuraṇṭu, kāṇpataṟku ariya kāṭciyākat tikaḻum.





﻿10. uṭal iyakka ceyalkaḷik kaṟpikkum

vaḻi muṟikaḷ

(TEACHING OF PHYSICAL ACTIVITIES)



* * *



uṭaliyakka ceyalkaḷ eṉpavi, kīkḻkāṇum vakikaḷākap. pirikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.

1. kaṭṭaḻakukkalip payiṟcikaḷ (Calisthenies)

2. intiya nāṭṭut tēkap payiṟcikaḷ (lndigenous Activities)

3. aṇinaṭip payiṟci (Marching)

4. ciṟu viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷ (Minor games)

5. mutaṉmi viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷ (Major games)

6. tāḷalayap payiṟcikaḷ (RhythmicActivities)

7. jimṉāsṭiks payiṟcikaḷ (Gymnastics)

8. taṟkāppuk kalikaḷ (Defensive Arts)

9. oṭukaḷap pōṭṭi nikaḻccikaḷ (Track and Field events)

10. nīccal pōṭṭi nikaḻccikaḷ (Swimming)

ittakiya payiṟcikaḷi eppaṭip payiṟṟuvikka vēṇṭum eṉṟa muṟikaḷi aṟintu koḷḷumuṉ, kaṟpikka vacatiyāka, māṇavarkaḷi eppaṭip pirikka vēṇṭum, niṟutti vikka vēṇṭum eṉṟa muṟikaḷi mutalil terintu koḷvōm.

oḻuṅkupaṭuttum payiṟcikaḷukkāṉa vakuppu aṇi muṟi.

vakuppu māṇavarkaḷi mutalil oru variciyil niṟutti (Single line) vittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

ataṟkāṉa kaṭṭaḷikaḷ



1. māṇavarkaḷē ! oru variciyil nilluṅkaḷ

(In a line - class - Fall in)



intak kaṭṭaḷiyit toṭarntu, māṇavarkaḷil kuḷḷamāṉavar valappuṟam iruppatu pōl niṟka, aṭuttu uyaramāṉavarkaḷ eṉṟa niliyil ōraṇivariciyil niṟka vēṇṭum.

2. nērē - fisio (Atten-Tion) kikaḷ pakkavāṭṭil uṭaluṭaṉ oṭṭiyapaṭi irukka, kālkaḷ iraṇṭiyum iṇittāṟpōl vittu, nimirntu niṟkac ceyyavum. aciyāmal niṟka vēṇṭum. 3. iyalpāka... nil (Stand at ease)

māṇavarkaḷ kikaḷip piṉpuṟamākak kaṭṭik koṇṭu, kālkaḷi akalamāka virittu niṟṟal.

4. oyvāka... nil (Stand at ease)

māṇavarkaḷ viṟippāka nimirntu niṟpatit tavirttu, kālkaḷi irunta iṭattiliruntu acikkāmal oyvāka niṟṟal.

5. valappuṟamiruntu - cīc cey (Right Dress) kuḷḷamāṉa mutalāvatu māṇavar, nērākap pārttu niṟka, valap puṟamāka māṇavarkaḷ taṅkaḷ taliyip valap puṟamākat tiruppi, taṅkaḷatu variciyi nērākac cīr ceyya vēṇṭum.

6. nērē - pār (Eyes - Front) māṇavarkaḷ taṅkaḷ talikaḷit tiruppi, muṉpuṟamākap pārkka vēṇṭum. tali tāṉ acintu pārkka vēṇṭumē tavira, nērē nil eṉṟa niliyil tāṉ kālkaḷ niṟka vēṇṭum.

itu vari oru nērkkōṭṭu varicikkāṉa kaṭṭaḷi muṟiyi aṟintu koṇṭōm. (Line or Rank).

aṭuttu, aṭukku varici muṟi (File or Column)

7. or aṭukkil nil (Class-in a File-Fall in)

kaṭṭaḷiyik kēṭṭatum, kuḷḷamāṉavar mutalil niṟka, avaratu piṉṉāl aṭuttaṭuttu uyaramāṉavarkaḷ niṉṟu koḷḷa vēṇṭum. itu tāṉ aṭukku muṟi eṉpatākum.

2. nērē - nil (Atten - Tion)

māṇavarkaḷ kālkaḷi cērttu, nimirntu, kikaḷ uṭalōṭu oṭṭiyirukka, virippāka niṟka vēṇṭum.

3. muṉṉōkkic - cari cey (Forward - Dress)

mutal māṇavaṉ nimirntu aciyāmal niṟka, avaṉ piṉṉē uḷḷavarkaḷ taṅkaḷ valakkiyi nērāka, muṉṉōkkit tūkki, taṅkaḷ aṇiyiṉ aṭukki cīr ceyya vēṇṭum. avarkaḷ valak kiyi, muṉpuṟamāka uyarttiyē irukka vēṇṭum.

4. kikaḷit - tāḻttu (Arms - Sink)

kaṭṭaḷikkup piṟaku, kiyit toṅka viṭṭu viṭṭu, mīṇṭum nimirnta niliyilē, viṟippākavē niṟka vēṇṭum.

iṉi vakuppi payiṟcikku ēṟpa vacatiyāka niṟuttu vataṟkākak koṇṭu varukiṟa muṟiyik kāṇpōm.iti aṇi tiṟappu muṟi eṉpārkaḷ.

aṇi tiṟappu muṟi (Open Order Formation) māṇavarkaḷ oru nērk kōṭṭu variciyil irukkum pōtu, piṉpaṟṟum kaṭṭaḷi muṟi iṅkē koṭukkappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.

1. oṟṟi nērk kōṭṭu aṇi - ataṉ tiṟappu muṟi 1. valappuṟamiruntu mūṉṟu mūṉṟāka ... eṇṇu (From the Right in Threes ...... Count)

māṇavarkaḷ valatu puṟattiṉ ārampattiliruntu, 1, 2, 3, 1, 2, 3 eṉṟu eṇṇik koṇṭē varavēṇṭum.

eṇṇukiṟa ovvoruvarum, taṉ taliyi iṭap puṟamākat tiruppi, cattamāka, cuṟu cuṟuppākak kūṟa vēṇṭum.

2. oṉṟukaḷ ellām avaravar iṭattilē nilluṅkaḷ



iraṇṭukaḷ ellām iraṇṭu kālaṭikaḷ. (Steps)



mūṉṟukaḷ ellām nāṉku kālaṭikaḷ.

muṉṉōkki ...... naṭa·





Number ONES - stay where you are



Number TWOS - Two steps



Number THREES - Four steps



Open Order Forward - March



āciriyar oṉṟu, iraṇṭu, mūṉṟu, nāṉku eṉṟu eṇṇukiṟa pōtu; oṉṟukaḷ aṅkēyē niṟka, iraṇṭukaḷ oṉṟukku oru kālaṭi, iraṇṭukku iraṇṭāvatu kālaṭikaḷi vittu naṭantu niṟka; mūṉṟukaḷ mūṉṟu nāṉku eṇṇikkikku naṭantu vantu niṟka vēṇṭum.

paṭam pārkkavum (Forward March)





mēlē viḷakkiyatu muṉṉōkki naṭakkum muṟi.



piṉ nōkki naṭakkac ceyvatu iraṇṭāvatu muṟi.

kaṭṭaḷi : oṉṟukaḷ aṅkēyē nilluṅkaḷ.

﻿iraṇṭukaḷ iraṇṭu kālaṭikaḷ muṉṉōkkiyum

﻿mūṉṟukaḷ iraṇṭu kālaṭikaḷ piṉṉōkkiyum

﻿- naṭa.

⁠Number ONES - Stay where you are

⁠Number TWOS - Two steps FORWARD

⁠Number THREES - Two steps Back ward

⁠Open Order ...... March.





iṉi aṭukku muṟi aṇiyiliruntu, tiṟappu muṟiyik kāṇpōm. (From File -Open order Formation).

mutalil māṇavarkaḷi aṭukku muṟiyil niṟutti, nērē nil, iyalpāka nil, nērē pār - cīr cey eṉṟellām kaṭṭaḷiyiṭṭu niṟkac ceyta piṟaku — :

1. muṉpuṟamiruntu mūṉṟu mūṉṟāka - eṇṇu.

(From the Front in Threes - Count)

⁠2. oṉṟukaḷ - aṅkēyē nilluṅkaḷ

﻿iraṇṭukaḷ - iṭappakkam iraṇṭu kālaṭikaḷ

﻿mūṉṟukaḷ - valap pakkam iraṇṭu kālaṭikaḷ,

﻿naṭantu ... cel. – 6 Number ONES - Stay where you are.

Number TWOS - Two steps to the Left.

Number THREES - Two steps to the Right

Open Order ... March

āciriyar eṇṇa eṇṇa, māṇavarkaḷ iṭap puṟamum valappuṟamumāka pirintu ceṉṟu niṟka vēṇṭum.

paṭam pārkka





pala aṭukkukaḷil, māṇavarkaḷi niṟutti vittiruntālum, ōr aṭukku muṟi pōlavē, pirittu vittu, payiṟcikaḷic ceykiṟa vacatiyāṉa niliyi ēṟpaṭuttik koḷḷavum. payiṟcikaḷukkēṟpa, pirittu vikkum muṟi kaḷil tēviyāṉa vakikaḷi cērttuk koḷḷavum.

iṉi, māṇavarkaḷi, mīṇṭum oru varicikku allatu aṭukku nilikkuk koṇṭu varum kaṭṭaḷi muṟiyik kāṇpōm.

mīṇṭum-uṉ iṭam nōkki-naṭa (Close order-march)

intak kaṭṭaḷiyip piṉpaṟṟi, māṇavarkaḷ taṅkaḷ iṭappuṟamō, valappuṟamō ceṉṟiruntālum, taṅkaḷatu paḻiya iṭam nōkki vantu viṭavēṇṭum.

āciriyarukku cila kuṟippukaḷ

1. aṇit tiṟappumuṟi naṭipeṟṟavuṭaṉ, āciriyar māṇavarkaḷi ōyvu niliyil (Stand at ease) niṟkac ceyya vēṇṭum.

2. māṇavarkaḷ iṭappuṟam payiṟci ceyya vēṇṭiyiruppiṉ, āciriyar taṉatu valappuṟattil ceytu kāṭṭa vēṇṭum. atupōlavē, valappuṟam māṇavarkaḷ ceytiṭa iṭappuṟamāka ceytu kāṭṭa vēṇṭum.

3 āciriyar eṇṇikkippaṭi, ovvoru payiṟciyiyum ceytu kāṭṭi, māṇavarkaḷi kavaṉamākki, karuttil patiya vikka vēṇṭum.

4. māṇavarkaḷi ceyya vittu, tavaṟukaḷit tirutta vēṇṭum.

5. cariyāka māṇavarkaḷ ceykiṟārkaḷ eṉṟu aṟinta piṟaku, atit tāḷalayattuṭaṉ (Rhythmic) ceytiṭak kaṟpikka vēṇṭum.

6. āciriyar virumpukiṟa vakiyil, māṇavarkaḷ niṟkum aṇiyamippu muṟiyi (Formation) māṟṟi niṟka vittu, payiṟcikaḷik kaṟpikkalām: iṉi, ovvoru uṭaliyakkac ceyalkaḷiyum kaṟpik kiṉṟa muṟikaḷi, curukkamāka kāṇpōm.

1 kaṭṭaḻaku tarum kalip payiṟcikaḷ (Calisthenics)

kaṭṭaḻaku tarum kalippayiṟcikaḷ eṉpatu, entavita eṭi cātaṉaṅkaḷumiṉṟi veṟuṅkiyuṭaṉ ceyyum payiṟcikaḷ ākum.



﻿naṉṟāka mūcciḻuttu, cuvācatti viṭṭu ceykiṟa veṟum payiṟcikaḷuṭaṉ, ṭampels, vāṇs eṉkiṟa kuṟuntaṭi payiṟcikaḷiyum, intap payiṟcikaḷuṭaṉ cērttuk koḷḷalām.

ippaṭippaṭṭa, uṭalaḻakup payiṟcikaḷi, iraṇṭu vakiyākap pirikkalām.

1. eṇṇikkiyuṭaṉ ceyvatu (Formal)

2. viruppam pōl ceyvatu (In formal) eṇṇikkiyuṭaṉ ceyvatu paḷḷi māṇaviyarkku tēviyāṉatākum. appoḻututāṉ, aṉivarum cērntu, oruṅkiṇintu ceyalpaṭum cīriṉi uṇṭākki viṭa muṭiyum.

mēlum viḷakkattiṟku, muṉṉar kūṟiyuḷḷa oḻuṅku paṭuttum payiṟcikaḷ eṉṟa pakutiyil kāṇka.

2. intiyat tēkap payiṟcikaḷ (Indigenous Activities)

intiyat tēkap payiṟcikaḷi nām 5 pirivākap pirittu aṟintu koḷḷalām.

1 . uṭalaḻakup payiṟcikaḷ

(a) eṭiyaṟṟa payiṟcikaḷ (taṇṭāl, paski)

(ā) eṭiyuṭaṉ payiṟcikaḷ (karaḷā kaṭṭi)

(i) cātaṉa utaviyuṭaṉ payiṟcikaḷ (mallar kampam)



2. yōkācaṉaṅkaḷ

3. kuḻu viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷ (kōkō, kapaṭi, aṭṭiya paṭṭiyā)

4. tāḷalayap payiṟcikaḷ (lecim, naṭaṉam)

5.taṟkāppuk kalippayiṟcikaḷ (malyuttam, laṭṭi)

2-1. uṭalaḻakup payiṟcikaḷ taṇṭāl paski

uṭalaḻakup payiṟcikaḷil mutalāvatu varuvatu taṇṭāl, paskip payiṟcikaḷ.

taṇ eṉṟa vaṭamoḻikku pujam eṉṟu poruḷ. intap payiṟci, kikaḷi valimiyuṭiyatākkum.

paskip payiṟcikaḷ, kāl tacikaḷi uṟutiyākkum.

intap payiṟcikaḷ koñcam kaṭumi niṟintatākum. āṉāl, atika eṇṇikkiyil ceykiṟa pōtu, kālkaḷum kikaḷum, nalla uṟutiyum valimiyum aṭiyum.

kaṣṭamāṉa payiṟci eṉpatāl māṇavarkaḷukkuk koñcaṅ koñcamākak kaṟpittu, piṟaku eṇṇikkiyi atikappaṭuttiṭa vēṇṭum.

taṇṭāl, paskip payiṟcikaḷit tērnteṭukkiṟa pōtu, vayatu, iṉam, māṇavar tiṟami mutaliyavaṟṟik kaṇakkil koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

payiṟṟuvikkum muṟi : vakuppi aṇit tiṟappu muṟiyil varacceytu, payiṟcikaḷ ceyya pōtiya iṭiveḷi koṭuttu, eṇṇikki muṟiyil kaṟpikka vēṇṭum.

utāraṇattiṟku oru paski ceyyum eṇṇikki muṟiyik kāṇpōm.

aṉumār paski.

1. nērāka, nimirntu viṟippāka niṟṟal (12" iṭiveḷi)

2. muḻaṅkālkaḷi pāti aḷavu maṭakki, arik kuntalāka (Squat) niṟṟal.

3. inta niliyiliruntu atē iṭattil, tuḷḷik kutittu iṭatu kāl oraṭi muṉṉēyum, valatu kāl ōraṭi piṉṉēyum vittu; iṭatu kiyi neṟṟikku muṉṉum, valatu kiyi mutukiṟkup piṉṉum vittal.

4. mutal nilikku varavum.

mēlum pala payiṟcikaḷit terintu koḷḷa, nāṉ eḻutiya 'intiya nāṭṭut tēkap payiṟcikaḷ’ eṉum puttakattip pārkkavum.

mallar kampam (Malcomb)

malyuttam ceypavarkaḷ, oru kampatti , etiriyāka pāvittu, pala piṭi muṟikaḷ pōṭṭu, malyuttam paḻakutal itaṟku utavum kampattiṟkup peyar mallar kampam.

cila mukkiyak kuṟippukkaḷ

1. mallar kampam niḻalmikunta iṭattil patikkappaṭa vēṇṭum.

2. kampatti cuṟṟiyuḷḷa tari mirutuvākavum, camamākavum irukka vēṇṭum.

3. viḷakkeṇṇey allatu ataṟku īṭāka uḷḷa poruḷ oṉṟu, kampattiṉ mītu taṭaviyiruttal nallatu. appoḻutu tāṉ atil vaḻavaḻappu irukkum.

4. māṇavarkaḷ jaṭṭiyuṭaṉ, allatu arikkāl caṭṭiyuṭaṉ tāṉ payiṟcikaḷic ceyya vēṇṭum.

5. taṉit taṉiyākavē māṇavarkaḷ payiṟcikaḷip payila vēṇṭum. āciriyar kaṭṭāyam aṅkē iruttu, utava vēṇṭum.

6. payiṟci ceyvataṟkumuṉ, patappaṭuttum payiṟcikaḷ tantirukka vēṇṭum.

7. kuṟippiṭṭa payiṟciyi muṉ kūṭṭiyē āciriyar allatu naṉku terinta māṇavar oruvar ceytu kāṭṭa vēṇṭum.

8. kampattil ēṟum pōtum, iṟaṅkum pōtum, kālkaḷāl piṭi pōṭum pōtum, āciriyar arukiliruntu utavutal vēṇṭum.

9. payiṟcikaḷi valatupuṟam, iṭatupuṟam eṉṟu māṟṟi māṟṟi ceytiṭumāṟu payiṟṟuvikka vēṇṭum.

10. eḷiya payiṟcikaḷil toṭaṅki, piṟaku kaṣṭamāṉa payiṟcikaḷukkuk koṇṭu pōvatu nallatu.

11. vayiṟu niṟiya cāppiṭṭu viṭṭu, intap payiṟciyil īṭupaṭakkūṭātu. vayiṟu kāliyāka iruppatu nallatu.

12. atikāli, allatu mālippoḻutu, intap payiṟcikaḷukku vacatiyāṉa nēramākum.

13. payiṟci muṭinta piṟaku, kampatti eṭuttu, pattiramāka vikkavum.

kuṟippu : intiyaṉ kiḷap eṉkiṟa karaḷā kaṭṭip payiṟcikaḷum, eṇṇikki aṭippaṭiyil ceyyappaṭukiṉṟa payiṟcikaḷ tām eṉpatāl, iṅkē viḷakkamāka eḻutavilli. oḻuṅkumuṟip payiṟcikaḷ taruvatu pōlavē, ituvum amintirukkiṟatu eṉpati aṟiyavum.

2-2. yōkācaṉaṅkaḷ

uṭal vaḷattiṟkum, uḷḷattiṉ nalattiṟkum uyarnta palaṉkaḷi viḷivikkum uṉṉata payiṟciyāka, ācaṉaṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. taṇṭāl payiṟcikaḷ kikaḷiṉ valimikkum; paskip payiṟcikaḷ kālkaḷiṉ valimikkum utavuvatupōla; ācaṉappayiṟcikaḷ vayiṟṟu uṟuppukaḷukkum mukkiyamāṉa muṟiyil valimiyi vaḷarttu viṭukiṉṟaṉa.

ippaṭippaṭṭa ācaṉaṅkaḷ nūṟṟukku mēṟpaṭṭatāka iruntālum, māṇava māṇavikaḷ ceyvataṟkeṉṟu amikkap peṟṟa ācaṉaṅkaḷ, 32 eṉṟum kūṟuvārkaḷ. avaṟṟi nām nāṉku vakikaḷākap pirittu ceyyalām.

1. niṉṟu koṇṭu ceyyum ācaṉaṅkaḷ.



2. uṭkārntu koṇṭu ceyyum ācaṉaṅkaḷ. 3. kuppuṟap paṭuttuk koṇṭu ceyyum ācaṉaṅkaḷ.

4. mallāntu paṭuttukkoṇṭu ceyyum ācaṉaṅkaḷ.

ācaṉattip payiṟṟuvikkum āciriyarukku cila kuṟippukkaḷ

1. kuṟinta aḷavu āṭiyuṭaṉ, ācaṉam ceyya vēṇṭum.

2. veṟuntariyil ācaṉam ceyyāmal, ētāvatu oru virippiṉ mēl ceyvatu nallatu.

3. ācaṉam ceyyum pōtu, vayiṟu kāliyāka irukkumāṟu pārttuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

4. atikāliyilō allatu mālippoḻutilō ācaṉam ceyvatu nallatu.

5. māṇavarkaḷukku ācaṉam ceyya iṭam iruppatu pōla, vaṭṭa vaṭiva amippil allatu ari vaṭṭa amippil māṇavarkaḷi iruttiṭa vēṇṭum.

6. kaṟṟuttarap pōkiṟa ācaṉattiṉ peyari mutalil kūṟi, ataṉ amippu, ceytāl kiṭikkum payaṉ mutaliyavaṟṟi viḷakki, ataṉi eṇṇikki muṟiyil eppaṭic ceyya vēṇṭum eṉpati, āciriyar ceyal viḷakkattuṭaṉ ceytu kāṭṭa vēṇṭum.

piṟaku māṇavarkaḷi, ovvoru eṇṇikkiyilum ceytiṭap paṇittu, kuṟikaḷi nivarttittu, piṟaku toṭarcciyākac ceyyumāṟu kaṟṟuttara vēṇṭum.

﻿7. ācaṉatti ārampikkiṟapōtu mūcciḻuttu, muṭikkiṟapōtu mūccuviṭukiṟa muṟiyil payiṟciyaḷikka vēṇṭum.

8. ācaṉam ceytu muṭikkiṟapōtu cavācaṉam eṉkiṟa ācaṉatti ceyyumāṟu aṟivuṟutti muṭittiṭa vēṇṭum.

9. ācaṉaṅkaḷ ceykiṟapōtu, āciriyar kaṭṭāyam arukē iruntu, kavaṉittu, kaṇkāṇittu, toṭara vēṇṭum. cūriya namaskāram eṉṟa ācaṉatti māṇavarkaḷukkuk kaṟpikkalām.

kuṟippu : payaṉ tarum yōkācaṉap payiṟcikaḷ eṉṉum eṉ puttakattil virivāka eḻutiyirukkiṟēṉ. āciriyarkaḷ paṭittum paṭippittum payaṉ peṟumāṟu kēṭṭuk koḷkiṟēṉ.

3. aṇi naṭip payiṟci (Marching)

aṇi naṭip payiṟci, māṇava māṇaviyarkku mikavum avaciyamākum. uṭaṟkalvip pāṭattiṭṭattil, kaṭṭāyam cērkkappaṭa vēṇṭiya pāṭamākum.

oḻuṅku (Discipline); kaṭṭaḷikkuk kīḻppa ṭital (obedience); tāḷalaya uṇarvu (Rhythm); nalla uṭal tōraṇi (Good posture), kuḻu uṇarvu (Group sense) mutaliya paṇpukaḷi aṇi naṭip payiṟci māṇavarkku arumiyāka vaḷarttu viṭukiṟatu.

aṇi naṭip payiṟciyik kaṟṟut tara, aṭippaṭi iyakkam paṟṟiya kuṟippukkaḷi, āciriyar viḷakkamāka mutalil kūṟa vēṇṭum.

kīḻkkāṇum kaṭṭaḷikaḷi āciriyar kūṟa, māṇavarkaḷ ceyya vēṇṭum.

1. oru variciyil nil (Stand in a Single line)

iyalpāka nil (Stand at ease)

nērē ... nil (Atten ...tion)

ōyvāka nil (Stand - easy)

piṟaku, variciyil nērāka niṟkac ceyyum kaṭṭaḷikaḷi āciriyar koṭukka vēṇṭum.

⁠valappuṟam ... caricey (Right...Dress)

⁠nērē ... pār (Atten...tion)

⁠iṭappuṟam ... pār (Left...Dress)

nērēpār eṉṟu aṟivippukkup piṟaku, 1, 2 eṉṟa eṇṇikkiyil, māṇavarkaḷi tirumpac ceyya (Turn) tarukiṟa kaṭṭaḷikaḷik koṭukka vēṇṭum.

⁠valappuṟam ... tiruppu (Left...Turn)
