⁠ iṭappuṟam ... pār (Left...Turn)

⁠piṉpuṟam ... pār (About...Turn)

ivvāṟu tirumpuvati (Turn) iraṇṭu eṇṇikkiyil māṇavarkaḷic ceyyumāṟu kaṟpikka vēṇṭum.

utāraṇamāka valappuṟam tirumpu eṉpatip pārppōm.

eṇṇikki 1: valatu kutikāl mītum, iṭatu kāl viralkaḷ mītum kālkaḷi cuḻaṟṟi, valatu pakkamākat tirumpa vēṇṭum.

eṇṇikki 2: iṭatu kāli valatu kāluṭaṉ koṇṭu vantu cērkka vēṇṭum. kālkaḷi cērttatum nimirntu, viṟippāka niṟka vēṇṭum nili naṭip payiṟci (Mark Time)



nili naṭi eṉpatu, niṉṟa iṭattilēyē kālkaḷi uyartti, iṟakki, naṭi pōṭukiṟa muṟiyākum.

niṟkum iṭattilēyē naṭa (Mark Time) eṉṟa kaṭṭaḷiyi āciriyar iṭa vēṇṭum.

variciyil viṟippāka niṟkum māṇavarkaḷ, iṭatu kālāl toṭaṅkuvar. iṭatu kāli uyarttum pōtu, iṭam (Left) eṉṟum, iṭatu kāli tariyil vittu, valatu kāli uyarttum pōtu valam (Right) eṉṟum āciriyar collik koṇṭē irukka vēṇṭum.

naṭiyi niṟuttu (Class ... halt) eṉṟatum, māṇa varkaḷ naṭiyi niṟuttuvar.

iṭatu kāl tarikku varum pōtu naṭiyi (Class) eṉṟum, valatu kāl tarikku varum pōtu niṟuttu (Halt) eṉṟum kūṟa vēṇṭum.

niṉṟa iṭattil naṭi pōṭak kaṟṟuk koṇṭa piṟaku, māṇavarkaḷukku, muṉṉōkki naṭa, iṭatu puṟam tirumpu, valatu puṟam tirumpu piṉ puṟam tirumpu eṉṟellām kaṭṭaḷiyiṭa, māṇavarkaḷ naṭakkum pōtē, tirumpiṭak kaṟṟuk koḷvar.

iṭatu puṟam, valatu puṟam eṉpataṟku 1, 2 eṉṟa iraṇṭu eṇṇikki eṇṇiṉāl pōtum.



piṉpuṟam tirumpu (About Turn) eṉṉum kaṭṭaḷikkum 2 eṇṇikki kūṟa vēṇṭum.

ovvoru muṟiyākap pārppōm.

vakuppu māṇavarkaḷi valatu pakkam tirumpac ceyya, valap puṟam tirumpu eṉṟu kaṭṭaḷi iṭa vēṇṭum.

valatu kāli tariyil vikkum poḻutu kaṭṭaḷi yit toṭaṅki, iṭatu kāl tarikku varum pōtu, iraṇṭāvatu kaṭṭaḷiyi muṭittu viṭa vēṇṭum.

piṉpuṟam tirumpu eṉṟa kaṭṭaḷiyi, ceyalpaṭuttum muṟi

kaṭṭaḷi - 1. iṭatu kāli iṭatu puṟamāka tariyōṭu tiruppi piṉpuṟamākak koṇṭu cellavum.

kaṭṭaḷi - 2. valatu kāli koṇṭu vantu, iṭatu kālōṭu cērttu viṭavum.

iṭap puṟam, valap puṟam, tirumpu eṉkiṟa pōtu, māṇavarkaḷ nērāka, viṟippāka uṭali vittut tirumpuvar.

ciṟitu vaḷivāka aṇiyi naṭakkac ceyya payaṉpaṭum kaṭṭaḷi Left...wheel Right wheel eṉpatākum.

iṭappuṟam vaḷintu cel (Left wheel) eṉṟa kaṭṭaḷi koṭuttatum, muṉ variciyil mutalāvatāka iṭap puṟam iruppavar; metuvākat tirumpa, muṉ variciyil valappuṟattil mutalāvatāka iruppavar vēkamāka iṭap puṟam vantu, aṇiyiṉ varici muṟiyi cari ceytu koḷḷa vēṇṭum.



tērcciyāṉa aṇinaṭi (Advanced Marching)

aṇi naṭiyil mukkiyamāṉa mūṉṟu tirumpum muṟikaḷik kaṟṟuk koṇṭa piṟaku. māṇavarkaḷukku. tērcci mikkatāṉa, kāṇpataṟkuk kavarcciyāṉa aṇi naṭi muṟikaḷik kaṟṟut taralām.

1. iraṭṭi aṇi naṭi (Double Time Marching)

2. metu aṇi naṭi (Slow Marching)

3. etir naṭi or maṟu naṭi (Counter Marching)

4. kōla naṭi (Figure Marching)

mukkiya kuṟippu

1. metu naṭi pōṭum pōtu maṇikku 80 kālaṭikaḷ (Step) irukkalām.

2. vēkanaṭi pōṭum pōtu maṇikku 120 kālaṭikaḷ.

3. iraṭṭinaṭi naṭakkum pōtu maṇikku 186 kālaṭikaḷ eṉṟu kaṇakkiṭṭuk koḷḷalām.

virivu allatu vēkanaṭiyi niṟutta 2 eṇṇik kikaḷ. iraṭṭi naṭiyi niṟutta 4 eṇṇikkikaḷil kaṭṭaḷi koṭuttu, niṟutta vēṇṭum.

4. ciṟu viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷ (Minor games)

culapamāṉa tiṟamikaḷiyum, eḷimiyāṉa vitikaḷiyum koṇṭatē ciṟu viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷākum.

ciṟu viḷiyāṭṭukaḷukku, viḷiyāṭṭu cātaṉaṅkaḷ atikam tēviyilli. kuṟinta iṭavacati, pōtum. nērakkaṭṭuppāṭum valiyuṟuttappaṭuvatilli. āṭṭakkārarkaḷiṉ kuṟippiṭṭa eṇṇikkiyum kaṭṭāyamilli

nērattiṟku ēṟpa, vacatikku ēṟpa, paṅku peṟupavar kaḷukku ēṟpa, vayatu vittiyācattiṟku ēṟpa, viḷiyāṭṭi amittuk koṇṭu, makiḻcci poṅka āṭuvatu tāṉ, ciṟuviḷiyāṭṭukkaḷiṉ ciṟapput taṉmiyākum.

ciṟu viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷiṉ pirivukaḷ

1. ōṭiviḷiyāṭum āṭṭam (Running game)

2. ōṭittoṭum āṭṭam (Tag game)

3. cātāraṇa pantāṭṭam (Simple ball games)

4. toṭar viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷ (Relays)

5. cākacac ceyalkaḷ (Stunts)

ciṟu viḷiyāṭṭik kaṟpikkum muṟikaḷ

1. viḷiyāṭṭukku ēṟpa, māṇavarkaḷi pirittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

2. cila māṇavarkaḷi āṭac colli, mātiri kāṭṭa vēṇṭum.

3. māṇavarkaḷi tiṟami vāriyāka kuḻuvākkiṭa vēṇṭum.

4. viḷiyāṭṭil paṅku peṟa iyalātavarkaḷi, āṭṭattik kaṇkāṇittu naṭattum poṟuppi aḷikka vēṇṭum.

5. paṅku peṟupavar ārvam kāṭṭi viḷiyāṭukiṟa variyil, āṭṭattit toṭara vēṇṭum.

atāvatu, āṭṭattil cuvāraciyam kuṟivataṟkuḷ. āṭṭatti niṟuttiviṭa vēṇṭum.

6. āṭṭam makiḻcciyiyē aḷittālum, āṭṭattiṉ vitikaḷi yārum mīṟātapaṭi, kaṭṭuppāṭṭukkuḷ aṭaṅki āṭukiṟapaṭi pārttuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

7. āciriyarum paṅkupeṟukiṟapōtu, māṇavarkku uṟcākamāka irukkum.

8. tōṟkiṟa kuḻuvit tēṟṟuvatum,veṟṟipeṟukiṟa kuḻuvi vāḻttuvatum āciriyar kaṭamiyākum.

9. viḷiyāṭṭit toṭaṅkuvataṟku muṉpāka, iṭatti cari ceytu tēviyāṉa utavip poruṭkaḷi āciriyar cēmittu vittiruppatu, ciṟappāṉa nirvākat tiṟku uttiravātamaḷikkum.

5 mutaṉmi viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷ (Major games)

mikavum nuṇmiyum, valimiyum niṟinta tiṟamikaḷ: virivupaṭuttappaṭṭu vaṟpuṟuttukiṉṟa vitimuṟikaḷ: atikamāṉa taramāṉa iṭavacati; tērcciyum ceḻuminiṟintatumāṉa utavi cātaṉaṅkaḷ; kuṟippiṭṭa nērak kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷ: ippaṭittāṉ āṭṭatti muṭikka vēṇṭum eṉṟa kaṭṭāya niyatikaḷ; ivvaḷavu āṭṭak kārarkaḷ irukka vēṇṭum eṉṟa vitikaḷ koṇṭaviyē. mutaṉmi viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷ eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

pōṭṭiyiṭukiṉṟa uṇarvukaḷukkut tīṉipōṭuvatu pōla, kuḻuvākak kūṭi āṭum vāyppukkaḷi ivi vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.

naramputtacikaḷ oruṅkiṇippu āṟṟal; vaḷarcci peṟukiṟa tiṟamikaḷ; kiḷarcci koḷkiṟatiṟaṉ nuṇukkaṅkaḷ talimip patavikkut takuti tarum cantarppaṅkaḷ ﻿viḷiyāṭṭup peruntaṉmi mutaliyavaṟṟi vaḷarttu viṭukiṉṟaṉa.

mutaṉmi viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷi, muḻumi pakuti muḻumi eṉṉum kaṟpikkum muṟiyil kaṟpikka vēṇṭum

kaṟpikkum muṟi

viḷiyāṭṭik kaṟpikkum muṉṉatāka, ataṉ toṭakkam: toṭakkattiṟkāṉa kāraṇam; āṭukaḷattiṉ aḷavu; āṭṭakkārarkaḷiṉ eṇṇikki; āṭṭattiṉ nōkkam, āṭṭattiṟkuriya utavi cātaṉaṅkaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟi, āciriyar teḷivāka, curukkamāka viḷakkiṭa vēṇṭum.

māṇavarkaḷi viḷiyāṭac ceykiṟa pōtu, ēṟpaṭukiṟa tavaṟukaḷit tirutta vēṇṭum. tēviyāṉa pōtu, vitimuṟikaḷiyum viḷakki, viḷiyāṭṭukkāṉa aṭippaṭi tiṟamikaḷiyum kuṟippiṭṭuk kaṟpikka vēṇṭum.

āciriyariṉ teḷivāṉa ceyal viḷakkam, māṇavarkkuk kaṟkiṉṟa ārvatti atikappaṭuttum.

māṇavarkaḷi pala ciṟu ciṟu kuḻukkaḷākap pirittu kaṟṟuk koḷḷa, payiṟci peṟa āciriyar utava vēṇṭum. ovvoru kuḻuviṟkum ceṉṟu, uṟcākap paṭutta vēṇṭum.

piṟaku, māṇavarkaḷi aḻittu, mīṇṭum avarkaḷi kuḻuvākap pirittu, muḻu āṭṭattiyum āṭa viṭa vēṇṭum. ﻿āṭṭattiṟkup piṟaku aṉivarum oṉṟukūṭi, kalan turiyāṭiṉāl, atu avarkaḷiṉ tiṟami vaḷara utavum.

6. tāḷa laya ceyalkaḷ (payiṟcikaḷ)

naḷiṉamāṉa uṭal iyakka acivukaḷi, naṭaṉam eṉpārkaḷ. ataṉitāḷamum layamum nayamāka iṇiyac ceytiṭa vēṇṭum. āṭiṭa vēṇṭum.

paratanāṭṭiyam, katakali, maṇippuri, kuccippuṭi pōṉṟavi uyartaramāṉa apinaya naṭaṉaṅkaḷākum. (Classical Dance)

kummi, kōlāṭṭam, pāṅkrā pōṉṟa naṭaṉaṅkaḷ kuḻuvāka āṭukiṟa kirāmiya naṭaṉaṅkaḷākum. (Folk dance)

uṭaṟ payiṟcikaḷi mikavum oyyāramāka kaṇkavar muṟiyil āṭukiṟa jimṉāsṭiks, lecim pōṉṟavikaḷyāvum, tāḷalayac ceyalkaḷukku utavuvaṉavākum.

tāḷalaya iyakkaṅkaḷi muṉṉēṟṟam tarum pakuti muṟi kaṟpikkum vaḻiyil (Progressive part method) kaṟpikkalām.

ovvoru iyakkattiyum, ovvoru kālaṭiyākak (Step) kaṟpippatu nallatu.

mutal kālaṭiyik kaṟpippatu (Step 1) piṟaku, iraṇṭāvatu kālaṭi iṭuvatik kaṟpippatu (Step 2). piṟaku mutaliraṇṭu kālaṭikaḷik kaṟpittu, 3vatu kālaṭiyik kaṟpippatu. ippaṭiyāka, ovvoṉṟiyum taṉittaṉiyē teḷivāka, kūṭṭākak kaṟpittāl, ciṟanta payaṉi etirpārkkalām.

kaṟpikkum āciriyarkku cila kuṟippukkaḷ

1. kaṟpikkum payiṟciyi, muḻumiyāka terintu koṇṭirukka vēṇṭum.

2. mutalil kālaṭi muṟiyik kaṟpittu, māṇavarkaḷ naṉku terintu koṇṭa piṟaku, ki acivu muṟiyik kaṟpittu, piṟaku iraṇṭiyum iṇittu muḻutākak kaṟpikka vēṇṭum.

3. māṇavarkaḷ kaṟṟu k koḷḷa kaṣṭappaṭukiṟa nērattil, āciriyar poṟumiyāka, viṭāmuyaṟciyuṭaṉ kaṟpikka vēṇṭum.

4. toṭarntu ceykiṟa muyaṟciyāl tāṉ, eḷimi yākak kaṟpikka muṭiyum. ākavē, eṇṇikki muṟiyil kaṟpittal nallatu.

5. eḷimiyāṉa acivukaḷiliruntu kaṣṭamāṉa acivukku, māṇavarkaḷi uṟcākattuṭaṉ koṇṭu cella vēṇṭum.

7. jimṉāsṭiks payiṟcikaḷ

jimṉāsṭiks payiṟcikaḷi, cīruṭal payiṟcikaḷ eṉṟu kūṟalām.

tāṉē taṉatu uṭali ciṟappākak kiyāṇṭu, cākacam mikunta ceyalkaḷic ceyvatu tāṉ cīruṭal payiṟcikaḷākum.

ittakiya cīruṭal payiṟcikaḷ uṭalukku valimi, nalla tiṟami uṟuppukkaḷ oruṅkiṇintu ceyalpaṭukiṉṟa āṟṟal, tiriyam, taṉṉampikki, puttikkūrmi, mūṉuṇarum muṉṉaṟivu; viḻippuṇarvu,cuṟucuṟuppu: tērnta nekiḻcci, turitamāka iyaṅkutal, pōṉṟa vallamikaḷi vaḷarttu viṭukiṉṟaṉa.

jimṉāsṭiks payiṟcikaḷi 4 vakiyākap pirikkalām



1. niṉṟu niliyākac ceyyum payiṟcikaḷ (Static





gymnastics)

2. Goog Gouéolo Luisb%hoir (Dynamic gymnastics)





3. Graf 35moor Luosi,956;t (Light gymnastics)





4. Gugyoutror Luo Sir (Heavy gymnastics)





1. niliyākac ceyyum payiṟcikaḷ

kuḻuvā c cērntu, kōpurappayiṟcikaḷ (Pyramids) ceyvati, itaṟku cāṉṟākak kūṟalām.

oruvar mēl oruvar niṉṟu allatu mutukiṉmēl amarntu, apapaṭiyē paṭippaṭiyāy iruntu, maṉitak kōpuram kaṭṭuvatu.

mikavum eccarikkiyuṭaṉ, karuttāṉa kavaṉattuṭaṉ intap payiṟcikaḷik kaṟpikka vēṇṭum.

kaṟpikkum muṟikaḷ

1. kōpurattiṉ aṭittaḷattil iruppavarkaḷ kaṉamāṉavarkaḷākavum; mēlē amarpavarkaḷ ilēcāka uḷḷavarkaḷākavum tērnteṭukka vēṇṭum.

2. kiyūṉṟi talikīḻāka niṟṟal, taliyil niṟṟal, vaḷintu halācaṉam ceytal pōṉṟa tiṟamikaḷ uḷḷa māṇavarkaḷākavum tērnteṭukkavum.

3. avacaram illāmal, nitāṉamāka, kavaṉattuṭaṉ māṇavarkaḷi ceyyac colla vēṇṭum.

eṇṇikkiyil kōpurap payiṟcikaḷ ceyyum muṟi

eṇṇikki 1 : payiṟci ceykiṟa māṇavarkaḷ, vara pōka vacatiyāka, mutalil variciyil niṉṟu koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

eṇṇikki 2 : vicilukkup piṟaku, aṭittaḷattil iruppavar, ataṟku mēl iruppavar. ataṟkum mēlē eṉṟu vantu, iruntu koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

eṇṇikki 3 : ellōrum vacatiyāka iruntapiṟaku, vicil oli kēṭṭatum, kōpura muḻu vaṭivik kāṭṭa vēṇṭum.

eṇṇikki 4 : vicilukkup piṟaku, kōpuram kaṭṭiya muṟipōlavē, iṟaṅki kalintu cella vēṇṭum.

kuṟippu : 1. vayatukkēṟṟavāṟu, iṉattiṟkēṟṟavāṟu (Sex) payiṟcikaḷit tērnteṭukkavēṇṭum.

2 mettipōṉṟa virippukaḷi, pātukāppu niliḷik kavaṉittu ceytu koḷḷavum.

2. viriviyakka jimṉāsṭik payiṟcikaḷ (Dynamic)

kaṟpikkum muṟikaḷ

1. tērnteṭukkum payiṟcikaḷ eḷitāka iruntu, piṉ kaṣṭamāṉatāka irukkumāṟu amiya vēṇṭum.



2.āciriyar col viḷakkattuṭaṉ, ceyal viḷakkattiyum muḻumiyāka, cariyāka ceytu kāṭṭa vēṇṭum.



3. māṇavarkaḷ payiṟciyi ceykiṟa pōtu, āciriyar arukiruntu utava vēṇṭum. 4. māṇavarkaḷ piṟari kalāṭṭā ceyyāmal, kēlipaṇṇik kuḻappāmal, vakuppi kaṭṭuppāṭṭuṭaṉ nirvakikka vēṇṭum.

5. māṇavarkaḷi ciṟukuḻukkaḷākap pirittu, ovvoru kuḻuviṟkum oru talivari niyamittu, payiṟci ceyyumāṟu paṇikka vēṇṭum. āṉāl kaṣṭamāṉa payiṟciyiṉ pōtu, āciriyar avaciyam arukila irukka vēṇṭum.

6. jimṉāsṭiks payiṟcikaḷ ceykiṟa cātaṉaṅkaḷ paḻutillāmal irukkiṉṟaṉavā eṉṟu pārttuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum

7. ellā māṇavarkkum vāyppu varuvatu pōl vakuppi nirvākittuk kaṟṟut taravēṇṭum.

8. enta nērattilum viḻutal, ki kāl piṭippukaḷ, pōṉṟavikaḷ naṭakkak kūṭum eṉpatāl, mutalutavip peṭṭiyum avaciyam.

9. muṭiyāta māṇavarkaḷi kaṭṭāyap paṭuttuvatō, valintu ceyya vaṟpuṟuttuvatō, tavaṟāṉa aṇuku muṟiyākum.

8. taṟkāppuk kalikaḷ

tiṭīreṉṟu allatu tiṭṭamiṭap paṭṭu, taṉ mēl nikaḻappōkiṉṟa āpattukkaḷi, taṉṉampikkiyāṉa āṟṟaluṭaṉ, taṭuttuk kāttuk koḷkiṉṟa tiṟamimiku ceyalkaḷiyē taṟkāppuk kalikaḷ eṉṟu aḻikkiṉṟaṉar. ﻿

ivi mukkiyamāka, 3 vakippaṭum. pala nūṟu vakikaḷ taṟkāppuk kalikaḷāka aḻikkappaṭṭālum, iṅkē nām 3 pirivukaḷ paṟṟiyē terintu koḷḷa irukkiṟōm.

1. kuttuccaṇṭi (Boxing)

2. malyuttam (wrestling)

3. kampuccaṇṭi (Lathi fight),



1. kuttuc caṇṭi

paḷḷi māṇavarkaḷukkuk kuttuc caṇṭiyik kaṟṟuttara parinturi ceyyappaṭavilli eṉṟālum, kuttuc caṇṭit tiṟaṉkaḷi, avarkaḷ kaṟṟuk koḷkiṟa pōtu, vāḻvil pala cūḻnilikaḷil veṟṟi pala peṟṟu vāḻvāṅku vāḻa utavum.

kuttuc caṇṭiyāṉtu vīram, vivēkam, viḻippuṇarvu, cuṟucuṟuppu, taṉṉampikki, nēram aṟintu nērttiyāka ceyalpaṭutal pōṉṟa kuṇaṅkaḷi vaḷarttu viṭukiṟatu.

kuttuc caṇṭikku, niṟiya neñcuram (Stamina) : uṭaluram, valimiyākak kuttum tiṟami mutaliyavi vēṇṭum.

kuttuc caṇṭikkāṉa aṭippaṭip payiṟcikaḷ. cāliyil oṭip paḻakutal; kayiṟu tāṇṭik kutittal: kaḻuttu, aṭivayiṟu, kikaḷ valimiyākkuvataṟkāṉa payiṟcikaḷ mutaliyaṉa vēṇṭum.

kaṟpikkum muṟi

1. mutalil niṟkum niliyi (Stance) kaṟṟuttara vēṇṭum.

2. niṟkum nili eṉpatu, uṭali camaniliyāka vittuk koḷvatu.

3. kālkaḷ muṉṉum piṉṉum, irupuṟamum pakka vāṭṭil virintu iyaṅkuvatu pōl kālkaḷi iyalpāka vittu niṉṟu: etiriyi oru kiyāl kuttuvataṟkāka muṉpuṟam, maṟṟoru kiyi etiriyiṉ tākkutalit taṭuttu koḷvatum pōṉṟa muṟiyil vittiruppatē, niṟkum niliyākum.

4. iyaṅkutal eṉpatu kālkaḷiṉ iyakkam (Footwork) kikaḷiṉ iyakkam (Arm movement) ākum. kicaḷum kālkaḷum cērntu tiṟamiyāka iṇintu ceyalpaṭumāṟu (Coordination) kaṟpikka vēṇṭum.

5. taṉittaṉiyāka ovvoruvarukkum tākkum muṟi kaḷiyum, taṭukkum tiṟaṉkaḷiyum (Blow and Block) kaṟpitta piṟaku, jōṭi jōṭiyāka māṇavarkaḷip pirittu, paḻakac ceyyutal

6. tākkum kali, taṭukkum kali iraṇṭilum tērcci peṟa nuṇukkaṅkaḷik kaṟpittal.

7. naṉku payiṟci peṟṟavarkaḷi, pōṭṭiyil paṅku peṟattakka nuṇukkattiyum, tiriyattiyum ūṭṭutal.

kuṟippu:

1.kikaḷil tuṇiyāl kaṭṭuppōṭṭu kiyuṟikaḷ māṭṭāmal; vāyil taṭuppup poruḷ (Mouth Piece) vikkāmal, caṇṭi ceyya aṉumatikkak kūṭātu.

2. mutalutavip peṭṭi vittiruppatu mikavum avaciyam.

2. malyuttam

kuttuc caṇṭiyip pōlavē malyuttamum ciṟanta taṟkāppuk kaliyākum. tiriyam, taṉṉampikki, tākkum āṟṟalkaḷ: cantarppattiṟkēṟṟavāṟu putticālittaṉamāka iyaṅkutal, tacikaḷiṉ valimi, uṟuppukkaḷiṉ uṉṉata valimi, ivaṟṟiyellām malyuttam vaḷarkkum mēṉmiyip peṟṟirukkiṟatu.

itaṟkāṉa uṭaṟpayiṟcikaḷ cila. taṇṭāl, paskikaḷ, mallar kampap payiṟcikaḷ, kuṉintu eḻuntu uṭkārutal (Push-up and Press-up), cāli ōṭṭam, kayiṟu tāṇṭik kutittal, oḻuṅkumuṟip payiṟcikaḷ ellām nalla payiṟcikaḷē.

tēvikaḷ

1. malyuttam ceyya mettikaḷi payaṉpaṭutta vēṇṭum.

2. viralkaḷil nakaṅkaḷ veṭṭappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum.

3. nakikaḷ, aṇiyak kūṭātu. eṇṇey allatu vaḻukkum tiravam etiyum mēṉiyil taṭaviyirukkak kūṭātu

4. mutalutavip peṭṭi mukkiyamāṉa tēviyākum

malyuttat tiṟaṉkaḷ

1. etiriyip piṭittu vīḻttak kūṭiya vakiyil niṟkum nili. atāvatu enta niliyilum camanili iḻantupōyviṭāta vaṇṇam, tariyil uṟutiyāka niṟkum nili.

2. etiriyiṉ kikaḷi, kālkaḷip piṭikkum muṟi (Holds)

3. etiriyiṉ mēl piṭi pōṭuvatupōla: avar pōṭukiṟa piṭikku etirppiṭi (Counter) pōṭutal.

4. etiriyiṉ piṭiyiliruntu viṭupaṭutal. (Escapes)

5. etiri timiṟi veḷivarātapaṭi, iṭukkip piṭi pōṭṭu, tariyil aḻuttutal. (Pinning)

kaṟpikkum muṟi

āciriyar māṇavarkaḷukku, ovvoru tiṟamiyāka mutalil viḷakki, ceyalmuṟiyil ceytukāṭṭa vēṇṭum.

intak kaliyil aṟimukamāṉa māṇavarkaḷi, ciṟuciṟu kuḻuvākap piritta pirivukku talivarkaḷāka vittuk kaṟṟuttarac ceyya vēṇṭum.

itaṟku pakuti kaṟpikkum muṟi payaṉpaṭum.

māṇavarkaḷkōpappaṭakkūṭiya cūḻnili ēṟpaṭuvati, āciriyar tavirkka vēṇṭum. taṭukka vēṇṭum.

malyuttap pōṭṭikkeṉṟu, cila muṉṉōṭip payiṟcikaḷ (Lead up Activity) uṇṭu.

a) kālāl pōṭum malyuttam (Leg Wrestling)

ā) kittaṭi koṇṭu malyuttam (Wand Wrestling)

i) kiyāl pōṭum malyuttam (Hand Wrestling)

iṉṉum, iraṭṭiyar pōṭakkūṭiya ciṟuciṟu pōṭṭikaḷum uṇṭu.

avaṟṟi māṇavarkaḷukkuk kaṟpittu, malyuttattil ārvattiyum tērcciyiyum atikappaṭutti, āciriyar tiṟamiyākak kaṟpittup payaṉ cērkkalām.

3. kampuc caṇṭi

kampuc caṇṭiyi cilampāṭṭam eṉṟum kūṟuvārkaḷ.

tiriyam, taṉṉampikki, viḻippuṇarcci, vīramuṭaṉ ceyalpaṭuttal pōṉṟa iyaṟkiyāṉa kuṇaṅkaḷi, merukēṟṟi vaḷarttuviṭukiṉṟa āṟṟal, cilampāṭṭattiṟku uṇṭu.

etirkāla vāḻkkikku kampuc caṇṭi payaṉpaṭum eṉpatāl, paḷḷi māṇavarkaḷukku, aṟimukappaṭuttuvatil tavaṟētum illi

vaḷikōl pantāṭṭattil payaṉpaṭum kōli, lāvakamāka iyakkuvatupōla, cilampiyum lāvakamāka cuḻaṟṟum taṉmi eḷitāka māṇavarkaḷukku ēṟpaṭum.

itaṟku kāl iyakkam; kampuṭaṉ uḷḷa ki cuḻaṟṟal; ataṟkāṉa niṟkum nili. ivaṟṟik kaṟṟut tarukiṟa āciriyar, tavaṟukaḷ ceykiṟa māṇavarkaḷit tirutti, ūkkuvittuk kaṟpikka vēṇṭum.

taṉit taṉiyāka ovvoru tiṟamiyiyum kaṟṟut tanta piṟaku, iruvar iruvarāka kampuc caṇṭi pōṭac ceyyalām.

tērnteṭukkum pōṭṭiyāḷarkaḷ iruvarum, cama aḷavil tiṟamiyuḷḷavarkaḷāka iruppavarkaḷāka iruntāl, pōṭṭiyum naṉku amiyum. āpattum atikam ēṟpaṭātu.

tākkavum, taṭukkavum kūṭiya niliyil kāyam ēṟpaṭalām. mutalutavip peṭṭi kivacam vittiruppatu nallatu.

9. oṭukaḷap pōṭṭi nikaḻccikaḷ

ovvoruvar uṭalilum niṟintu ūṭāṭik koṇṭirukkiṉṟa iyaṟkiyāṉa ceyalkaḷāṉa oṭutal, tāṇṭutal, eṟital pōṉṟavaṟṟi, vaḷarttu viṭavum, valimi koḷḷavum , mēṉmi peṟavum utavukiṉṟa muṟikaḷē, oṭukaḷap pōṭṭikaḷ eṉṟu peyar peṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa.

oṭukaḷap pōṭṭikaḷi mūṉṟu vakiyākap pirittirukkiṉṟārkaḷ. ovvoru pirivil uḷḷa nikaḻccikkum taṉit taṉiyēyāṉa tiṟaṉ nuṇukkaṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa.

āciriyarkaḷ nikaḻccikkēṟṟa tiṟaṉ nuṇukkaṅkaḷiyum, kaṟṟuk koḷḷum māṇavarkaḷukkēṟṟa aṟivu nili, āṟṟal nili, eḻucci nili, ēṟṟa nili mutaliyavaṟṟi aṟintukoṇṭē, ataṟkēṟpa kaṟpikkavēṇṭum.

māṇavarkaḷukkuk kaṟṟuk koḷḷak kūṭiya vāyppukkaḷi niṟiya nalki, avvappōtu ēṟpaṭum kuṟikaḷit tirutti, avarkaḷukkut taṉṉampikkiyi vaḷarttu, kaṟpittiṭa vēṇṭum.

cila kuṟippukkaḷ

1. uṭali naṉku vēkamākavum, tīviramākavum oru nikaḻcciyil īṭupaṭuttuvatāl, māṇavarkaḷukku patap paṭuttum payiṟcikaḷi koṭukkātu, enta nikaḻcciyiyum ceyyumāṟu kūṟak kūṭātu.

2. māṇavarkaḷukku āpattu ēṟpaṭā vaṇṇam, pātukāppākak kaṟpikka vēṇṭum (tāṇṭutal, eṟital tikaḻccikaḷ)

3. mutalutavip peṭṭiyit tayārāka vittirukkavum.

paḻutu paṭāta cātaṉaṅkaḷākap pārttu, payaṉpaṭuttiṭa vēṇṭum.

iṉimēl, ovvoru nikaḻccikkum uriya mukkiya tiṟaṉkaḷ (Skills) eṉṉeṉṉa eṉpati maṭṭum koṭuttuc celkiṟōm. āciriyarkaḷ mēlum viḷakkamāka avaṟṟip paṭittu terintu koṇṭu pōtikkumāṟum kēṭṭuk koḷkiṟōm.

1. ōṭṭattiṟkāṉa kikaḻccikaḷ

oṭṭa nikaḻccikaḷi mūṉṟu niliyākap pirikkalām.

1. virivōṭṭam (Sprint)

2. iṭi nili ōṭṭam (Middle distance Race)

3. neṭuntūra oṭṭam (Long distance Race) viri vōṭṭatti ōṭat toṭaṅkupavarkaḷ, mūṉṟu vakiyāka amarntiruntu ōṭat toṭaṅkuvar. anta ōṭṭat toṭakkattiṟkuriya peyarkaḷ

a) kuvi nilit toṭakkam (Bunch Start)

ā) iṭi nilit toṭakkam (Medium Start)

i) nīḷ nilit toṭakkam (Long Start)

inta mūṉṟu nilikaḷilum, evvāṟu amarvatu eṉpati āciriyar, māṇavarkaḷukku viḷakka vēṇṭum.

ōṭa utavum cātaṉattil (Starting Block) eppaṭi amarvatu eṉpatiyum payiṟciyaḷikka vēṇṭum.

ōṭa viṭupavar (Starter) eppaṭi ōṭa viṭuvār? avaratu kaṭṭaḷikaḷ eppaṭi eḻum eṉpatiyum viḷakka vēṇṭum.

uṅkaḷiṭattil uṭkāruṅkaḷ (on your marks)

tayārāka iruṅkaḷ (Set)

ōṭuṅkaḷ allatu tuppākki oli (Go or Gun)

eppaṭi ōṭa vēṇṭum eṉpatiyum āciriyar viḷakka vēṇṭum.

mūṉ pātaṅkaḷ maṭṭumē tariyil paṭa, kikaḷ iraṇṭiyum muṉṉum piṉṉum virivāka vīci ōṭa vēṇṭum.

ōṭṭat toṭakkattil irunta vēkatti viṭa, ōṭṭa elliyi neruṅka neruṅka, atika vēkattuṭaṉ pāyntōṭa vēṇṭum.

ellik kōṭṭik kuṟikkum nāṭāvi mutalil toṭa muyaṟcikka vēṇṭum eṉpatiyellām viḷakkik kūṟa vēṇṭum.

nīṇṭatūra ōṭṭattiṉ toṭakkattiṟku,

uṅkaḷiṭattil nalluṅkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟiyavuṭaṉ tuppākki oli kiḷampum. uṭaṉē ōṭat toṭaṅka vēṇṭum eṉpatiyum māṇavarkaḷukkuk kūṟa vēṇṭum.

iṉi tāṇṭum nikaḻccikaḷip pārppōm

tāṇṭukiṉṟa nikaḻcciyiṉ mukkiyamāṉa pakutikaḷi maṭṭum iṅkē kūṟiyirukkiṟōm. avaṟṟi evvāṟu ceyal paṭutta vēṇṭum eṉpati āciriyar viḷakkuvatu mukkiyam eṉpatiyum iṅkē kuṟippiṭukiṟōm,

kuṟippu mēlum viḷakkamāka terintu koḷḷa vēṇṭu meṉṟāl nāṉ eḻutiyirukkum "nīṅkaḷum olimpik vīrarākalām eṉṟa puttakattip paṭittu payaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷavum.

1. nīḷam tāṇṭal (Broad jump)

tāṇṭuvataṟku muṉ, tēviyāṉa patappaṭuttum paciṟcikaḷic ceyyac collavum.

tāṇṭuvataṟkuṟiya valimiyāṉa kāl (Strong leg) etu eṉpatik kaṇṭaṟiya, māṇavarkaḷi pala muṟi ōṭi vantu tāṇṭac ceytu, kaṇṭupiṭittiṭac ceyyavum.

metuvāka mutalil ōṭi vantu tāṇṭac ceyyavum, maṇaṟparappip pārttē tāṇṭāmal, mēlē pārttu tāṇṭac collavum.

uyaramāka 8 aṭikkuḷḷāka oru palūṉik kaṭṭi viṭṭu, ōṭi vantu tuḷḷi tāṇṭukiṟapōtu, taliyil palūṉi iṭippatu pōla uyaramākat tāvik kutikkac ceyyavum. (Heading the baloon)

tāṇṭi maṇaṟ parappil kutippataṟku muṉ, kāṟṟil naṭappatu pōla, kālkaḷi muṉpuṟamāka nīṭṭik kāluṉṟu māṟu paḻakkavum (Hitch Kick)

nāṉku pirivukaḷ itil uṇṭu.

1. utitteḻum palaki vari ōṭi varutal (Approach)

2. palakiyil utitteḻal (Take-off)

3. kāṟṟil naṭattal (Walking in the air)

4. maṇaṟparappil kālūṉṟal (Landing)

aṇuku muṟikku 16 tappaṭi kālaṭi muṟi (16 Steps) allatu 20 tappaṭi kālaṭi muṟiyik kaṟpikkavum,

2. mummuṟit tāṇṭa l (Triple Jump)

poruttamāṉa patappaṭuttum payiṟcikaḷic ceytu muṭitta piṟaku, niṉṟu koṇṭē mummuṟit tāṇṭum payiṟciyit taravum. (Standing Triple Jamp).

itaṟku nīḷattāṇṭal pōla ōṭi vantu, valiya kālāl tāṇṭi atē kālil tariyil ūṉṟi, (Hop) aṭutta kālil oru tappaṭi vittu, piṟaku, (Step maṇaṟ parappil iru kālkaḷālum kālūṉṟutal (Jump).

inta muṟiyi ōṭi vantu ceyyac collavum.



tāvi tappaṭi vittut tāṇṭal eṉpatu mūṉṟu muṟi tāṇṭuvatu pōla amivatāl, ati 3 : 2 : 3 eṉṟa vikitattil, ōṭivarum parappil kuṟittu vittut tāṇṭip paḻakkavum.

piṟaku maṇalil kālūṉṟum muṟiyik kaṟṟut taravum.

utitteḻum palakiyi nōkki varukiṟa cariyāṉa aṇuku muṟiyi, uriya vaḻiyil paḻakkavum. (Check Mark)

3. uyaram tāṇṭal (High Jump)

uyaram tāṇṭalil 4 vakikaḷ uṇṭu.

1. kattarikkōl tāṇṭu muṟi (Scissor Cut)

2. mēṟkattiya uruḷal tāṇṭu muṟi (Western Roll)

3. kuṟukkuk kampu mēl uruḷal muṟi (Belly Roll)

4. pāspari tāṇṭu muṟi (Fosbury Flop)

poruttamāṉa patappaṭuttum payiṟcikaḷi ceyya vēṇṭum.

kālkaḷi tariyil vēkamāka utittut tuḷḷit tāṇṭi maṇaṟparappil kutikkavum. intap payiṟciyi kuṟukkuk kampam (Crossbar) illāmalē paḻakavum.

inta muṟiyil, tariyil utittu eḻukiṉṟa kālālēyē (Take off leg) maṇaṟparappil ūṉṟa vēṇṭum. piṉṉāl varum aṭutta kāli (Rear leg), naṉku uyartti, uyaramākak koṇṭu varumāṟu paḻakavum.

kuṟukkuk kampam illāmal paḻakiyatu pōla, kuṟukkuk kampatti iṭatupuṟam uyartti, tāṇṭukiṟa pakkam tariyil iruppatu pōla vittu, tāṇṭac collavum.



iṭatu kālāl tāṇṭuvōrukku valatu puṟattil kuṟukkuk kampam tariyilum, valatu kālāl tāṇṭu vōrukku valatu puṟattil tariyil iruppatu pōl vittut tāṇṭac ceyyavum.

kampu mēl uruṇṭu tāṇṭum muṟiyi, 30 allatu 40 ṭikiri kōṇa aḷavil ōṭi vantu tāṇṭumāṟu kaṟpikkavum. 5 allatu 7 tappaṭikaḷil virivāka ōṭi vantu tāṇṭukiṟa uttiyi uṇarttavum. itaṟkuriya aṭiyāḷak kuṟippi (Check Mark) cariyāka amittuk koḷḷak kaṟpikkavum.

4. kōlūṉṟit tāṇṭal

poruttamāṉa patappaṭuttum payiṟcikaḷit taravum.

tāṇṭavarukiṟavar iṭatu kip paḻakkamuḷḷavarā allatu valatuki paḻakkam uḷḷavarā eṉpati mutalil kaṇṭaṟiyavum.

tāṇṭa utavukiṉṟa kōli, eppaṭip piṭippatu eṉpatik kaṟṟut taravum. valatukip paḻakkam uḷḷavarukku kippiṭippil iṭatukikku mēlāka valatuki iruppatu pōl piṭikkac ceyyavum, iṭatu kiyarukku valatukikku mēlāka iṭatuki irukka vēṇṭum.

kōluṭaṉ cīrāṉa vēkattuṭaṉ oṭi varukiṉṟa muṟiyik kaṟṟut taravum.

kōluṭaṉ ōṭi varukiṉṟavar. ōṭi varukiṉṟa ticiyi nōkki, tōḷ aḷavu cariyāṉa kōṇattil (Right Angle) amintiruppatu pōla, ēntik koṇṭu varavēṇṭum.





ēnti varum kōlāṉatu, muṉṉum piṉṉum pōyvarāmal, piṭittuk koḷvatu nallatu.

kōluṭaṉ ōṭi vantu, kōli muṉpuṟamuḷḷa kōlūṉṟum peṭṭiyil cariyāka ūṉṟukiṉṟa muṟikku, niṟiya paḻakkam tēvi.

tariyil iruntē kōli ūṉṟi, oru kālāl tariyi utiktut tuḷḷi, kōluṭaṉē uṭali oṭṭiyavāṟu uyarē tākkit tāṇṭa muyaṟcippatit tavaṟiṉṟic ceytiṭap paḻakkavum.

ippaṭip paḻaki, kuṟukkuk kampattik kaṭakkiṟapōtu mēlē celvataṟkum, talikīḻāka niṟpatu pōla, kampiṉ, piṭiyiliruntu, kālkaḷi mēlē uyartti, kampukku mēlāka uṭampi vaḷittuk kutippatum oru ariya kaliyākum.

kuṟinta uyarattil kuṟukkuk kampatti vittu, pala muṟi kōluṭaṉ ōṭi vantu, peṭṭiyil kōli ūṉṟi, oru kālāla tuḷḷit tāṇṭi, uyarntu ceṉṟu. ilāvakamāka kuṟukkuk kampattik kaṭakkum muṟiyi ovvoṉṟākak kaṟṟut taravum.

cariyāṉa aṭiyāḷak kuṟippi amittuttara āciriyar utava vēṇṭum.

eṟiyum nikaḻccikaḷ (Throws)

1. kuṇṭu eṟital (Shot put)

poruttamāṉa patappaṭuttum payiṟcikaḷ tarutal.

irumpuk kuṇṭiṉi eppaṭi kiyil eṭuppatu; eppaṭi piṭippatu (Hold) eṉpatik kaṟṟut tarutal.





irumpuk kuṇṭiṉi niṉṟu koṇṭu eṟivataṟkup payiṟci aḷittal.

eṭi kuṟinta irumpuk kuṇṭip payaṉpaṭutti, eṟiyap paḻakumāṟu vāyppukaḷik koṭuttal. itaṉāl, eṟiyum māṇavarkaḷukku, maṉattempiṉi vaḷarttiṭa muṭiyum.

piṟaku, kiyil vittirukkum kuṇṭiṉi, eppaṭi viralkaḷāl taḷḷiṭa vēṇṭum? (Finger Flip) eṉpati viḷakki, eṟiyumāṟu paḻakkutal.

niṉṟu koṇṭē, irumpuk kuṇṭi viralkaḷāl taḷḷi, uyarē, uyaramāka eṟintu paḻakutal.

ataṟkāka, atāvatu eṟiyataṟku muṉ niṟkum nili (Stance) paḻakutal. uṭal caktiyi muḻuvatum, irumpuk kuṇṭiṉi eṟiyum kikkuk koṇṭu varap payiṟciyaḷittal.

kuṇṭi eṟinta piṟaku, uṭal camaniliyi iḻakkāmal vaṭṭattiṟkuḷḷē niṉṟiṭap payiṟciyaḷittal.

ippaṭi ovvoru tiṟaṉiyum kaṟṟut tantu, piṟaku mottamāka aṉittut tiṟaṉiyum ceyya vittal.

2. taṭṭeṟital (Discus Throw)

patappaṭuttum payiṟcikaḷi aḷittal.

taṭṭiṉi, evvāṟu viralkaḷāl paṟṟip piṭippatu eṉṟa muṟiyi viḷakkutal.

mutaṟkaṭṭamāka, eṟitaṭṭiṉi, viral caktiyip payaṉ paṭutti, tari mītu ōṭuvatupōla, uruṭṭi viṭutal. ataṟkup payaṉpaṭum cuṭṭuviralip payaṉpaṭuttum nuṇukkatti viḷakkutal.

aṭuttu, tarikkum mēlē antarattil (Air), taṭṭu cuḻaṉṟu pōvatu pōla, cuṭṭu viralāl cuḻaṟṟi eṟiyac ceytal.

piṟaku, niṉṟu koṇṭē taṭṭeṟiyac ceytal.

ataṟkup piṟaku, vaṭṭattiṟkuḷḷēyē cuṟṟukiṟa ārampac cuḻaṟcikaḷi (Priliminary Swings), taṭṭu illāmal cuṟṟiṭak kaṟṟut tarutal.

ippaṭi cuḻalak kaṟṟut tanta piṟaku, taṭṭuṭaṉ cuṟṟi, taṭṭi vīci eṟiyak kaṟṟut tarutal.

taṭṭāṉatu, kaṭikāram cuṟṟuvatu pōṉṟa ticiyil, (Clock wise) cuḻalum muṟiyil cuḻaṉṟu pōvatu pōla eṟiya vittal.

ippaṭi ellā aṭippaṭit tiṟaṉkaḷiyum māṇavar kaḷ kaṟṟuk koṇṭa piṟaku, mottamāka aṉittut tiṟaṉkaḷiyum cērttu, taṭṭeṟiyac ceytal.

3 vēleṟital (Javelin throw)

tēviyāṉa patappaṭuttum payiṟcikaḷit tarutal.

vēliṉi, eppaṭip piṭippatu eṉkiṟa piṭimuṟiyi (Grip) mutalil kaṟṟut tarutal.

tōḷukku mēlē, vēliṉi evvāṟu piṭittuk koṇṭiruppatu eṉpatiyum collit tarutal.

vēliṉip piṭittirukkum ki etu eṉpati tīrmāṉitta piṟaku, vēl illāmal, veṟum ki koṇṭu. uṭalit tiruppi (Turn) eppaṭi eṟivatu eṉpatik kaṟṟut tarutal

piṟaku, vēluṭaṉ niṉṟu koṇṭu eṟital.

ōṭivantu, vēleṟikiṟa vitattik kaṟṟut tarutal.

eṟikiṟa nērattil. kuṟukkuttappaṭi (Cross step) vikkum muṟiyi nuṇukkamākak kaṟṟut tarutal.

ataṟkāṉa, aṭiyāḷak kuṟikaḷiyum (Check marks) kuṟittu, niṟiya muṟi, ōṭivantu eṟiyumāṟu ceytal.

caṅkilik kuṇṭu vīcutal (Hammer throw)

poruttamāṉa uṭaṟpayiṟcikaḷit tarutal.

caṅkilik kuṇṭiṉi eppaṭip piṭippatu, evvāṟu niṟpatu eṉpati collit tarutal.

caṅkiliyip piṭittapaṭi, eppaṭi cuḻaṟṟuvatu eṉkiṟa ārampac cuṟṟu muṟiyik kaṟṟut tarutal.

ārampac cuḻaṟcikkāka, eṭi kuṟinta kuṇṭukaḷip payaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷalām.

taṭṭu eṟivatu pōla, itaṟkum kāl tappaṭiyum cuḻalum muṟiyum (Foot work) uṇṭu. ataṉi, ovvoru kālaṭiyākak kaṟṟuttaravum.

mutalil oru cuṟṟu (One turn) piṟaku eṟital.



piṟaku iraṇṭu cuṟṟuc cuṟṟi eṟital.

piṟaku, mūṉṟu cuṟṟukkaḷic cuṟṟi eṟital.

ippaṭiyāka, muḻumiyāka eṟiyap payiṟciyaḷittal.

ituvari, ōṭukaḷap pōṭṭikaḷik kaṟṟut tarukiṟa muṟikaḷiyellām kaṇṭaṟintu koṇṭōm.

10 . nīccal pōṭṭi nikaḻccikaḷ

nīccal eṉpatu niṟinta payaṉkaḷi nalkukiṉṟa nallatoru uṭaṟpayiṟciyākum. uṭal uṟuppukkaḷ aṉittiyum iyakkukiṉṟa aḷavil amintiruppatuṭaṉ, ciṟanta poḻutu pōkku amcamākavum, makiḻccitarukiṉṟa campavamākavum nīccal irukkiṟatu.

māṇavarkaḷukku eḷitāka nīntum kaliyik kaṟṟut taralām eṉṟālum, apāyam niṟintuḷḷa itaṉi, caṟṟu eccarikkiyuṭaṉ ceyalpaṭṭāl, ciramamiṉṟi cantōṣamākkik koḷḷa muṭiyum

mukkiyamāṉa pātukāppuk kuṟippukaḷ.

1. āḻamillāta iṭattil taṇṇīr kuṟintuḷḷa iṭattil, nīccal payiṟciyit toṭaṅka vēṇṭum.

2. tūymiyāṉa taṇṇīr uḷḷa kuḷattil, nīccal kaṟṟut taruvatu nallatu.

3. mitakkiṉṟa rappar vaḷiyaṅkaḷ, kayiṟukaḷ, kiṭippatāka iruntāl, payiṟcikkup payaṉ paṭuttik koḷḷalām.

4. mutalutavi cātaṉap peṭṭi kūṭavē iruppatu nallatu.

5. tōl viyāti maṟṟum, nōyāḷikaḷ nīccal kuḷattil iṟaṅkāmal, pārttuk koḷḷavum.

6. māṇavarkaḷ taṇṇīril iṟaṅka, kuḷikka, kutittu viḷiyāṭa ārvamākat tāṉ iruppārkaḷ. avarkaḷiṉ ācikkāka, appaṭiyē viṭṭu viṭuvatu, āpattāṉa viṣayamākum.

ākavē, koñcaṅ koñcamāka, avarkaḷukku intak kaliyik kaṟṟut tara vēṇṭum.

1. taṇṇīril niṉṟu koṇṭu, kaḻuttaḷavu iruntu taṇṇīrip payamiṉṟi cantikkum tuṇivi vaḷarttal.

2. taṇṇīril mitakkum kaliyik kaṟṟut tarutal. (Water Balance)

3. taṇṇīrukku mēlē mitantu, muṉṉēṟac ceytal.

4. taṇṇīrukkuḷ mūccaṭakkik koḷkiṟa payiṟciyi aḷittal.

5. piṟaku, taṇṇīril ūrntu celkiṟa nīccal payiṟciyi (Crawl stroke) kaṟṟut tarutal.

6. kālkaḷi piṉpuṟamāka vēkamāka utittu muṉṉēṟum valimiyi vaḷarttu viṭutal. (Leg kick)



7. taṇṇīrik kikaḷāl kiḻittu, otukki muṉṉēṟum vallamiyik kaṟṟut tarutal. (Arm movement)

piṟaku, ovvoru nīccal muṟiyiyum, toṭarntu kaṟṟut taralām.

āṉāl, nīccal payiṟciyil muḻu nēramum, āciriyar arukē irukka vēṇṭum. avaratu kaṇkāṇippum utaviyum atikamāka irukka vēṇṭum.

āciriyar inta viṣayattil eccarikkiyākavum, kavaṉamākavum iruppatu mika mika avaciyamākum.





﻿11. toṭar pōṭṭip pantayaṅkaḷ

(TOURNAMENTS)



* * *





viḷiyāṭṭu eṉpatu, taṉakkuriya tiṟamiyi terintu koṇṭu, makiḻcci peṟuvataṟkāka ēṟpaṭṭatākum.

viḷiyāṭṭup pōṭṭi eṉpatu, taṉatu tiṟamiyuṭaṉ maṟṟavarkaḷ tiṟamiyi oppiṭṭup pārttuk koḷḷa ēṟpaṭṭatākum.

taṉatu tiṟami maṟṟavarkaḷ tiṟamiyuṭaṉ caricamamāka irukkiṟatā, allatu carintupōy kiṭakkiṟatā eṉpatip purintu koṇṭu, mēlum tiṟamikaḷil tērcci koḷḷa, eḻucci peṟa, uṇarcci koḷḷa, pōṭṭikaḷ utavukiṉṟaṉa.

appaṭippaṭṭa aṟivārnta niliyil, uyarnta taṉmiyil amintuḷḷa pōṭṭip pantayaṅkaḷi, eppaṭi, naṭatta vēṇṭum eṉpataṟku oru niyatiyum nērmiyum irukka vēṇṭumallavā!

ellārkkum camavāyppu, camaantastu eṉṟa jaṉanāyakap paṇiyilē uruvākkappaṭṭirukkum, pōṭṭit toṭar pantayaṅkaḷ paṟṟi, iṅkē virivākak kāṇpōm.



toṭar pōṭṭip pantayaṅkaḷiṉ aṭippaṭi nōkkam veṟṟi tōlviyi viḷivittuk koṭuppatu tāṉ eṉṟālum, vaḻimuṟikaḷum, vāyppu nilikaḷum koñcam māṟiyē tāṉ amintirukkiṉṟaṉa.

ellā cālikaḷum rōm nakari nōkkiyē celkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu oru mēṉāṭṭup paḻamoḻi.

ellā toṭar pōṭṭip pantayaṅkaḷum, āṭṭakkārarkaḷiṉ tiṟamiyi matittu; āṭum yuktiyi vaḷarttu. pōṭṭi naṭattupavar, paṅku peṟupavar. naṭuvarākap paṇi yāṟṟukiṟavar, pārviyāḷarkaḷ eṉṟu ellōrukkumē ellām taruvatāka amintiruppatāl tāṉ, palvēṟu vitamākap pirintu, pāṅkāṉa nōkkuṭaṉ, paṇpāṉa, amippuṭaṉ paṇipurintu, pārāṭṭukkaḷip peṟṟuk koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.

iṉi, pantayaṅkaḷiṉ pala vaki pirivukaḷ paṟṟi aṟintu koḷvōm.



I oru vāyppu muṟi allatu nīkkumuṟip pantayaṅkaḷ

(Knock out or Elimination Tournaments)

itilē irukkiṉṟa vevvēṟu pirivukaḷ.

a) orē oru vāyppu muṟi (Single elimination)



ā) āṟutal vāyppu muṟi (Consolation Type)



i) iraṭṭi vāyppu muṟi (Double Knock out)



ī) pākṉāl oyilṭu vāyppu muṟi (Bagnall wild elimination)





II toṭar vāyppup pantayaṅkaḷ allatu cuḻal muṟi pantayaṅkaḷ (League or Robinhood Tournaments)

a) oru vāypput toṭar (Single league)

ā) iraṭṭi vāypput toṭar (Double league)

III oruṅkiṇinta toṭar pōṭṭip pantayaṅkaḷ (Combination Tournaments)

a) oru vāyppu muṟiyuṭaṉ oru vāyppu muṟi (Knock out Cum Knock out)

ā) oru vāyppu muṟiyuṭaṉ toṭar vāyppu muṟi (Knock out Cum League)

i) (pala) toṭar vāyppu muṟiyuṭaṉ toṭar vāyppu muṟi (League Cum League)

ī) toṭar vāyppu muṟiyuṭaṉ oru vāyppu muṟi (League Cum Knock out)

IV cavāl pōṭṭip pantayaṅkaḷ (Challenge Tournaments)

a) ēṇi muṟi (Ladder)

ā) kōpura aṭukku muṟi (Pyramid)

ittaṉi pirivukaḷ etaṟkeṉṟa aiyam, uṅkaḷukku ēṟpaṭuvatu iyaṟkiyē.

oru pōṭṭit toṭari naṭattuvatu eṉṟāl, kīḻk kāṇum nikaḻccikaḷi aṉucarittut tāṉ ēṟpāṭu ceytiṭa vēṇṭum.

1. pōṭṭiyi naṭattukiṉṟa kālam (Season)

2. pōṭṭiyi naṭattakkūṭiya nēram (Time)

3. irukkiṉṟa āṭukaḷa vacati, utavi cātaṉaṅkaḷ (Play ground & Equipment)

4. pōṭṭi naṭattukiṟa viḷiyāṭṭiṉ taṉmi (Type of game)

5. āṭṭa atikārikaḷ eṇṇikkiyiṉ aḷavu (Officials)

6. pōtiya poruḷātārattip poṟuttu (Finance)

iṉi, ovvoru vaki toṭarpōṭṭip pantayatti naṭatta, pōṭṭi niral (Fixtures) tayārikkum muṟiyiṉiyum, ataṟkāṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭu mūlam aṟintu koḷvōm.



1. oru vāyppup pōṭṭi muṟi (Single Knock out or Single Elimination)

iti nīkkumuṟi pōṭṭi eṉṟum kūṟuvārkaḷ.

inta oru vāyppup pōṭṭit toṭaril, paṅku peṟukiṉṟa kuḻukkaḷ, oru muṟi tōṟṟup pōṉālē, pōṭṭiyiliruntu iṭamiḻantupōkiṉṟaṉa. mīṇṭum āṭa vāyppē ataṟku kiṭiyātu. kiṭikkātu.

intap pōṭṭiyil paṅku peṟukiṉṟa kuḻukkaḷiṉ eṇṇikkikku ēṟpa, pōṭṭi āṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ eṇṇikkiyum amiyum.

utāraṇamāka, 12 kuḻukkaḷ pōṭṭiyil paṅku peṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟāl, naṭakkiṉṟa pōṭṭi āṭṭaṅkaḷ 11 eṉṟu amiyum.

itaṟkāṉa cūttiram n-1 eṉpatu.

N eṉṟāl Number of Teams eṉpatu arttam.



atil oṉṟik kaḻittāl, ettaṉi āṭṭam eṉṟu teriyum.

ākavē 12 kuḻukkaḷ eṉṟāl, 12-1 = 11 eṉṟu terintu koḷḷalām.

pōṭṭi niral tayārikkum muṟi (Drawing Fixtures)

pōṭṭiyil kalantu koḷkiṟa kuḻukkaḷiṉ eṇṇikki- iraṭṭippaṭiyil, 2ṉ perukkattil iruntāl, ataṟkāṉa pōṭṭi niral tayārikkum muṟi vēṟu. atāvatu 2, 4, 6, 8, 16, 32, 64, 128, eṉṟa kaṇakkāka irukkum pōtum iraṭṭip perukkattiṉ aṭippaṭiyil amintirukkāmal, 5, 7, 9, 10, 11, 12, 13, 14, 15, 17liruntu 31, 33liruntu 63 eṉpatāka iruntāl, pōṭṭi niraliṉ tayāripput taṉmi vēṟāka irukkum.

iraṭṭip perukka eṇṇikkiyil uḷḷa kuḻukkaḷiṉ eṇṇikki 8 eṉṟu koḷvōm. ataṟkāṉa pōṭṭi nirali eppaṭi tayārippatu eṉṟu pārppōm.

1. cariyāṉa aḷavuḷḷa kākitattuṇṭukaḷ 8 eṭuttu, avaṟṟil 1 mutal 8 eṇkaḷi taṉittaṉiyē eḻuti, curuṭṭi vittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

2. piṟaku ovvoru kākitac curuḷiyum kulukki eṭuttu, atiluḷḷa eṇṇip paṭittu, ataṉpaṭi variciyāka eḻuta vēṇṭum. atāvatu, mēl taṭṭil uḷḷa (Top), aṭittaṭṭil uḷḷa eṇkaḷukkēṟṟa kuḻukkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷi eḻuta vēṇṭum.

3. mutal cuṟṟu āṭṭattiṟkāṉa kuḻukkaḷi aṭippukkuṟi mūlam iṇittu, āṭakkūṭiya tēti, nēram, iṭam mutaliyavaṟṟik kuṟikka vēṇṭum.

4. iraṇṭām cuṟṟu mutal iṟutic cuṟṟu vari, toṭarntu aṭippuk kuṟiyil kāṭṭiṭa vēṇṭum.

8 kuḻukkaḷ eṉkiṟapōtu, mēl taṭṭil 4m, kīḻt taṭṭil 4m, eṉṟum amiyum.

iṉi, pōṭṭi niral paṭṭiyalip pārppōm.



1. 8 aṇikaḷukkāṉa pōṭṭi niral





2. 11 aṇikaḷukkāṉa pōṭṭi niral

pōṭṭiyiṭukiṉṟa kuḻukkaḷiṉ eṇṇikki 2ṉ perukkattil illāmal iruntāl (8, 16, 32); ataṟ kēṟpa pōṭṭi niralit tayārikka vēṇṭum eṉpati nām aṟivōm.

ataṟku utavuvatutāṉ ciṟappiṭam tarukiṉṟa vilakku muṟi (Bye)

ettaṉi kuḻukkaḷ eṉṟu aṟintu koṇṭa piṉ, anta eṇṇikkiyi nām uṟuti ceytu koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

11 kuḻukkaḷ vantiruntāl 5m,

12 kuḻukkaḷ vantiruntāl 4m,

13 kuḻukkaḷ vantiruntāl 3m,

20 kuḻukkaḷ vantiruntāl 12m eṉṟa eṇṇikkiyil ciṟappu vilakku (Bye) koṭukka vēṇṭum.

itu eppaṭi varukiṟatu? ēṉ koṭukka vēṇṭum? eṉṟu kēḷvikaḷ eḻuvatu uṇmi tāṉ.

nām iraṇṭiṉ perukkam eṉṟu mutalil kūṟiṉōm allavā! atu 2,4, 8, 16, 32,64 eṉṟu varukiṟatu allavā!

6 kuḻukkaḷ eṉṟāl 8kku uṭpaṭṭatallavā! appoḻutu ciṟappu vilakku peṟuvatu 2 kuḻukkaḷ. 8-6 = 2 11 kuḻukkaḷ eṉṟāl 16kku uṭpaṭṭatallavā! appoḻutu ciṟappu vilakku peṟuvatu 5 kuḻukkaḷ. 16–11 = 5

20 kuḻukkaḷ eṉṟāl 32kku uṭpaṭṭatallavā! appoḻutu ciṟappu vilakku peṟuvatu 12. 32-20 = 12

ippaṭiyāka, nām kaḻittu, muṭivu ceytuviṭa vēṇṭum.

ciṟappu vilakku peṟukiṉṟa kuḻukkaḷ, mutal cuṟṟukku maṭṭumē viḷiyāṭāta vāyppip peṟukiṉṟaṉa.

oru pōṭṭi āṭṭatti āṭāmal iruppataṉālē, antak kuḻukkaḷukkup peritākap payaṉ ētum viḷintuviṭap pōvatilli.

oru cuṟṟu āṭṭam āṭiya piṟaku, ōyvu kiṭittālum nallatu. cuṟṟukku muṉṉē ōyvu kiṭippatāl eṉṉa lāpam?

eṉavē, mutal cuṟṟu āṭṭattiṟku maṭṭum ciṟappu vilakkut tarukiṉṟa kuṟippi maṭṭum nīṅkaḷpurintukoḷḷa vēṇṭum.

ciṟappu vilakku aḷikkiṉṟa muṟi

1. 5 kuḻukkaḷ ciṟappu vilakku peṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟāl, anta 5 kuḻukkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷiyum, taṉittaṉiyē oru tuṇṭuk kākitattil eḻuti, curuṭṭi vittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

3. patiṉōru kuḻukkaḷ paṅku peṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟāl, anta 11 kuḻukkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷiyum variciyāka eṇkaḷ tantu, paṭṭiyalil nirappa vēṇṭum.



3. piṟaku, 5 tuṇṭuc curuḷkaḷiliruntu, oṉṟu oṉṟāka, oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟāka eṭuttup pārttu, mutalil varukiṉṟa kuḻuviṉ peyari, kīḻt taṭṭu paṭṭiyalil uḷḷa 11vatu iṭattil eḻuta vēṇṭum. mutal vilakku, kīḻppaṭṭiyalil kaṭici. 2m vilakku mēl paṭṭiyalil mutal iṭam 1, 3vatu vilakku kīḻppaṭṭiyalil 10. nāṉkāvatu vilakku mēlpaṭṭiyalil 2. 5vatu vilakku kīḻil uḷḷa 9vatu iṭam. inta muṟiyi aṭippuk kuṟippil kāṇpittirukkiṟōm. aṟika.



11 kuḻukkaḷukkāṉa pōṭṭi niral



cuṟṟu 1﻿ cuṟṟu 2﻿ cuṟṟu 3﻿ cuṟṟu 4





pōṭṭi niralil kuḻukkaḷi tērnteṭukkum pōtu, cīṭṭuk kulukkip pōṭukiṟa muṟiyip piṉpaṟṟavum. itil mēlpāti, kīḻppāti eṉṟu nām aṭikkaṭi kuṟippiṭukiṟōm. iti eppaṭik kaṇṭupiṭippatu?

1. kuḻukkaḷiṉ eṇṇikki iraṭṭippaṭi eṇṇikkiyil iruntāl eḷitu. ataṟkāṉa cūttiram . 10 aṇi eṉṟāl, mēlpāṭu yil 5m, kīḻppātiyil 5m varum.



2. kuḻukḷiṉ eṇṇikki oṟṟippaṭiyil iruntāl, ataṟkāṉa cūttiram (n + 1)÷2.



mēlpātikkāṉatu itu.



um : 11 kuḻukkaḷukku : 11 + 1 = 12. = 6.



āka mēlpāti 6 kuḻukkaḷik koṇṭatāka irukkum kīḻppātikku cūttiram : (n-1)÷2,



um : 11 kuḻukkaḷukku : 11-1 ÷ 10. = 5.





āka, mēlpāti 6. kīḻppāti 5 eṉṟu koḷḷa vēṇṭum.



ciṟappu vilakku (Bye) koṭukkiṟapōtu, kaṭippiṭikka vēṇṭiya muṟi kīḻē tarappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.

vilakkukku iraṭṭippaṭi eṇṇāka aṇikaḷ iruntāl

mēlpātikkut taravēṇṭiya cūttiram nb÷2.



um : 6 ciṟappu vilakku eṉṟāl : = 3.



kīḻppātikkum atuvē cūttiram nb÷2.



um : 6 ciṟappu vilakku eṉṟāl : = 3.



vilakkukku oṟṟippaṭi eṇṇāka iruntāl

mēlpātiyi aṟiya : (mb-1)÷2.



um : 5 vilakkuk kuḻukkaḷ : 5-1 = 4 ; = 2.



kīḻppātikkuriyatu : (mb + 1)-2,



um : 5 vilakkuk kuḻukkaḷ : 5 + 1 = 6 ; = 3.



ākavē, 11 kuḻukkaḷ paṅkupeṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉkiṟapōtu, 6 ciṟappu vilakku eṇṇikki varukiṟatu.

atil kīḻppātiyil 3m, mēlpātiyil 2m eṉkiṟa vikitattil, iṭampeṟṟu iruppatik kaṇṭu teḷika.

cila utāraṇaṅkaḷit tarukiṟōm

13 kuḻukkaḷ mottam eṉṟāl : 3 ciṟappu vilakku. ḻēppātiyil 2; mēlpātiyil 1.

19 kuḻukkaḷ mottam eṉṟāl 13 ciṟappu vilakku. kīḻppātiyil 7. mēlpātiyil 6.

27 kuḻukkaḷ mottam eṉṟāl 5 ciṟappu vilakku. iḻppātiyil 3. mēlpātiyil 2.

oru vāypput toṭar pōṭṭi pantayaṅkaḷil uḷḷa, kiṟikaḷum kuṟikaḷum.

niṟikaḷ

1. pantayaṅkaḷik kuṟitta nērattil, kuṟukiya kālattiṟkuḷ virivāka naṭatti muṭikka muṭiyum.

2.ellām tiṭṭamiṭṭapaṭi naṭappatāl, atika poruṭcelavu varātu.

3.pantayattil oru vāyppu tāṉ uḷḷatu eṉpatāl, pōṭṭiyil atika ārvattōṭum, tīvira muyaṟciyōṭum kuḻukkaḷ paṅkupeṟum, tōṟkum kuḻu iṭam iḻantu pōkiṟatallavā!

kuṟikaḷ

1. pōṭṭi oṉṟil etirpārāta vitamāka tōṟṟup pōkiṟa kuḻu, mīṇṭum taṉatu tiṟamiyi veḷik kāṭṭa iyalāmal pōkiṟatu.

2. maṟṟa kuḻukkaḷ veṟṟi peṟṟu, ataṉuṭaṉ pōṭṭiyiṭuvataṟkāka oru kuḻu, kāttirukka vēṇṭiyirukkiṟa kāla tāmatam nērkiṟatu.

3. cīṭṭuk kulukkip pōṭṭu, pōṭṭi niral tayārikkappaṭuvatāl, nalla tiṟamiyuḷḷa aṇikaḷ oru pātiyil vantu, cantikkiṟapōtu, nalla kuḻu iṭamiḻantu pōka nēriṭukiṟatu.

cila camayaṅkaḷil tiṟaṉaṟṟa kuḻuvum tiṟamiyāṉa kuḻuvum iṟutip pōṭṭiyil cantikka nēriṭuvatum uṇṭu; anta niliyi inta oru vāypput toṭar aṭikkaṭi uṇṭākki viṭukiṟatu.

intak kuṟiyi nīkkattāṉ ciṟappiṭam tarum muṟi (Seeding Method) piṉpaṟṟappaṭukiṟatu.

oru pōṭṭikku 12 aṇikaḷ pōṭṭikku vantiruk kiṉṟaṉa. ciṟappu vilakku 4 aṇikaḷ uṇṭu eṉṟu nām aṟivōm.

anta iṭattil varuvataṟku, cīṭṭuk kulukkippōṭukiṟa muṟiyil, muṉpu nām tērnteṭuttōm.

iṅku, ciṟappiṭam tarum muṟiyil, cīṭṭuk kulukkal mūlam aḷikkāmal, pōṭṭiyi naṭattupavarkaḷē aṇikaḷit tērnteṭukka vēṇṭum.

eppaṭi tērnteṭuppatu?

ataṟku 2 vaḻikaḷ uḷḷaṉa.

1. kaṭanta kālattil, ciṟappāka āṭiya aṇikaḷiṉ cātaṉikaḷik koṇṭum;

2. appaṭi teriyāviṭṭāl, nēril anta aṇikaḷ paṟṟi kēṭṭuk koṇṭum, tīrmāṉikkalām.

ivvāṟu tērnteṭukkappaṭṭa kuḻukkaḷi maṭṭum (iṅkē 4 eṉṟu vittuk koḷvōm) cīṭṭuk kulukkaliṉ mūlamāka allatu parinturi mūlamāka, ciṟappiṭam koṭuttiṭalām.

itil kaṭipiṭikka vēṇṭiya mukkiya amcam eṉṉaveṉṟāl, paṅkupeṟukiṟa kuḻukkaḷ 4m, varukiṟa tūram, peṟṟirukkiṟa tiṟami, taram mutaliyavaṟṟiyum kuṟippil koḷḷalām.

ceṉṉiyil pōṭṭi naṭakkiṟatu. nākar kōvil, kōvi, tirucci, maturi eṉum iṭaṅkaḷiliruntu kuḻukkaḷ varukiṉṟaṉa. anta aṇikaḷiṉ taramum tiṟamum naṉṟāka irukkiṟatu eṉpatāl, anta nāṉku aṇikkum ciṟappiṭam (Seeding) tarukiṉṟōm.

72 kuḻukkaḷukku ciṟappiṭam tantiṭum oru vāypput toṭar pōṭṭi niral





kuṟippu itu pōl kuḻukkaḷukku ciṟappiṭam tarukiṟa pōtu, ari iṟuti āṭṭattil, tiṟamiyuḷḷa kuḻukkaḷ pōṭṭi yiṭum vāyppu kiṭikkiṟatu. ēṉeṉil, nalla kuḻukkaḷ 4m, mēṟpātiyil 2; kīḻppātiyil 2 eṉṟu pirikkappaṭṭu viṭukiṉṟaṉa.

2. ciṟappiṭam tarukiṟa kuḻuvi, parinturi mūlam pōṭalām. avaṟṟiṉ tiṟamikaḷ cariyāka. irukkum paṭcattil, cīṭṭuk kulukkip pōṭṭut tērnteṭuppataṉ mūlam, cikkali cariceytu viṭalām.

ivvāṟu ciṟappiṭam tantu, vilakkaḷikkum muṟiyi (Seeding - byes) eppaṭi ceyvatu eṉpati, iṉi nām kāṇpōm.

mottam 25 kuḻukkaḷ paṅku peṟukiṉṟaṉa.

avaṟṟil 8 aṇikaḷukku nām ciṟappiṭam taravēṇṭum.

8 aṇikaḷum orē pātiyil vantu viṭakkūṭātu.

mēl pāti, kīḻppāti eṉa pirittut tantālum, 4, 4 eṉṟu varukiṟa pōtu, oru pakutiyilēyum vantu viṭak kūṭātu.

ākavē, anta eṭṭu aṇikaḷum, kāl iṟutip pōṭṭikaḷil kalantu koḷvatu pōlac ceytāka vēṇṭum.

muḻu pōṭṭi niraliyum nām ituvari iraṇṭākap pirittu, mēlpāti, kīḻppāti eṉṟu peyar tantirukkiṟōm.



ippoḻutu mēlpātiyi iraṇṭākavum, kīḻp. pātiyi iraṇṭākavum pirittāka vēṇṭum. anta arippāti iraṇṭākap pirikiṟa pōtu, kāl pakuti (Quarter) eṉṟa peyarip peṟukiṟatu.

eṉavē, iṅku nāṉku kālpakutikaḷ ēṟpaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟi nām 1, 2, 3, 4 eṉṟu peyariṭuvōm.

ovvoru kālpakutiyilum 2 aṇiyāka 4 kālpakutiyilum 8 aṇikaḷ iṭam peṟukiṉṟaṉa.

ivvāṟu iṭam peṟukiṟa ciṟappiṭam peṟṟa aṇikaḷ, kāl iṟutip pōṭṭiyil tāṉ, vantu pōṭṭiyiṭum.

appaṭiyeṉṟāl, ariyiṟutip pōṭṭiyil 4 aṇikaḷ, iṟutip pōṭṭiyil 2 aṇikaḷ eṉṟu iṭam peṟum.

itaṟkiṭiyil ciṟappiṭaṅkaḷ peṟukiṟa kuḻukkaḷ, mutal cuṟṟilēyē tōṟṟup pōṉālum pōkalām. atu vēṟu viṣayam.

nām ciṟappiṭam taruvatu eṉpatu tāṉ, iṅkē mukkiyamāṉatākum.

anta ciṟappiṭam tarukiṟa muṟiyi, kīḻkkāṇum muṟippaṭi taralām.

25 kuḻukkaḷukkuriya pōṭṭi niral





mukkiyak kuṟippukaḷ

1. 8 aṇikaḷ ciṟappiṭam peṟukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷ taṉittaṉi tuṇṭu cīṭṭil eḻutappaṭṭu, cīṭṭuk kulukkal mūlam eṭukkappaṭa vēṇṭum.



mēlē uḷḷa pōṭṭi niralip pāruṅkaḷ.

atil ciṟappiṭam 1, ciṟappiṭam eṉṟu kuṟittirukkiṟōm.

atāvatu, cīṭṭuk kulukkalil mutalāvatāka varukiṉṟa aṇi kīḻpātiyil uḷḷa 4vatu kālpakutiyil kaṭiciyilum: 2vatāka varukiṟa aṇi, mēlpātiyil uḷḷa 2vatu kāl pakutiyilum, ippaṭiyāka iṭampeṟa vēṇṭum.

kulukkalil mutal cīṭṭu-kīḻpātiyil-4vatu pakuti.



2vatāka varukiṟa cīṭṭu-mēlpāti-2vatu pakutiyil.



3vatāka varukiṟa cīṭṭu-kīḻpāti-3vatu pakuti.



4vatāka varukiṟa cīṭṭu-mēlpāti-1vatu pakuti.



mītiyi paṭam pārttup purintu koḷka.



25 kuḻukkaḷ eṉṟāl, 7 ciṟappiṭam tāṉē uṇṭu. namakku. 8 aṇikaḷukku iṭam tara vēṇṭumē!

ataṟkāka, 8vatāka cīṭṭil varum aṇikku, mēl pātiyil uḷḷa 1vatu iṭatti aḷittiṭa vēṇṭum.

2. piṟaku, mītiyuḷḷa 17 iṭaṅkaḷukkāka, cīṭṭuk kulukkal pōṭṭu, avaṟṟi variciyākak kuṟittuk koṇṭē varavēṇṭum. atāvatu mēliruntu, kīḻākak kuṟittuk koṇṭuvara vēṇṭum.



3. ataṟkup piṟaku āṭavēṇṭiya kuḻukkaḷi iṇi iṇiyāka (Pair) iṇittu, aṭippuk kuṟiyiṭṭuk kāṭṭa vēṇṭum.

tērnteṭuttu ciṟappiṭam tarum muṟi (Special Seeding)

oru āṭṭattil, tiṟami peṟṟa āṭṭakkārarkaḷ allatu āṭum kuḻukkaḷ iruppatuṇṭu.

avaṟṟi avari mutal cuṟṟiliruntu āṭaviṭāmal, nēraṭiyāka kāl iṟuti āṭṭattil kalantu koḷvatu pōla, ciṟappiṭam aḷikkum muṟiyum irukkiṟatu.

im muṟi cariyāṉatu illiyeṉṟālum, pōṭṭit toṭaril puttuṇarcciyi ēṟpaṭuttuvataṟkākavē kiyāḷappaṭukiṉṟatu.

iṅkē nām 12 kuḻukkaḷa kalantu koḷḷum oru toṭar pōṭṭiyil, 4 aṇikaḷit tērntu ciṟappiṭam tarukiṟōm. antap pōṭṭip paṭṭiyali eppaṭit tayārippatu eṉṟu pārppōm. 12 kuḻukkaḷukkāṉa pōṭṭi niral

mutal cuṟṟu kāl iṟuti ari iṟuti iṟuti āṭṭam





kuṟippu :

1. 4 kuḻukkaḷi mutalil tērnteṭuttuk koṉkiṟōm.



2. avaṟṟi cīṭṭuk kulukkal mūlam poṟukki eṭukkiṟōm.



3. mutalāvatāka varukiṟa kuḻuvi — kīḻppāti kaṭiciyil 12l kuṟikkiṟōm.



4. 2vatāka varuvati mēlpāti kīḻil 6l kuṟikkiṟōm.



5. 3vatāka varuvati kīḻppāti mēlil 7l. kuṟikkiṟōm.



6. 4vatāka varuvati mēlpāti 1l kuṟikkiṟōm.

7. piṟaku kuḻukkaḷi iṇi iṇiyāka cērttu viṭukiṟōm.

ippaṭiyāka, intap pōṭṭi niralit tayārikka vēṇṭum. itu pōl, ettaṉi kuḻukkaḷ vantālum, muṟiyāka ciṟappiṭak kuḻukkaḷi tērnteṭuttu, ciṟappiṭa vilakkukaṉik kaṇakkiṭṭu, nirali tayārikka vēṇṭum.

āṟutal vāyppu muṟi paktayaṅkaḷ

(Consolation Tournaments)



oru vāyppuḷḷa pōṭṭit toṭaril, tiṟami. vāynta oru kuḻu ētō oru kāraṇattiṟkāka, acātāraṇa muṟiyil tōṟṟuppōvatuṇṭu.

appaṭi ēṟpaṭukiṟa tōlviyāl, iṭamiḻantu pōkum. antat tiṟamiyāṉa kuḻu, taṉatu muḻu tiṟamiyiyum. veḷippaṭuttik kāṭṭa muṭiyāmal pōy viṭuvatāl, appaṭippaṭṭa kuṟiyip pōkki, mīṇṭum āṭa vāyppaḷippataṉ mūlam, avarkaḷ taṅkaḷ muḻut. tiṟamiyiyum veḷippaṭuttikkāṭṭi, miñci irukkiṉṟa. veṟṟiyiyum, paricukaḷiyum, pārāṭṭukkaḷiyum peṟa. vaḻi ēṟpaṭutti vāypput taruvatē, āṟutal vāypput toṭar pōṭṭi eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭukiṟatu.

itaṉi, iraṇṭu vakiyākki, vāypput taruvārkaḷ. mutalil mutal vakiyip pārppōm. (First Type) 13 kuḻukkaḷukkāṉa āṟutal vāyppuk toṭar pōṭṭi





mutal cuṟṟil tōṟṟa 2, 4, 5, 9, 11 ciṟappiṭam peṟṟa mutal cuṟṟil āṭāmal, 2m cuṟṟil āṭit tōṟṟa 13m, āṟutal pōṭṭiyil iṭam peṟṟuk koḷkiṉṟaṉa

avaṟṟiṟku. nām pōṭṭi niral tayārikka vēṇṭum.

tōṟṟirukkum kuḻukkaḷiṉ eṇṇikki 6.

ākavē, itil 2 ciṟappiṭa vilakku nām aḷikka vēṇṭum.



āṟutal vāyppu pōṭṭi niral





kuṟippum vilakkamum

1. inta āṟutal vāyppil, tōṟṟa kuḻu, iraṇṭāvatāka viḷiyāṭukiṟa vāyppip peṟukiṟatu.

2. tōṟṟak kuḻuviṉar taṅkaḷukkuḷḷē, mīṇṭum vāypput toṭar pōṭṭiyi āṭi, niṟivu koḷkiṉṟaṉar.

3. mutal cuṟṟil tōṟṟa kuḻukkaḷiyum, ciṟappiṭam peṟṟu 2vatu cuṟṟil āṭit tōṟṟa kuḻukkaḷiyum, intap pōṭṭi niralil cērttuk koḷḷalām.

4. āṟutal vāyppup pōṭṭikkāṉa pōṭṭi niral tayārikkiṟa poḻutum, iraṭṭip perukkam, atil vilakkam tarukiṟa vitimuṟikaḷiyum, iṅkē kaṭipiṭittiṭa vēṇṭum.

5. atikamāṉa pōṭṭikaḷ naṭipeṟuvatāl, pōṭṭit toṭarāṉatu puttuṇarcci ḷittiṭum perumiyiyum vāyppiyum peṟukiṟatu.

4 ciṟappuvilakku kuḻu (Bye) = 4 — 1 = 3 pōṭṭikaḷ atika aḷavu : 8 ciṟappu vilakku peṟāta kuḻukkaḷ

ākavē 8 — 1 = 7 pōṭṭikaḷ

3. kalantu koṇṭirukkiṉṟa kuḻukkaḷ, naṭuvṉ eṇṇukkum kīḻāṉatāka iruntāl (less than the middle number)

naṭuvaṉ eṇṇuḷḷa amippiṉpaṭiyē, āṟutal āṭṭaṅkaḷ āṭappaṭum.

naṭuvaṉ eṇṇukkum atikamāṉa amippil, kuḻukkaḷ iruntāl 2ai kaḻikka vēṇṭum.

um : 10 kuḻukkaḷ paṅku peṟukiṉṟaṉa.

kuṟinta paṭca āṭṭam kaṇṭupiṭikka :10kku naṭueṇ 5 eṉṟu koḷḷavum.

ākavē, 5 — 2 = 3 āṟutal āṭṭaṅkaḷ kuṟinta paṭcam. atikapaṭca āṟatal āṭṭam evvaḷavu eṉpati aṟiya (n - minus 2 minus minimum) eṉṟu kaṇakkiṭuka.

motta kuḻu eṇṇikki 10.

atil 2ai kaḻittāl —

kuṟintapaṭca āṭṭaṅkaḷi kaḻittāl 10 — 2 — 3 = 5

āka, 5 pōṭṭi āṟutal āṭṭaṅkaḷi naṭatta vēṇṭum.

4. kalantu koḷḷum kuḻukkaḷiṉ eṇṇikki, naṭuvaṉ eṇṇukkum atikamāka iruntāl. (more than the middle number).

ataṟkāṉa cūttiram - naṭuvaṉ eṇ ettaṉiyō anta eṇṇikki tāṉ kuṟinta paṭca pōṭṭi āṭṭam.

atika aḷavu āṭṭam kaṇṭupiṭikka- (n- minus 2 and minus maximum)

utāraṇattiṟku pārppōm.

15 kuḻukkaḷ paṅku peṟukiṉṟaṉa.

kuṟinta paṭcam pōṭṭi āṭṭam : naṭuvaṉ eṇ 15kku 7.

ākavē 7 āṟutal āṭṭaṅkaḷ āṭalām. atikapaṭca āṭṭaṅkaḷ kāṇa : 15-2-7 = 6āṭṭaṅkaḷ

iṉi, āṟutal pōṭṭiyiṉ 2vatu vakiyip pārppōm.

inta iraṇṭāvatu vaki āṟutal pōṭṭiyil, pōṭṭiyil tōṟṟu vāyppiḻanta ellāk kuḻukkaḷumē, pōṭṭiyiṭṭu, ataṟkuriya veṟṟip perumiyi aṭiya vāyppu ēṟpaṭukiṟatu.

ārampac cuṟṟukaḷil mōtiya kuḻukkaḷiyē, mīṇṭum mōtaviṭāmal tavirttu, pōṭṭi niralit tayārikka vēṇṭum.

intap pōṭṭi nirali iraṇṭu muṟiyāka nām tayārikkalām.

1. ciṟappu vilakku tarātapaṭi tayārittal.

2. ciṟappiṭam (vilakku) koṭuttu tayārittal.

inta pōṭṭi niral muṟi eppaṭi amintatu eṉṟu iṅkē viḷakkamākak kāṇpōm.

1. eṭṭu kuḻu mutalil tōṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟukku iṭiyē pōṭṭi naṭatti, 4 kuḻuvi tērnteṭukkiṟōm.



2. iraṇṭāvatu cuṟṟil tōṟṟuḷḷa 4 kuḻukkaḷuṭaṉ, mutal cuṟṟil veṟṟi peṟṟa nāṉku kuḻuviyum pōṭṭi iṭa vikkiṟōm. 3. inta 2vatu cuṟṟil veṟṟi peṟṟu varukiṟa 2 kuḻukkaḷuṭaṉ, 3vatu cuṟṟil tōṟṟirunta 2 kuḻukkaḷuṭaṉ pōṭṭiyiṭa vikkiṟōm.

4. itil jeyitta oru kuḻuvi iṟutip pōṭṭiyil tōṟṟa kuḻuvuṭaṉ pōṭṭiyiṭa vikkiṟōm.

iṉi, ciṟappiṭam koṭuttu (Byes) eppaṭi inta āṟutal pōṭṭi niralit tayārikka vēṇṭum eṉpatip pārppōm.

1. mutal cuṟṟil oru oru kuḻu tāṉ tōṟṟirukkiṟatu.

2. 2vatu cuṟṟil 4 kuḻukkaḷ tōṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa.

3. 2vatu cuṟṟil tōṟṟativiṭa, mutal cuṟṟil tōṟṟa kuḻu kuṟinta eṇṇikkiyil iruppatāl, atiyum intak kuḻukkaḷuṭaṉ cērttuk koṇṭu, 3vatu cuṟṟil tōṟṟa kuḻu eṇṇikkikku ēṟpa, kuṟivāka varuvatu pōl, ceytu koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

4. maṟṟa cuṟṟukkaḷukku, muntiya āṟutal niralik kāṇka.

ippoḻutu, ciṟappiṭam tanta, 9 kuḻukkaḷukkuriya pōṭṭi niralik kāṇpōm.

6 ciṟappiṭam tarappaṭṭuḷḷatu.

āṟutal pōṭṭi niral





itil 9, veṟṟik kuḻuvāka aṟivikkappaṭukiṟatu.

inta 2vatu muṟiyil uḷḷa ciṟappamcam eṉṉaveṉṟāl, tōṟṟa kuḻukkaḷi, ovvoru cuṟṟāka eṭuttu, pōṭṭiyiṭa vittiruppatu tāṉ.

ovvoru cuṟṟilum pōṭṭiyiṭṭa kuḻukkaḷil veṉṟa kuḻu, aṭutta cuṟṟil veṟṟi peṟṟa kuḻuvuṭaṉ pōṭṭiyiṭṭu, iṟutiyāka, veṟṟi nilikku varuvatu tāṉ ciṟappamcamākum.

inta 2vatu vakiyil uḷḷa āṟutal pōṭṭi niralil, 2vatu muṟiyē ciṟantatāka, vallunarkaḷāl ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.

muṟiyāka veṟṟiniliyi aṭinta kuḻu veṟṟi yāḷarākavum (Winner) , āṟutal pōṭṭiyil veṟṟiyi aṭinta kuḻuvi 2vatu veṟṟik kuḻuvākavum (Runner8 tp) aṅkīkarikkappaṭukiṟatu.

inta iraṇṭu muṟikkum uḷḷa oṟṟumi-muṉṟiyāka pōṭṭiyil veṟṟi peṟṟu vanta kuḻuvi mutaliṭam koṭuttu pārāṭṭuvatu tāṉ.

āṉāl, āṟutal pōṭṭiyil veṟṟi peṟṟa kuḻuvi 2vatu veṟṟik kuḻuvāka ēṟṟuk koṇṭālum, antak kuḻu ellāk kuḻukkaḷiṭamum viḷiyāṭi vellāmal, āṟutal pōṭṭik kuḻukkaḷi maṭṭumē veṉṟuvaruvatāl, itu oru kuṟiyākavē irukkiṟatu.

itil ettaṉi pōṭṭi āṭṭaṅkaḷ naṭakkum eṉṟu kaṇṭaṟiya cūttiram (2n-3).

3. iraṭṭi vāyppu muṟi (Double Knock Out)

iraṭṭi vāyppu muṟi toṭar pōṭṭip pantayattil, oru kuḻu, pōṭṭiyiliruntu nīkkappaṭa vēṇṭumeṉṟāl, atu iraṇṭu muṟi tōṟkaṭikkappaṭa vēṇṭum.

pōṭṭiyil paṅku peṟukiṟa kuḻukkaḷ aṉittum, iraṇṭu muṟi tōṟkiṟa variyil, intap pōṭṭit toṭar toṭarum.

intap pōṭṭiyāṉatu, oṟṟi vāyppu muṟi, āṟutal vāyppu muṟi pantayaṅkaḷi viṭa ciṟantatākum.

ituvum āṟutal pōṭṭit toṭar muṟiyiṉ, iraṇṭāvatu vakiyic cērntatākum. ēṉeṉṟāl, muṟiyākap peṟṟa veṟṟik kuḻuvum (RW), āṟutal pōṭṭi niralil veṟṟi peṟṟa kuḻuvum pōṭṭiyiṭṭa piṟaku tāṉ, uṇmiyāṉa veṟṟik kuḻuvi terintu koḷḷa muṭiyum.

itaṟkāṉa cūttiram (2n—2) allatu (2n—1).

9 kuḻukkaḷukkāṉa iraṭṭi vāyppu muṟi pōṭṭi niral





kuṟippu : 8m nampar kuḻu, 1m nampar kuḻuvukkup patilāka veṟṟi peṟṟiruntāl, iraṇṭu kuḻukkaḷum, mīṇṭum viḷiyāṭiyē ākavēṇṭum. ēṉeṉṟāl, kuḻu 8m 1m, oru muṟitāṉ tōṟṟirukkiṟatu eṉpati aṟiyavum. 4. pakṉāl oylṭ nīkku muṟi pōṭṭit toṭar

(Bagnall wild Elimination Tournament)

uṇmiyāṉa mutal 3 veṟṟik kuḻukkaḷiyum intap pōṭaṭi muṟi naṉku terivu ceyyum vāyppiṉi nalkukiṟatu.

10 kuḻukkaḷukkāṉa pōṭṭi niral





eppaṭi mutal mūṉṟu iṭam vantatu eṉṟu iṅkē kāṇpōm. mutal nili I : oru vāyppu muṟi pōṭṭiyiṉ mūlamāka 6 mutal iṭam peṟṟu viṭṭatu.

iraṇṭām nili 1 : mutaliṭam peṟṟa (6) kuḻuviṭam iṟutip pōṭṭiyil tōṟṟa (4) kuḻuvittavira, maṟṟa kuḻukkaḷ mīṇṭum pōṭṭiyiṭukiṟa vāyppip peṟṟu. atil mutalāvatāka varukiṟa kuḻu (7), iṟutip pōṭṭiyil tōṟṟa (4) kuḻuvuṭaṉ pōṭṭiyiṭa iraṇṭām nili nirṇayikkappaṭukiṟatu.

mīṇṭum pārppōm. 6 mutalnili peṟṟa kuḻu. 6 iṭam tōṟṟa kuḻu 4 mīṇṭum tōṟṟa kuḻukkaḷ pōṭṭi āyiṭukiṟapōtu, 9m 7m mōtukiṟatu, atil 7 veṟṟi peṟukiṟatu.

veṟṟi peṟṟa 7m nampar kuḻuvum, muṉṉar iṟutim pōṭṭiyil tōṟṟa 4m nampar kuḻuvum pōṭṭiyiṭa 4m tampar kuḻu jeyikkavē, atu iraṇṭām iṭattip peṟukiṟatu.

mūṉṟām nili III : iṟutip pōṭṭiyil tōṟṟak kuḻuviṭam (4) tōṟṟuppōṉa kuḻukkaḷ (mēlpakutiyil uḷḷatu) taṅkaḷukkuḷḷē pōṭṭiyiṭṭu: atil veṟṟi peṟukiṟa kuḻu iṟutip pōṭṭiyil tōṟṟak kuḻuviṭam mōta, atil vellukiṟa kuḻu 2m iṭattip peṟukiṟatu.

um : iṟutip pōṭṭiyil tōṟṟa 4m nampar kuḻuviṭam 1m 5m tōṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa. 4m 5m pōṭṭiyiṭa, atil veṟṟipeṟukiṟa kuḻuvāṉa 5m namparuṭaṉ (kīḻppātiyil) aṭutta pātiyil veṟṟi peṟṟuḷḷa 7m namparuṭaṉ pōṭṭiyiṭa vēṇṭum. itil vellukiṟa kuḻu 3m iṭattip peṟukiṟatu.

kuṟippu : intap pōṭṭiyil 4m nampar jeyippataṟkup patilāka, 7m nampar kuḻu jeyittuviṭṭāl, 7m nampar kuḻu 2m iṭattip peṟukiṟatu. tōṟṟa 4m nampar kuḻu, muṉṉarē, iṟutip pōṭṭiyil tōṟṟiruntālum 7m nampariṭam tōṟṟataṉ kāraṇamāka, mūṉṟām niliyip peṟukiṟatu.



11. toṭar vāyppup pantayaṅkaḷ

(League or Round Robin Tournment)

intap pantaya muṟiyi, 2 vakiyākap pirikkalām.

1. oru toṭar vāyppumuṟi. (Single league)

2. iraṭṭit toṭar vāyppu muṟi Double league)

1. oru toṭar vāyppu muṟiyil, ellāk kuḻukkaḷum, oṉṟukku oṉṟu eṉa oru taṭavi maṭṭum. pōṭṭiyiṭukiṉṟaṉa.

itaṉāl ēṟpaṭum mottam pōṭṭi āṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ eṇṇikki ataṟkāṉa kuttiram n (n-1) + 2.

﻿um : 8 kuḻukkaḷ pōṭṭiyiṭukiṉṟaṉa.

8 (8-1) ÷ 2 = 8 ×7÷2 = 56÷2 = 28

motta pōṭṭi āṭṭaṅkaḷ 28 ākum.

2. iraṭṭi toṭar vāyppu muṟiyil, ellāk kuḻukkaḷum, oṉṟukku oṉṟu iraṇṭu taṭavi pōṭṭi pōṭukiṉṟaṉa. motta pōṭṭi āṭṭaṅkaḷik kaṇṭu piṭippataṟkāṉa cūttiram : n (n-1).

um: 8 kuḻukkaḷ eṉṟāl 8 (8-1) = 8x 7 = 56

toṭar vāyppup pantayattil uḷḷa niṟiyum kuṟiyum

niṟikaḷ

1. pōṭṭiyiṭum kuḻukkaḷiliruntu, uṇmiyāṉa tiṟamivāynta veṟṟik kuḻuvik kaṇṭupiṭikka utavukiṟatu.

2. atikamāṉa eṇṇikkiyil pōṭṭi āṭṭaṅkaṉ naṭipeṟukiṉṟaṉa.

3. kuḻukkaḷiṉ tiṟamiyi varicippaṭutta itu utavukiṟatu.

4. oru vāyppu muṟiyil naṭappatu pōla, oru kuḻu āṭi jeyittu varukiṟa varikkum, maṟṟa kuḻukkaḷ vīṇākak kāttuk koṇṭirukka vēṇṭiya avaciyamilli. āṭuvataṟkut tayārarka enta kuḻu irukkiṟatō, ataṉuṭaṉ āṭiṭa muṭiyum.

kuṟikaḷ

1. atikamāṉa pōṭṭi āṭṭaṅkaḷ naṭipeṟuvatāl, atika nēram eṭuttuk koḷkiṟatu. vacatikaḷ ceytu koṭukka vēṇṭiyiruppatāl, poruṭ celavum atikamākiṟatu.

2. aṭikkaṭi tōṟkiṟa kuḻu, uṟcākam iḻantu, pōkiṟatu. pōṭṭiyil atika īṭupāṭu koṉvatum ataṉāl kuṟikiṟatu.

oṟṟit toṭar vāyppu pōṭṭi viral (Single)

6 kuḻukkaḷukkāṉatu. cūttiram 6 × (6-1)÷2 = 15.

atāvatu 6 × 5 = 30 ÷ 2 = 15 ākum.

mutalcuṟṟu II III IV V

6-1 5–1 4–1 3–1 2 -1 mottam

5–2 4-6 3-5 2-4 6-3 15 āṭṭaṅkaḷ

4–3 3–4 2–6 6–5 5-4

itu (cikkiḷ cuḻal) vaṭṭap pōṭṭi niral muṟi. (Cyclic method) eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭukiṟatu.

aṭuttuḷḷa iṉṉoru muṟi : aṭṭavaṇi muṟi (Tabular method) 5 kuḻukkaḷukkāṉatu





inta aṭṭavaṇi muṟikkuriya kaṭṭaṅkaḷi piṉvarumāṟu amittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

3. paṭikkaṭṭu muṟi (Stircase method)

7 kuḻukkaḷukkāṉa pōṭṭi niral





intap paṭikkaṭṭu muṟiyil uḷḷa kuṟikaḷāvaṉa:-

1. kuḻukkaḷ ellām oṉṟiyoṉṟu cantittu āṭa vēṇṭiya cuṟṟukkaḷi, nām itil kaṇṭaṟivatilli.

﻿ 2. vaṭṭamuṟi, aṭṭavaṇimuṟi ivikaḷip pōla, tayārittuḷḷa paṭikkaṭṭup pōṭṭi niral muṟiyil, ententa nāṭkaḷil ententak kuḻukkaḷ pōṭṭiyiṭa vēṇṭum eṉpati eḷitil kuṟittuk kāṭṭavum iyalātu.

3. pantayattil kalantu koḷkiṟa kuḻukkaḷiṉ eṇṇikki oṟṟippaṭiyāka iruntāl, ententa cuṟṟil (Round) ententak kuḻuviṟku ciṟappiṭam aḷittu vilakkaḷikka (Bye) vēṇṭum, eṉpatiyum aṟintu koḷḷa iyalātu.

toṭar vāyppup pantayattil, veṟṟik kuḻuvit tīrmāṉikkum muṟi (To decide the winners)

kuḻuvum camanili (Tie) aṭintāl -

1 veṟṟi eṇ





pōṭṭit toṭaril, atikamāṉa veṟṟi eṇkaḷip peṟukiṟa kuḻuvē, veṟṟi peṟṟatu eṉṟu aṟivikka vēṇṭum.

veṟṟi eṇkaḷ vikitattil, iraṇṭō allatu ataṟkum mēṟpaṭṭa kuḻukkaḷ camanili peṟṟiruntāl, antat taṭiyit tīrttu, veṟṟikkuḻu etuveṉṟu tīrmāṉikka, kīḻkkāṇum vitimuṟikaḷip payaṉ paṭuttalām.

camakiliyi camāḷittu veṟṟiyit tīrmāṉikkum vitikaḷ

1. camanili peṟṟ 2 kuḻukkaḷil, mutal pōṭṭiyiṉ pōtu oṉṟi mutalil veṉṟuḷḷa kuḻuviyē veṟṟik kuḻuveṉṟu aṟivikkalām.

2. appoḻutum veṟṟi tōlviyi aṟiṉ muṭiyāta aḷavil cikkal iruntāl, camaniliyil irukkum kuḻukkaḷil, entak kuḻu, atikamāṉa kuḻukkaḷi veṉṟirukkiṟatu eṉpatik, kaṇṭaṟintu, mutaliṭam koṭukkalām.

3. mīṇṭum tīrkka muṭiyātapaṭi cikkal iruntāl. entak kuḻu antap pōṭṭi muḻuvatilum atika veṟṟi eṇkaḷ peṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa (Points), allatu atika kōlkaḷ (Goal) pōṭṭiruk kiṉṟaṉa eṉpatik kaṇṭaṟintu, muṭivi eṭukkalām.

mēlum purintu koḷvataṟkāka, tantiṭum cāṉṟiṉim pāruṅkaḷ. AB eṉṟa 2 kuḻukkaḷ, hākkiyil camaniliyil uḷḷaṉa. ataṟkāṉa muṭiviṉi aṟiya utavum paṭṭiyal itu.

inta eṇṇikki aḷavilum iru kuḻukkaḷum camaniliyil iruntāl,

III oruṅkiṇinta toṭar pōṭṭip pantayaṅkaḷ (Combination Tournaments)

atikamāṉa eṇṇikkiyil, kuḻukkaḷ paṅku peṟukiṟapōtu, intap pantaya muṟiyip payaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷalām.

varukiṟa kuḻukkaḷi pala pirivukaḷāka (Group) allatu pala tokutikaḷākap (Zones) pirittuviṭṭu, ovvoru piriviyum taṉittaṉiyē āṭaviṭṭu, avaṟṟil varukiṟa veṟṟik kuḻukkaḷi maṭṭum, oru vāyppu muṟi allatu pala vāypput toṭar muṟi mūlamāka āṭaviṭṭu, muṭivu kāṇalām.

tamiḻakam muḻuvatum kalantu koḷkiṟa pōṭṭi eṉṟu vittuk koḷvōm. ellā kuḻukkaḷiyum koṇṭu vantu, ōriṭattil pōṭṭi naṭattuvatu eṉpatu mikavum ciramamāṉa kāriyam.

ākavē, tamiḻakatti 4 tokutiyākap pirittu (A,B,C,D Zones) ovvoru tokutiyiyum āṅkāṅkē āṭaviṭukiṟa muṟi.

﻿ 4. cavāl pōṭṭip pantayaṅkaḷ (Challange Tournaments)

cavāl pōṭṭip pantayaṅkaḷ oṟṟiyar pantayam, allatu iraṭṭiyar pantayam uḷḷa viḷiyāṭṭukaḷukku maṭṭumē poruntum. (Singles and Doubles). pūppantāṭṭam, ṭeṉṉis, mēcip pantāṭṭam, ṭeṉṉi kāṭṭ pōṉṟa āṭṭaṅkaḷi itaṟkuc cāṉṟākak kūṟalām.

kirikkeṭ, kippantāṭṭam pōṉṟa mutaṉmi viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷ pōṉṟa kuḻu viḷiyāṭṭukaḷukku, itu tēvippaṭātatu.

intap pōṭṭiyil kalantu koḷkiṟa aṉivarum, oruvarukkoruvar taṉit taṉiyē pōṭṭiyiṭṭu, veṟṟi peṟṟu vara vēṇṭum.

inta muṟiyil, taṉippaṭṭa ciṟanta vīrarkaḷi, kavaṉattuṭaṉ tērnteṭuttu viṭa muṭikiṟatu.

iti oraṭukku muṟi eṉṟu kūṭa kūṟalām.

ciṟanta āṭṭakkārarkaḷuṭaṉ, tiṟamiyaṟṟa āṭṭakkārarkaḷ mōtuvatu eṉpatu itil illi. ellōrum ellāruṭaṉum pōṭṭiyiṭṭē āka vēṇṭum eṉpatu tāṉ, inta muṟiyiṉ ciṟappamcamākum.

cīṭṭuk kulukkal mūlamākavō, terintu vittirukkum taṉ muṉippu mūlamākavō, pōṭṭi niralit tayārikkalām.

appaṭi āṭavarātavar allatu maṟuppavar, tamatu takuti iṭatti viṭṭu māṟṟappaṭak kūṭiya niliyi aṭikiṟār.

4–2. kōpura muṟi (Pyramid method)

ēṇi muṟiyil caṟṟu māṟupaṭṭa vaṭivamippuḷḷatu. itu.

ēṇi muṟikkāṉa viti muṟikaḷ. aṉittum itaṟkup poruntum. cīṭṭuk kulukkal mūlam allatu āṭṭakkārarkaḷit terinteṭuttup pōṭuvataṉ mūlamākap pōṭṭi niralit tayār ceyyalām.

taṉakkuriya pakutiyil iruntu (Rank) aṭuttu mēl variciyil uḷḷavarkaḷi maṭṭumē cavāl iṭṭu aḻikkalām. atuvum taṉatu pakutiyil uḷḷavarkaḷuṭaṉ veṟṟi peṟṟiruntāl maṭṭumē atu iyalum.

12. kuḻup pōṭṭikaḷ (Group Competitions)

uṭaṟkalvip pāṭa vakuppukaḷil, palvēṟu vitamāṉa, uṭal iyakkac ceyalkaḷ, aṭippaṭit tiṟamikaḷuṭaṉ viḷakkam tantu kaṟpikkap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.

ārvamuṭaṉ avaṟṟik kaṟṟuk koḷkiṟa māṇavarkaḷ payiṟcikaṇit tiṟampaṭac ceytu, tiṟamikaḷi vaḷarttuk koḷkiṉṟaṉar.

vaḷarttuk koṇṭa tiṟamikaḷi, maṟṟavarkaḷuṭaṉ pōṭṭiyiṭṭut terintu koḷḷavum, mēlum mēlum tiṟamikaṉi vaḷarttuk koḷḷavum kūṭiya vāyppukaḷi, kuḻup pōṭṭikaḷ koṭukkiṉṟaṉa.

ataṟkuriya vāyppukḷāka amivaṉa-vakuppukaḷuk kiṭiyē pōṭṭikaḷ. māṇavar pirivukaḷukkiṭiyē pōṭṭikaḷ ākum.

kuḻup pōṭṭikaḷil kiṭikkum naṉmikaḷ

1. kuḻup pōṭṭikaḷ, māṇavarkaḷiṭiyē ārvattiyum, uṟcākattiyum ūṭṭi vaḷarkkiṉṟaṉa.

2. paṅku peṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟu māṇavarkaḷiṉ viruppam mēlum virivaṭikiṟatu. vēkam koḷkiṟatu.

3. itaṉāl viḷiyāṭṭut tiṟamikaḷiṉ vaḷarcci mēlōṅkukiṟatu.

4. ovvoru māṇavarum taṉippaṭṭa muṟiyil paṅku peṟuvatil ēṟpaṭukiṟa makiḻcciyiyum, taṉatu kuḻuvukkut taṉ paṅkiṉi āṟṟukiṟōm eṉṟa tiruptiyiyum peṟukiṉṟaṉar.

5. pōṭṭikaḷil paṅku peṟuvatāl, kuṟippiṭṭa anta viḷiyāṭṭil uḷḷa pala vitimuṟikaḷi, teḷivākat terintu koḷḷa muṭikiṟatu.

6. viḷiyāṭṭu pōṭṭikaḷiṉ mūlamāka, talimi ēṟṟu naṭattal; talimikkup paṇital, ūkka muṭaṉ iṟutivari pōṭṭiyiṭutal, veṟṟi tōlviyāl pātikkappaṭāta maṉam peṟutal ākiya paṇpāṉa kuṇaṅkaḷ meṉmēlum vaḷarcci peṟukiṉṟaṉa.

ippaṭippaṭṭa kuḻu viḷiyāṭṭukaḷukkup porutta māṉa viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷi cūḻnili pārttu, tērnteṭukka vēṇṭum.

iṟu viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷ, muṉṉōṭi viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷ, cākacac ceyalkaḷ. taṉittiṟaṉ pōṭṭikaḷ, taṉinaṭip vayiṟcikaḷ, oḻuṅku muṟi uṭaṟpayiṟcikaḷ, kōpurap payiṟcikaḷ, jimṉālṭik payiṟcikaḷ ellām ciṟappāṉa mūḻup pōṭṭikaḷākum,

kuḻup pōṭṭikaḷi kaṭattum muṟikaḷ 1. 10 allatu 15 māṇavarkaḷ oru kuḻuvil iruppatu pōla, pala kuḻukkaḷip pirittu, ovvoru kuḻuviṟkum oru kuḻut talivaṉi niyamikkavēṇṭum.

2. kuḻukkaḷip pirikkiṟapōtu, tiṟamiyil, uṭal valimiyil camamāka māṇavarkaḷ iruppatu pōla, pirikka vēṇṭum.

3. ovvoru kuḻuviyum aḻippatu pōla, ētāvatu oru peyar taralām. (pūkkaḷ, mirukaṅkaḷ paṟavikaḷ, niṟaṅkaḷ ētāvatu oṉṟil)

4. muṉṉatākac cuṟṟukkoṭutta viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷil ētāvatu oṉṟip pōṭṭi viḷiyāṭṭākat tērnteṭukka vēṇṭum.

5. kuṟippiṭṭa nērattiṟkuḷ, muṭivu peṟuvatāka, antap pōṭṭi amiya vēṇṭum.

6. kuḻukkaḷ peṟukiṟa veṟṟi nilikku ēṟpa, veṟṟi eṇkaḷi aḷikka vēṇṭul. mutal iraṇṭu, mūṉṟu, nāṉku nilikaḷukkāka 5, 3, 2, 1 eṉṟu veṟṟi eṇkaḷ taralām. 7. 200 catavikitam varuki tarukiṟa kuḻukkaḷukku, ciṟappu veṟṟi eṇkaḷ koṭukkavum ceyyalām.

8. varuṭat toṭakkattiliruntē, pōṭṭikaḷit. toṭaṅki, naṭattik koṇṭē vantu, varuṭak kaṭiciyil paricukaḷ taralām.

itaṟkāṉa veṟṟi eṇ aṭṭavaṇiyi piṉ pakkattil kāṇpōm.
