﻿13. uḷḷakap pōṭṭikaḷ

(INTRAMURAL COMPETITÍONS)

Intramural eṉṟa āṅkilac collukku with in the walls atāvatu nāṉku cuvarkaḷukkuḷḷē epuaiṟa poruḷākum.

oru niṟuvaṉam allatu paḷḷi, kallūrikkuḷṉē taṭiai peṟukiṉṟa pōṭṭikaḷi uḷḷakap pōṭṭikaḷ eṉṟu nām aḻikkalām.

kōkkaṅkaḷ

1. kuṟippiṭṭa oru viḷiyāṭṭu ceyalkaḷukkāṉa tiṟamikaḷi vaḷarttu, māṇavarkaḷit tiṟamiyākavumkaḷākka, uḷḷakap pōṭṭikaḷ utavukiṉṟaṉa. 2. ellā māṇavarkaḷukkum cama vāyppu eṉkiṟa, muṟiyil, (paḷḷi) māṇavarkaḷ aṉivarum, atika eṇṇikkiyil pōṭṭiyil kalantu koṇṭu, taṅkaḷ tiṟamikaḷi veḷippaṭuttum vāyppiṉip peṟukiṉṟaṉar.

3. talimiyēṟṟup piṟari vaḻi naṭattavum, talimi vakippavarkaḷiṉ vaḻi naṭantu piṉpaṟṟic cella vum kūṭiya pērāṇmi kuṇaṅkaḷi vaḷarttuviṭa.pōṭṭi kaḷ utavukiṉṟaṉa.

4. makiḻcci, kēḷikki, cantōṣam, tiruptiyil māṇavarkaḷ tiḷikkum vāyppukaḷ kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa. payiṟci niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷil payilum māṇavarkaḷukku, pōṭṭikaḷi naṭattukiṉṟa aṉupavaṅkaḷ taruvatu, pāṭat tiṭṭaṅkaḷil oṉṟāka viḷaṅkukiṟatu.

pōṭṭikaḷi naṭattuvatu, pōṭṭikaḷil atikāri نجینه (taḷākap paṇiyāṟṟuvatu, pōṉṟa kāriyaṅkaḷil nalla aṉupavaṅkaḷit tantu, āṟṟali vaḷarttuttaravum uḷḷa kap pōṭṭikaḷ utavukiṉṟaṉa.

uḷḷakap pōṭṭikaḷi kaṭattum muṟikaḷ

uḷḷakap pōṭṭikaḷi naṭattuvataṟku muṉpāka, ḻēkkāṇum kuṟippukaḷik kaṇṭaṟintu, kaṭipiṭippatutallatu. 1. niṟuvaṉattiṉ taṉmi (māṇavarkaḷ viṭutiyil taṅkiyiruntu paṭikkum niṟuvaṉamā, vīṭṭiliruntu varupavarkaḷā eṉpataṟkēṟpa).

2. niṟuvaṉam irukkum iṭattiṉ taṭpa veppa amippu.

3. irukkiṉṟa vacatikaḷ (āṭukaḷaṅkaḷ utavip poruṭkaḷ, utaviyāḷarkaḷ mutaliyaṉa).

4. poruḷātāra vacati.

5. pōṭṭikaḷ naṭattak kiṭikkum nēram

6. māṇavarkaḷ peritum virumpip paṅkēṟkum viḷiyāṭṭukaḷ.

7. caka āciriyarkaḷiṭamiruntu kiṭikkiṉṟa utavi

pōṭṭikaḷukkākap kuḻu pirittal

1. māṇavarkaḷi , avaravarkaḷ tiṟamikkum cāmarttiyattiṟkum ēṟpa, pala pirivukaḷākap pirittu pōṭṭiyiṭac ceyyalām.

2. atikat tiṟamiyuḷḷavarkaḷ tiṟamiyuḷḷavarkaḷ, tiṟami kuṟintavarkaḷ eṉṟu mūṉṟu pirivukaḷākki, kuḻuk kaḷip pirikkalām.

3. viṭutiyil taṅkip paṭippavarkaḷ atikam iruntu, viṭutikaḷ oṉṟukku mēṟpaṭṭu iruntāl, viṭutikaḷuk kiṭiyē pōṭṭikaḷ eṉṟu naṭattalām.

4. viṭutikaḷ, vīṭṭiliruntu varupavarkaḷ, ippaṭiyum pōṭṭikaḷ naṭattalām. viṭutikaḷ pala iruppatāl, antanta viṭuti oru kuḻuvākum. vīṭṭiliruntu varupavarkaḷi, antanta ēriyā pārttup pirittu, pala kuḻukkaḷākkiyum pōṭṭiyiṭa vikkalām.

5. viṭutikaḷ illāta paḷḷikaḷ, niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷil, vakuppu vāriyākap pirikkalām : ciṟuvar, iḷiyōr. mūttavar eṉṟum pākupaṭuttalām.

6 . kallūriyāka iruntāl, tuṟi vāriyākap (Department) pirittu, pōṭṭikaḷ naṭattalām.

ippaṭippaṭṭa pōṭṭikaḷi naṭattiṭa, atiyum veṟṟikaramāka naṭattiṭa, ataṟkeṉṟu nirvākak kuḻu oṉṟi amittiṭa vēṇṭum. antak kamiṭṭiyil iṭam peṟupavarkaḷ paṟṟi, iṉi aṟintu koḷvōm.

uḷḷakap pōṭṭi naṭattum kuḻu (Intramural Committee)



1. uḷḷakap pōṭṭik kuḻu iyakkunar (Intramural Director)



mūtta uṭaṟkalvi āciriyar oruvarē iyakkunarākap poṟuppu ēṟpār. maṟṟa uṭaṟkalvi āciriyarkaḷil oruvar, tuṇi iyakkunarākap poṟuppēṟṟu utavuvār. maṟṟa vakuppu āciriyarkaḷum pōṭṭi naṭipeṟukiṟapōtu, utavi ceyvārkaḷ.



2. kuḻut talivarkaḷum, kuḻut tuṇittalivarkaḷum

ovvoru kuḻuviṉ talivarum, utavit talivarum, pōṭṭi naṭattum kuḻuvil, uṟuppiṉarāki viṭukiṟārkaḷ ivarkaḷil oruvar ceyalarākavum, tuṇic ceyalarākavum tērnteṭukkappaṭukiṟārkaḷ.

intap pōṭṭik kuḻutāṉ, pōṭṭi naṭattuvataṟkuriya vitimuṟikaḷi uruvākkit tarum.

ceyalar poṟuppēṟṟuḷḷavar, pōṭṭikkuḻu naṭattukiṉṟa kūṭṭam, atil niṟivēṟṟappaṭukiṉṟa nīrmāṉaṅkaḷ pōṭṭikaḷ paṟṟiya pōṭṭi niral, pōṭṭikaḷ , veṟṟi eṇ kuṟippēṭukaḷ, pōṉṟavaṟṟip pativu ceytu, pattirappaṭutti vittiṭal vēṇṭum.

etirppu maṉu ētāvatu vantāl, atṉit tīrkkum poṟuppu, pōṭṭik kuḻuvukkuriyatākum.

3. māṇavarkaḷukkup piṭitta poruttamāṉa viṉi yāṭṭukkaḷit tērnteṭuttal

mutaṉmi viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷ, oṭukaḷap pōṭṭikaḷ, kuṭṭikkaraṇamaṭittal, kōpurap payiṟcikaḷ, aṇi naṭi muṟikaḷ, taṟkāppuk kalikaḷ, tāḷalayap payiṟcikaḷ ellām pōṭṭi naṭattuvataṟku poruttici" ceyalkaḷākum.

ivaṟṟil ētāvatu oṉṟit tērnteṭuttu, ataṉi pōṭṭip paṭṭiyalil cērttu, kamiṭṭiyiṭam aṉumatiyum peṟṟukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum.

4. kāla nēra avakācam

uḷḷakap pōṭṭikaḷ āṇṭu muḻuvatu? naṭattappaṭa lām. kāl āṇṭu, ariyāṇṭut tērvu naṭattappaṭum kālattiṟkuḷḷē tāṉ (First and Second Term) atikamāṉa pōṭṭikaḷ naṭattappaṭa vēṇṭiyirukkum.

muṉṟāvatu paruvam (Term) muḻut tērvukkuriya kāla māṉatāl, appoḻutu pōṭṭikaḷ kuṟikkappaṭṭu, paṭippukku mukkiyattuvam aḷikkiṉṟa avaciyattiṟku āḷāka vēṇṭiyirukkiṟatu.

paḷḷi muṭinta piṟaku uḷḷa nēram. viṭumuṟi nāṭ kaṉil pōṭṭi.kaḷ naṭattuvatu poruttamāka irukkum. pantayaṅkaḷi oru vāyppu muṟi allatu toṭar vāyppu muṟiyil naṭattalām.

5. veṟṟi eṇ vaḻaṅkutal

veṟṟi peṟukiṟa kuḻukkaḷiṉ varicikku ēṟpa, veṟṟi eṇkaḷ vaḻaṅkappaṭal vēṇṭum. mutal kuḻuvukku 5 , iraṇṭāvatu kuḻuvukku 3, mūṉṟāvatu kuḻuvukku ,ē nāṉkām kuḻuvukku 1 eṉpatāka pōṭṭi muṭintavuṭaṉ vaḻaṅki, ati veṟṟi eṇ aṭṭavaṇiyil patintu viṭa vēṇṭum.

āṇṭu iṟutiyil, aṉittup pōṭṭikaḷum muṭinta piṟaku, atikamāṉa veṟṟi eṇkaḷip peṟṟirukkiṟa kuḻuvē, uḷḷakap pōṭṭi veṟṟik kuḻu (Intramural Champion) eṉṟu muṭivu ceyya vēṇṭum.

6. vekumatiyum pārāṭṭum

pōṭṭikaḷil veṟṟi peṟukiṟavarkaḷukku, niṉivil iruppatupōl, nalla paricukaḷi vaḻaṅkalām. ovvoru viḷiyāṭṭukkum oru veṟṟik kēṭayam allatu veṟṟik kōppiyi vaḻaṅkalām. cuḻal kōppikaḷum taralām.

uḷḷakap pōṭṭikaḷ pukaḻp palaki (Honour Board) eṉṟu oṉṟi amittu, atil veṟṟik kuḻukkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷip poṟittu vikkalām.

veṟṟik kuḻuvip pukippaṭam eṭuttum, pukaḻp palakiyil oṭṭi, veṟṟiyāḷarkaḷip perumippaṭuttalām.

uḷḷakap pōṭṭikaḷ paṟṟi cila kuṟippukaḷ

1. ciṟuvar, iḷiyōr, mūttōr eṉṟu māṇavarkaḷip pirittu, antantap pirivukkuḷ pōṭṭikaḷ naṭatti, ellā māṇavarkaḷiyum pōṭṭikaḷil paṅku peṟac ceyya vēṇṭum.

2. tiṟamiyāṉavarkaḷāka kuḻukkaḷil iṭampeṟac ceytu, antanta āṟṟal mikka kuḻu, appaṭippaṭṭa kuḻukkaḷuṭaṉ pōṭṭi iṭukiṉṟa cūḻniliyi amittut tarutal vēṇṭum.

3. paḷḷikaḷ cārpāka viḷiyāṭukiṟa tiṟamimikka āṭṭakkārarkaḷ, uḷḷakap pōṭṭikaḷil paṅku peṟāmal ceyya vēṇṭum.

4. pala pōṭṭi ril oru cila māṇavarkaḷē paṅku peṟuvatit tavirttu ōriraṇṭu pōṭṭikaḷil maṭṭumē paṅku peṟalām ṉṟa vitiyi ēṟpaṭuttik kaṭṭuppaṭuttuvatu nallatu.

5. paḷḷikaḷil irukkiṉṟa iṭavacati, utavi cātaṉaṅkaḷ, poruḷātāra vacati pōṉṟavaṟṟip payaṉpaṭuttik koāvatu pōla, pōṭṭikaḷi naṭatta vēṇṭum.

6. muḻu aḷavil paṅku peṟukiṟa kuḻukkaḷukku, atikamāka veṟṟi eṇkaḷi vaḻaṅka vēṇṭum.

7. māṟṟāṭṭakkārarkaḷi veḷiyē niṟuttiviṭṭu, nirantara āṭṭakkārarkaḷē āṭṭam muḻuvatiyum āṭi viṭuvārkaḷ. māṟṟāṭṭakkārarkaḷ kuṟintatu 10 nimiṭam āṭavēṇṭum. (kāl pantāṭṭim) allatu 4 veṟṟi eṇkaḷ eṭukkum (kip pantāṭṭam) variyilāvatu āṭukaḷattiṟkuḷ iṭam peṟṟu āṭavēṇṭum eṉkiṟa viṉi, kaṭṭāyappaṭutti, naṭimuṟippaṭutta vēṇṭum.

8. taṉippaṭṭa veṟṟiyāḷarkaḷ, allatu veṟṟik kuḻuviṟkum pārāṭṭum aḷittu keḷaravikka vēṇṭum.

ittaṉik karuttukkaḷum, māṇavarkaḷ mikutiyāṉa eṇṇikkiyil, uḷḷakap pōṭṭikaḷil paṅku peṟa ūkkuvikkum. uṟcākappaṭuttum. pōṭṭikaḷi veṟṟi mayamākkum.





﻿14. puṟa veḷip pōṭṭikaḷ





(EXTRAMURAL COMPETITIONS)





* * *



paḷḷikaḷukkiṭiyē allatu (kalvi maṟṟum toḻil, niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷukkiṭiyē naṭipeṟukiṉṟa viḷiyāṭṭup pōṭṭikaḷi puṟa veḷip pōṭṭikaḷ eṉṟu aḻikkiṉṟaṉar.

pōṭṭikaḷil paṅku peṟupavarkaḷ, taṅkaḷ tiṟami kaḷi maṟṟavarkaḷuṭaṉ pōṭṭiyiṭṭu, veḷippaṭuttik kāṭṭi, taṅkaḷuṭiya niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷukkup peyarum pukaḻum īṭṭit tarukiṉṟa vāyppukaḷi, puṟaveḷip pōṭṭikaḷ vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.

niṟikaḷ 1. puṟaveḷip pōṭṭikaḷil paṅku peṟupavarkaḷiṉ tiṟamiyum valimiyum tērcci peṟukiṉṟaṉa.

2. tāṅkaḷ cārntirukkum niṟuvaṉattiṉmēl taṉiyāta paṟṟum paktiyum perukum nili, eḻucciyaṭikiṉṟaṉa.

3. kuḻuttalivar mēl mariyāti, avar collukkuppaṇital, paṇpāṉa viḷiyāṭṭukkuṇaṅkaḷ vaḷartal, pōṉṟa ciṟanta paṇpāṭukaḷ vaḷarcci peṟukiṉṟaṉa.

4. veḷiyāruṭaṉ paḻakum vāyppu ēṟpaṭuvatāl, putiya putiya naṭpum, uṟavum kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa.

5. pōṭṭikkākap pala iṭaṅkaḷukkum. pōvatāl, pala putiya iṭaṅkaḷ paṟṟiya aṟivum teḷivum ēṟpaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

6. pōṭṭikaḷiṉ mūlam, makiḻcci, tirupti, uṟcākam ellām niṟiyavē kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa.

kuṟikaḷ 1. pōṭṭi eṉṟu vantu viṭṭālē, veṟṟipeṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟa veṟiyum vēkamum vantu viṭukiṟatu. ataṉāl eppaṭiyāvatu veṟṟipeṟa vēṇṭum eṉpataṟkāka, kuṟukku muṟikaḷip piṉpaṟṟi, tavaṟiḻittāvatu peṟṟāka vēṇṭum eṉṟu celkiṉṟa maṉappāṅku vaḷara vāyppirukkiṟatu.

2. cukātāramaṟṟa pōṭṭiyum, poṟāmi uṇarcciyum ēṟpaṭṭu viṭukiṉṟatu.

3. atika nēram vīṇ ; atika paṇac celavu ; atika uṭal cakti iḻappu eṉṟum ēṟpaṭṭu viṭukiṟatu.

4. māṇavarkaḷukkut tēviyaṟṟa maṉap pataṭṭam, maṉac cōrvu, maṉakkaḷippu ēṟpaṭukiṟatu.

5. cilar, taṅkaḷ tōlviyit tāṅkik koḷḷa muṭiyāmal, piṟariṭam colla veṭkappaṭṭuk koṇṭu poy pēci, perumiyaṭittuk koḷḷukiṟa pētamik kuṇamum vaḷara, vāyppi ēṟpaṭuttukiṟatu.



mēṟkūṟiya kuṟikaḷ ellām taṟkālikamāṉatu tāṉ. takartteṟiyappaṭṭu, cari ceytu viṭak kūṭiyavikaḷtāṉ.

takutiyāṉa talimiyuṭaṉ kaṭṭuppāṭu, maṉa uṟuti, ūkkam uḷḷavarkaḷāka māṇavarkaḷi vaḻi naṭattukiṟa pōtu, kuṟikaḷ kaḷinteṟiyappaṭṭu viṭum.

nalla viḷiyāṭṭu vīrarkaḷāka iruntāl kūṭa, tiṉantōṟum payiṟcikkum kūṭṭu muyaṟcikkum vantāl tāṉ, avarkaḷi kuḻuvil cērttuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum. appaṭi varukiṟa pōtu, tāṉ, oḻukkamum uyarnta paṇpu kaḷum vaḷarum. mēlē kūṟiya kuṟikaḷ varāmalum irukkum.

ataṟkum mēlāka, nalla talimi vēṇṭum. antat talimi tavaṟiṉṟi vaḻikāṭṭum takkārākavum viḷaṅka vēṇṭum.

puṟaveḷip pōṭṭikaḷi naṭattum muṟikaḷ puṟaveḷip pōṭṭikaḷi nām mūṉṟu vakiyil taṭattalām. 1. payirci pōṭṭikaḷ (Practice matches) 2. oru tuṟip pōṭṭikaḷ (Closed competitions) 3. potu nilip pōṭṭikaḷ (Open competitions)

1. payiṟcip pōṭṭikaḷ

tērnteṭukkappaṭṭa kumuviṉ tērccikkum, tiṟami vaḷarccikkum utava, aṇṭi niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷil uḷḷa kuḻukkaḷi aḻittu, pōṭṭi āṭṭaṅkaḷi āṭac ceytu, paḻakum muṟiyē itu.

itaṟku muṉ kūṭṭiyē pēci, tēti, nēram, iṭam mutaliyavaṟṟik kuṟittukkoṇṭu, tīrmāṉittu naṭattik koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

2. oru tuṟip pōṭṭikaḷ (Closed Competitions). oru kuṟippiṭṭa tuṟiyic cērnta, pirivic cārnta kalvi niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷukkiṭiyē naṭipeṟukiṉṟa pōṭṭikaḷ. (u.m) kallūrikaḷukkiṭiyē (Inter Collegiate) paḷḷikaḷukkiṭiyē (Inter School) maṭṭum naṭattappaṭutal. kallūrikaḷukkiṭiyē paḷḷikaḷukkiṭiyē eṉkiṟapōtu, vēṟu tuṟi cārnta niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷ vantu paṅkēṟka muṭiyātu. atuvum kaṭṭuppaṭuttum poṟuppēṟṟuḷḷa caṅkaṅkaḷ mūlamāka naṭattappaṭum.

oru māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa paḷḷikaḷ aṉittum, anta māvaṭṭappaḷḷikaḷ uyarkalvik kaḻakattiṉ kaṭṭup pāṭṭil irukkiṉṟaṉa. ovvoru paḷḷiyum antak kaḻakattiṉ uṟuppiṉarkaḷ.

ataṉ nirvākakkuḻu, oru talivar, ceyalar poruḷāḷar, tuṇic ceyalar, ceyaṟkuḻu eṉpatāka amintirukkum.

oru māvaṭṭam pala tokutikaḷāka (zones) pirikkappaṭṭu, ovvoru tokutiyic cērnta paḷḷikaḷ, avaṟṟiṟkiṭiyē pōṭṭikaḷi naṭattum. piṟaku, tokuti: veṟṟik kuḻukkaḷiṭiyē pōṭṭikaḷi naṭattum. piṟakē, atu māvaṭṭap pōṭṭiyāka naṭattum.

nēramum, paṇa vacatiyum kiṭittāl, māvaṭṭaṅkaḷukkiṭiyēyum pōṭṭikaḷi naṭattum.

﻿

itupōlavē kallūrikaḷukku iṭiyē, vaṅkikaḷukku iṭiyē, aracu tuṟi niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷukkiṭiyē, toḻilakat tuṟiyiṉarukku iṭiyē pōṭṭikaḷ naṭattappaṭum.

3. potu nili pōṭṭikaḷ (Open Competitions) yār vēṇṭumāṉālum vantu pōṭṭiyil kalantu koḷḷalām eṉpatit tāṉ, potu nilip pōṭṭikaḷ eṉkiṟōm. oru mānila viḷiyāṭṭuk kaḻakam eṉpatu taṉatu viḷiyāṭṭukkāṉa pōṭṭikaḷi naṭattukiṟapōtu, anta mānilattic cērnta entak kuḻuvum vantu pōṭṭiyiṭalām.

ituvē potu nilip pōṭṭiyākum,





﻿15 ōṭukaḷap pōṭṭikaḷ





(SPORTS MEET)



* * *



ōṭukaḷap pōṭṭi nikaḻccikaḷi, taṉittiṟaṉ pōṭṭikaḷ eṉṟum, viḷiyāṭṭup pōṭṭikaḷ eṉṟum kūṟuvārkaḷ. nām ōṭukaḷap pōṭṭikaḷ eṉṟē kūṟuvōm.

intap pōṭṭikaḷ iraṇṭu vakippaṭum.

1. taramāṉa pōṭṭikaḷ (Standard Meet)

2. taramaṟṟa pōṭṭikaḷ (Non-standard Meet)



taramāṉa pōṭṭikaḷ eṉpatu, akila ulaka ameccūr kaḻakam amittut tantuḷḷa vitikaḷiṉpaṭiyē naṭipeṟuvatākum.

taramaṟṟa pōṭṭikaḷ eṉpatu, antanta vaṭṭārattiṉarukku ēṟpa, vacatikkēṟpa, iṭattiṟkēṟpa amittuk koḷvatākum.

1. taramāṉa pōṭṭikaḷ

ōṭukaḷap pōṭṭikaḷi naṭattukiṟa viti muṟikaḷ

oṭukaḷap pōṭṭikaḷi naṭattuvataṟku, mikavum tērcciyāṉa tiṭṭamum, tayārippum tēvi. ārvamum aṉupavamum niṟinta palariṉ ottuḻippum tēvi.

tiṭṭam tayārittal : tērnta āṭkaḷi paṇiyil iyakkutal pōṉṟa ceyal muṟikaḷi, iṅkē tokuttuk kāṇpōm. ātalāl, nām itaṉi 3 vakiyākap pakuttup pārppōm.

1. pōṭṭikaḷukku muṉpuḷḷa paṇikaḷ (Pre-meetwork)

2. pōṭṭikaḷiṉpōtu ceyya vēṇṭiya vēlikaḷ (Meet work)

3. pōṭṭikkup piṟaku mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya Gauss, so 5 sir (Post meet work)

1. pōṭṭikaḷukku muṉpuḷḷa paṇikaḷ

ōṭukaḷap pōṭṭikaḷi naṭattuvataṟkeṉṟu, nirvākakkuḻu niccayamākat tēvippaṭukiṟatu. (Organising Committee).

talimiyiṭamākak tikaḻum nirvākakkuḻu, pala tuṇi ceyaṟkuḻukkaḷi (Sub-Committee) amikka vēṇṭum.

tuṇik kuḻukkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum kaṭamikaḷum 1. 1. viḷamparak kuḻu (Publicity Committee)

pōṭṭikaḷ naṭipeṟum tēti, iṭam, pōṭṭi nikaḻccikaḷ pōṉṟa mukkiya kuṟippukkaḷi intak kuḻu viḷamparappaṭuttum pattirikki, rēṭiyō, pōṭṭi niral cīṭṭukaḷ mūlamum itu ellōrukkum aṟivikkiṉṟa paṇiyi, ēṟṟuk koḷkiṟatu.

3.2. ōṭukaḷattari maṟṟum utavi cātaṉak kuḻu Committee for the Grounds and equipments:

pōṭṭikaḷukkāṉa mitāṉattik kēṭṭu, aṉumati vāṅki nikaḻccikaḷ naṭattum iṭaṅkaḷil kuṟiyīṭukaḷi ﻿amittu, vēṇṭiya viḷiyāṭṭu utavip poruṭkaḷi cērttu, cumukamāka taṭiyiṉṟi pōṭṭikaḷ naṭattum ceyalkaḷi mēṟkoḷkiṟatu.

3.3. atikārikaḷi niyamikkum kuḻu (Committee for the officials)

pōṭṭikaḷi naṭattum tiṟami peṟṟirukkum, atikārikaḷukku eḻuti, avarkaḷatu opputalippeṟṟuk koṇṭu, atikārikaḷ aṭaṅkiya paṭṭiyaliyum intak kuḻu tayārikka vēṇṭum.

nirvākak kuḻuviṉ aṉumatiyuṭaṉ, talimi atikāriyiyum niyamikka vēṇṭum.

3.1 irukkikaḷ, taṅkum vacatikaḷ amittuttarum kuḻu. (Committee for accommodation and seating arrangements)

veḷiyūriliruntu varukiṟa pōṭṭiyāḷarkaḷ, atikārikaḷ aṉivarukkum taṅkum vacatikaḷi ceytu tarutal; viḷiyāṭṭup pōṭṭi viḻā naṭipeṟum pantal, uṭkārum irukki vacatikaḷ; pōṭṭiyāḷarkaḷ taṅki oyvukoḷḷum iṭaṅkaḷ, pārviyāḷarkaḷ, viruntiṉarkaḷ amarntirukkum iṭavacatikaḷ; kārkaḷ maṟṟum vākaṉaṅkaḷ niṟka vacatikaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟi, intakkuḻu ceytu taravēṇṭum.

3.5 varavēṟpuk kuḻu (Committee for reception)

pōṭṭikaḷ naṭipeṟukiṉṟapōtu, aḻikkappaṭṭirukkum ciṟappu viruntiṉarkaḷ, mukkiya piramukarkaḷi varavēṟṟu, avarkaḷukkeṉṟu otukkappaṭṭirukkum irukkikaḷil amarttum paṇiyik kikkoḷḷum kuḻuvāka itu amintirukkiṟatu.



3-6. ōṭukaḷattiṉ alaṅkāram maṟṟum vipavakkuḻu.

kaṇkaḷukku kavarcciyākavum pacumiyākavum pantaya mitāṉam tōṟṟam taruvatu pōṉṟu alaṅkāram ceytal, pantayat toṭakkaviḻā, veṟṟip paricaḷippu viḻā, iṟuti viḻā pōṉṟavi ciṟappāka naṭipeṟuvataṟkāka, kāriyamāṟṟutal, vekumatikaḷ veṟṟik kōppikaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟiṟku ēṟpāṭu ceyyum poṟuppum, intak kuḻuvic cārntatēyākum.

3 7. ciṟṟuṇṭi, maṟṟum kali nikaḻcci amippukkuḻu. (Committee for Refreshment and entertinment)

pōṭṭiyāḷarkaḷ, atikārikaḷ , ciṟappu viruntiṉarkaḷ, mukkiya piramukarkaḷ pōṉṟōrukku avvappōtu parukapāṉam, ciṟṟuṇṭiyum vaḻaṅkutal. pōṭṭi niṟi vuṟukiṟa nērattil paṭṭu āṭṭam pōṉṟa kali nikaḻccikaḷi ēṟpāṭu ceyvatum intak kuḻuviṉ poṟuppāka amikiṟatu.

38. peyarppativu, pōṭṭi niral tayārikkum kuḻu. (Committee for Entries and Programmes)

intak kuḻuvāṉatu, nuḻivu maṉuvi, muṉkūṭṭiyē pōṭṭiyāḷarkaḷukku aṉuppi, avarkaḷiṉ opputali nērattil peṟṟu, pativu ceytu avarkaḷukkāṉa oṭṭa eṇkaḷ (Numbers) otukki, avaṟṟiṟkāṉa pativēṭṭil eḻuti nirappi, kuṟippiṭṭa nikaḻccikaḷ kuṟippiṭṭa nērattil, taṭiyiṉṟi naṭakka ēṟpāṭu ceykiṟa poṟuppi ēṟṟirukkiṟatu.

ittakiya poṟuppukkaḷi, kīḻkkāṇum muṟikaḷil ceyyalām nikaḻcci niral (Programmes) puttakattil.

1. mēl aṭṭiyil pōṭ kaṭattum niṟuvaṉattiṉ peyar, viḻāvukku varukiṟa muṟiyē viruntiṉariṉ peyar pōṭṭikaḷ naṭipeṟum iṭam, nāḷ, nēram mutaliya vivaraṅkaḷik kuṟikkavēṇṭum.

2. palvēṟupaṭṭa kuḻukkaḷ, tuṇik kuḻukkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiyum, kuḻukkaḷil iṭam peṟṟiruppōriṉ peyarkaḷiyum kuṟittiṭa vēṇṭum.

3. pōṭṭikaḷi naṭattukiṟa atikārikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ, avarkaḷ poṟuppēṟṟirukkum tuṟi paṟṟiyum kuṟittiṭa vēṇṭum.

4. pōṭṭiyil paṅkēṟkiṟa kuḻukkaḷi (Clubs) akaravaricippaṭutti, pōṭṭiyāḷarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷiyum tirappiṭa vēṇṭum.

5. pōṭṭi nikaḻccikaḷ variciyi (order of events). kuṟittu, eṉṉeṉṉa nikaḻccikaḷ, ententa nērattil naṭipeṟum eṉpatik kuṟittuk kāṭṭutal.

6. ovvoru pōṭṭi nikaḻcciyilum ēṟpaṭuttam paṭṭirukkum muntiya cātaṉikaḷ (Records).

7. muḻumiyāka nirappu taṟkuriya veṟṟi eṇ kuṟippēṭu.

pōṭṭi kikaḻccikaḷ variciyi tayārikkum muṟi

1. oṭṭap pōṭṭikaḷ eṉṟāl, taṭitāṇṭum pōṭṭi, virivōṭṭam, neṭuntura ōṭṭaṅkaḷ, toṭarōṭṭaṅkaḷ eṉṟu varicippaṭutti, tayārikka vēṇṭum.

2. oṭṭappōṭṭikaḷ naṭipeṟṟuk koṇṭirukkum. nērattilēyē, kaḷappōṭṭikaḷum aṭuttaṭuttu naṭattiṭa vēṇṭum.

3. virivōṭṭakkārarkaḷ ellōrum, tāṇṭum pōṭṭikaḷil paṅku peṟukiṉṟārkaḷ eṉpatāl irumpukkuṇṭu eṟiyāḷarkaḷ, taṭṭeṟital, caṅkilik kuṇṭu vīcutal pōṉṟa nikaḻccikaḷil, paṅkupeṟukiṉṟārkaḷ eṉpatāl, ippaṭippaṭṭa nikaḻccikaḷi orē camayattiv nikaḻccikaḷ variciyil cērkkāmal, nēram koṭuttu varicip paṭutta vēṇṭum.

4. kōlūṉṟittāṇṭum pōṭṭi, uyaram tāṇṭutal, taṭṭeṟital, vēleṟital pōṉṟa nikaḻccikaḷ naṭantu muṭiya atika nēram piṭikkum eṉpatāl, ittakiya nikaḻccikaḷi, muṉkūṭṭiyē ārampittu naṭattappaṭuvatu pōla, nikaḻcci niralil cērttuka koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

2. pōṭṭi naṭipeṟum pōtuḷḷa vēlikaḷ (Meet Work)

1. pōṭṭiyil paṅku peṟukiṉṟa pōṭṭiyāḷarkaḷ : naṭattukiṉṟa atikārikaḷ aṉivarum, pōṭṭi toṭaṅkukiṉṟa nērattiṟku caṟṟu muṉṉatākavē vanta viṭa vēṇṭum.

2. atikārikaḷukkuriya aṭiyāḷa aṭṭikaḷ, pōṭṭiyik kāṭṭum nikaḻcci niral piratikaḷ, kuṟippiṭṭa nikaḻccikkāṉa kuṟippēṭukaḷ, aṉittiyum koṭuttiṭa vēṇṭum.

3. pōṭṭiyāḷarkaḷukkuriya eṇkaḷ, (Numbers) : nikaḻcci niral piratikaḷ, maṟṟum avarkaḷukkuriya vaḻikāṭṭum aṟivurik kuṟippukaḷ aṉittiyum tantu viṭa vēṇṭum.

4. pōṭṭiyāḷarkaḷ aṇivakuppu, uṟuti moḻi eṭuttal pōṉṟavaṟṟuṭaṉ pōṭṭikaḷit toṭaṅkiṭa vēṇṭum.

5. nikaḻcki niral varicikku eṟpa, nikaḻccikaḷit toṭarntu naṭattavēṇṭum.

6. ovvoru pōṭṭiyiṉ iṟutippōṭṭi muṭintatum, veṟṟi mēṭi, paricaḷippu nikaḻcciyi uṭaṉē naṭattiṭa vēṇṭum.

7. pōṭṭinikaḻccikaḷ ellām muṭintapiṟaku, ellā pōṭṭiyāḷarkaḷum, viḷiyāṭṭup pōṭṭiyiṉ muṭivu viḻāvukkākak kuṟippiṭṭirukkum iṭattil vantu kūṭiṭa vēṇṭum.

8. muṭivu viḻāvil, veṟṟik kēṭayaṅkaḷ, niṉivup paricukaḷ, cāṉṟitaḻkaḷ mutaliyavi vaḻaṅkappaṭum.

9. piṟaku, nāṭṭuppaṇ pāṭa viḻā iṉitē muṭivu peṟum.

3. pōṭṭikkup piṟakuḷḷa vēlikaḷ 1. viḷiyāṭṭup pōṭṭikaḷ muṭinta piṟaku, kaṇakku vaḻakkic cariceytu viṭa vēṇṭum. maṟṟavarkaḷiṭamiruntu kimāṟṟāka vāṅki vantap poruṭkaḷik koṇṭu pōy koṭukka vēṇṭum.

2. pōṭṭikaḷukku utavi ceyta aṉivarukrum, naṉṟi terivittuk kaṭitam eḻuta vēṇṭum. nēril colvatum nallatutāṉ.

11 taramaṟṟa pōṭṭi nikaḻccikaḷ ulaka vitikaḷukku uṭpaṭāmal, vacatikkēṟpa naṭattukiṉṟa pōṭṭikaḷit tāṉ, taramaṟṟa viḷiyāṭṭup pōṭṭikaḷ eṉkiṟōm.

ulaka vitimuṟikaḷip piṉpaṟṟāmal, naṭimuṟikku utavumāṟu pala putiya vitikaḷi ēṟpaṭuttik koṇṭu, atika eṇṇikkiyil (māṇavarkaḷi) pōṭṭiyāḷar kaḷip paṅku peṟac ceyyum utti, inta viḷiyāṭṭup pōṭṭikaḷil, kaṭipiṭikkappaṭukiṟatu.

irukkiṉṟa koñca vacatikaḷē, intap pōṭṭikku pōtumāṉatu. itil veṟṟi peṟukiṟavarkaḷiṉ cātaṉikku avvaḷavu peṟumitamilli, eṉṟālum, kuḻukkaḷukkup perumi pōyc cērukiṟatu. avvaḷavu tāṉ.

intap pōṭṭiyil iṭampeṟum cila pirivukaḷ.

1. kuḻukkaḷiṉ kūṭṭup pōṭṭi (Team or mass Athletics)

2. calukip pōṭṭikaḷ (Handicap sports)

3. takaval vaḻi pōṭṭikaḷ (Telegraphic sports)

4. tiṟamaṟiyum pōṭṭikaḷ (Tabloid sports)

kuḻukkaḷum kūṭṭuppōṭṭiyum

atika eṇṇikkiyil māṇavarkaḷ paṅku peṟukiṟa pōtu, oṭukaḷap pōṭṭikaḷi tiṭṭamiṭṭu naṭatta vēṇṭum.

1. māṇavarkaḷi ciṟu ciṟu kuḻukkaḷākap pirikka vēṇṭum. oru kuḻu eṉṟāl 20 mutal 25 irukkalām.

2. cila pōṭṭi nikaḻccikaḷil avarkaḷip pōṭṭi yiṭac ceyyalām.

ōṭṭa nikaḻccikaḷ (a) oru kuḻuvil uḷḷavarkaḷ ovvoruvarum oru vaṭṭam (lap) cuṟṟi ōṭi vara, oruvar piṉ oruvarākac ceytal. itil entakkuḻu kuṟinta nērattil ōṭi muṭikkiṟatō, atuvē veṉṟatākum. itaṟku toṭar muṟi (Follow on method) eṉṟu peyar.

ā) toṭarōṭṭa (Relay) pōla, etiretirē māṇavarkaḷip pirittu niṟkac ceytu (100 mīṭṭar tāram). taṟi ōṭṭam pōla. (Shuttle Relay) oṭiṭac ceytal. itaṟku taṟimuṟi (Shuttle method) eṉṟu peyar.

i) ovvoru māṇavaṉum kuṟippiṭṭa tūratti (85 mīṭṭar tūram) oṭac ceytu, entak kuḻu kuṟinta nērattil mottattil, oṭi muṭikkiṟatō, antak kuḻu veṉṟatāka aṟivittu, ataṟkāṉa veṟṟi eṇkaḷi aḷikka vēṇṭum. (5, 3, 2.) eṉpatu pōla) itaṟku kuḻup pōṭṭi muṟi (Zonal method) eṉṟu peyar.

kaḷa nikaḻccikaḷ nīḷam tāṇṭutal, taṭṭeṟital, irumpukkuṇṭu pōṭutal pōṉṟa nikaḻccikaḷil, kuḻukkaḷip paṅku peṟac ceytu, atika tūram tāṇṭukiṟa, atika tūram eṟikiṟa kuḻukkaḷukku, veṟṟip pariciṉi aḷikkalām.

kaḷap pōṭṭikaḷum itē pōla, toṭar muṟi, taṟi muṟi, kuḻup pōṭṭi muṟi mūṉṟiyum kaṭip piṭikkalām.

2. tiṟaṉ kuṟintōrkku, calukip pōṭṭikaḷ tiṟaṉ kuṟintōriyum, caluki muṟiyil vāyppaḷittu, camamākap pōṭṭiyiṭukiṉṟa muṟi, ippōṭṭikaḷil piṉpaṟṟappaṭukiṟatu.

(u. m) 100 mīṭṭar oṭṭap pōṭṭi eṉṟāl, tiṟaṉ kuṟintavarkaḷi, 10 aṭi allatu 20 aṭi tūram muṉṉē niṟka vittu, oṭac ceytu, pōṭṭiyiṭa vippatākum.

tiṟaṉ kuṟintavarkku oru vāyppu tarukiṟa calukip pōṭṭiyēyākum.

3. takaval vaḻip pōṭṭikaḷ pala iṭaṅkaḷil, ūrkaḷil pōṭṭikaḷi naṭatti, anta muṭivukaḷi tanti, ṭelipōṉ mūlamākat takaval tara vēṇṭum; anta cātaṉikaḷukkēṟpa, muṭivukaḷ aṟivikkappaṭum.

itil oru kuṟi eṉṉaveṉil, pala iṭaṅkaḷil pōṭṭikaḷ eṉkiṟa pōtu, antanta iṭam cūḻnili kāraṇamāka, cātaṉikaḷ vēṟupaṭum, tiṟamikaḷi veḷippaṭutta muṭiyāta taṉmiyum amiyum eṉpatu maṟṟapaṭi, inta muṟip pōṭṭiyiṉāl nēram vīṇākāmal, paṇac celavu atikamākāmal miccam piṭikkalām. kāppāṟṟalām.

4. tiṟaṉaṟiyum pōṭṭikaḷ (Tabloid Sports) inta muṟip pōṭṭikaḷil kalantu koḷpavarkkāka, mutalilēyē, cātaṉi nērattiyum tūrattiyum (Time and distance) tīrmāṉamākak kuṟittu vittu viṭa vēṇṭum.

taram I taram II taram III

(u.m)

100 mīṭṭar tūram 12.5 viṉāṭi 13 viṉāṭi 14 viṉāṭi

nīḷam tāṇṭal 15 aṭi 14 aṭi 13 aṭi

irumpukkuṇṭu eṟital (12) 30 aṭi 28 36

﻿ ovvoru tarattiṟkum ovvoru vaṇṇa rippaṉ eṉṟu terivittiṭa vēṇṭum.

kuṟippiṭṭa oru nērattil oṭi muṭippavarkku: turattil tāṇṭupavarkkuk, kuṟippiṭṭa rippaṉik koṭuttu, keḷaravittiṭa vēṇṭum.

mēlē kuṟippiṭṭa 3 nikaḻccikaḷ, 3 taraṅkaḷ. itil ovvoru māṇavaṉum pōṭṭiyiṭṭu muyaṟcikkiṟāṉ. mūṉṟu nikaḻcciyilum kuṟippiṭṭa cātaṉiyik kaṭantāl tāṉ, rippaṉ tarappaṭum. oṉṟil kaṭakka muṭiyāmaṟ pōṉālum, rippaṉ kiṭikkātu.

atika eṇṇikkiyil rippaṉ peṟṟa (ovvoru pēpaṉukkum veṟṟi eṇ evvaḷavu eṉṟu kuṟit tirukka vēṇṭum.) kuḻukkaḷē, veṟṟikkuḻu eṉṟu aṟivikkappaṭum.

ivvāṟu atika eṇṇikkiyil māṇavarkaḷip paṅkupeṟac ceykiṟa pōtu : māṇavarkaḷi ciṟuvar, iḷiyōr mūttavar eṉṟu pirittal; kuṟippiṭṭa pōṭṭikaḷil maṭṭum māṇavarkaḷip pōṭṭiyiṭa vittal; tiṟamuḷḷa māṇavarkaḷi cērkkāmal iruttal; calukippōṭṭi muṟiyip payaṉpaṭuttutal; ellōrum kaṭṭāyamākap pōṭṭiyil kalantu koḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉṟu āṇiyiṭutal; māṟuvēṭap pōṭṭi, pōṉṟavaṟṟi aṉumatittāl pōṉṟavaṟṟilum āciriyarkaḷ tīvira kavaṉam celutta vēṇṭum.





﻿16. viḷiyāṭṭu nāḷ viḻā





﻿(PLAY DAY)





* * *



paḷḷikaḷukkiṭiyē naṭipeṟukiṟa pōṭṭikaḷukkup patilāka, peṇkaḷukkeṉṟu, paḷḷikaḷukkuḷḷēyē naṭattappaṭṭa nikaḻccikaḷē viḷiyāṭṭu nāḷ eṉṟa peyarip peṟṟiruntaṉa. atuvē māṟi pakkam pakkamāka uḷḷa paḷḷikaḷ ellām vantu, kalantukoṇṭu, kaḷippaṭikiṟa mārkkattiṟkākap piṉṉar, māṟṟam ceyyappaṭṭatu.

pala paḷḷikaḷ vantu kalantukoṇṭālum, itu pōṭṭikaṉ pōla naṭattappaṭuvatu alla.

kūṭi viḷiyāṭuvatu tāṉ. etirttu viḷiyāṭuvatu alla.

viḷiyāṭṭu nāḷiṉ kuṟikkōḷāṉatu, ovvoru māṇavaṉum ētāvatu oru viḷiyāṭṭil avaciyam paṅku peṟṟāka vēṇṭum eṉpatu tāṉ.

viḷiyāṭṭu nāḷ viḻāviṉ payaṉum perumiyum

1. palatarappaṭṭa makiḻcci tarum viḷiyāṭṭukkavil, peruvāriyāṉa eṇṇikkiyil māṇava māṇavikaḷ kalantu koṇṭu, pēriṉpam peṟa utavukiṉṟatu.

2. camūkattoṭarpu koḷḷum vāyppiyum vaḻaṅkukiṟatu.

3. talimip paṇpi vaḷarttukkoḷḷum cantarp paṅkaḷi aḷikkiṟatu. 4. puṟaveḷip pōṭṭikaḷil ēṟpaṭukiṟa kuṟikaḷik kaḷintu, kutūkalam peṟum nalaṉi ēṟpaṭuttit tarukiṟatu.

viḷiyāṭṭu nāḷ viḻāvi naṭattum muṟikaḷ

1. paḷḷik kuḻantikaḷukkākavē naṭattappaṭukiṟa nikaḻcciyātalāl, itaṉi oru nāḷilō allatu nāḷ pakutikkuḷḷō naṭattalām.

2. nikaḻccikaḷi naṭatta, pala kuḻukkaḷi amittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum (u. m.) viḷamparkkuḻu : nikaḻcci niral kuḻu varavēṟpuk kuḻu vacatikkuḻu : viḷiyāṭṭu poruṭkaḷ kuḻu ; ciṟṟuṇṭikkuḻu ciṟappuk koṇṭāṭṭakkuḻu eṉṟu palavakikkuḻukkaḷ.

3. viḷiyāṭṭu nāḷ viḻā naṭipeṟum nāḷ, iṭam, nēram, piṉpaṟṟa vēṇṭiya nikaḻcci niral pōṉṟa oṟṟi. paḷḷikaḷukku muṉkaṭṭiyē takaval tantu, ovvoru paḷḷiyiliruntum varukiṟa kuḻantikaḷ eṇṇikkiyiyum peṟṟukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum. -

4. varukiṟa kuḻantikaḷ eṇṇikkikkēṟpa taṅkum iṭavacati kaḻivaṟikaḷ vacati : uṇavu vacati, taṇṇir ācati, pōṉṟavaṟ niṟkum ciṟappāṉa ēṟpāṭukaḷic ceytu koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

5. eṉṉeṉṉa nikaḻccikaḷ iṭam peṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpataṟkēṟpa, āṭukaḷaṅkaḷittayār ceytu vittu, ataṟkuriya utavip poruṭkaḷiyum niṟivāka ēṟpāṭu ceytu vittal vēṇṭum.

6. paḷḷikaḷiliruntu varum kuḻantikaḷi, pala kuḻukkaḷākap pirittu, avarkaḷ māṟi māṟic ceṉṟu viḷiyāṭṭil paṅkupeṟum vaḻi muṟikaḷiyum, avarkaḷukkut teḷivāka viḷakkutal vēṇṭum.

7. ellā kuḻukkaḷukkum camavāyppu, niṟivāṉa vāyppukaḷ kiṭikkumpaṭi, kavaṉamākavum poṟuppākavum pārttuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

8. nikaḻcci niralil, ciṟuciṟu viḷiyāṭṭukaḷa, kōpurap payiṟcikaḷ lecim pōṉṟa tāḷalayap payiṟcikaḷ, cākacac ceyalkaḷ pōṉṟa makiḻccitarum ceyalkaḷ iṭampeṟukiṟa muṟiyil irukkavēṇṭum.

9. nikaḻccikaḷukkiṭiyē pāṉaṅkaḷ vaḻaṅkutal nērattiṟku uṇavu koṭuttal pōṉṟavaṟṟiyum cariyākat tarutal vēṇṭum.

10. nikaḻccikaḷ ellām naṭipeṟṟa piṟaku ellōriyum oriṭattil kūṭacceytu, avarkaḷ taṅkaḷatu tiṟamikaḷik kāṭṭukiṟa cantarppam aḷikkavēṇṭum (āṭal, pāṭal, oppaṉi, māṟuvēṭappōṭṭi, ciṟu ciṟu nāṭakam pōṉṟavi).

11. ovvoru paḷḷiyum tamatu kuḻantikaḷ mūlam ali nikaḻcciyi aḷikkavum vāyppukkaḷ taralām.

12. ippaṭi ceyalviḷakkam, ciṟappu nikaḻccikaḷ iṭam peṟṟa piṟaku, muṭivu viḻā oṉṟiṟkum ēṟpāṭu ceytal vēṇṭum.

13. viḻāvukkut talimi ēṟka oruvar, varavēṟpuri, aṟikki vācittup paricukaḷ vaḻaṅkutal, naṉṟi. navilal, nāṭṭuppaṇ ippaṭiyāka muṭivu viḻā naṭipeṟa ēṟpāṭukaḷ ceytal vēṇṭum.

14. ellā nikaḻccikaḷum kuṟippiṭṭa kālattiṟkuḷ muṭivaṭintu iruṭṭuvataṟku muṉ kuḻantikaḷ vīṭu pōyc cērvatu pōla, viḷiyāṭṭu nāḷ viḻāvi niṟivu ceytal vēṇṭum.

vacatikkāka, viḷiyāṭṭu nāḷ viḻā oṉṟiṉ nikaḻcci niralpaṟṟi, kīḻē koṭuttirukkiṟōm.

viḷiyāṭṭu nāḷ viḻā nikaḻcci kiral (mātiri)



nāḷ : 18-8-1989.

nēram : māli 2 maṇi mutal 6 maṇi vari.

iṭam : oy. em. ci. ē. uṭaṟkalvik kallūri.

nikaḻcci :

1. paḷḷik kuḻantikaḷiṉ kuḻu kūṭutalum, aṇi vakuppum.

2. koṭiyēṟṟutal-tiṟappu viḻā.

3. viḻā nāḷ paṟṟiya viḷakkamum kuṟippum.

4. viḷiyāṭṭu nikaḻccikaḷ.





(a) tāḷalayac ceyalkaḷ.



(ā) cākacac ceyalkaḷ.



(i) ciṟu viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷ.



(ī) pāṭṭum katiyum iṇinta ceyalkaḷ.



5. iṭivēḷi-ciṟṟuṇṭi nēram.

6. ovvoru paḷḷiyum paṅkupeṟum nikaḻccikaḷ ceyal viḷakkam, ciṟappup payiṟcikaḷ.

7. mēṭi muṉ kūṭutal : māṇavarkaḷiṉ kali nikaḻccikaḷ.

8. ciṟappuk kūṭṭam (muṭivu viḻā).



(a) varavēṟpuri.



(ā) viḻā aṟikki vācittal.



(i) talivar ciṟappuri.



(ī) paricukaḷ vaḻaṅkutal.



(u) naṉṟi uri.



9. nāṭṭup paṇ.





17. ceyal māṭciyum kaṇkāṭciyum





﻿ (DEMONSTRATIONS AND EXIBITION)





* * *



paḷḷiyil naṭipeṟukiṉṟa mukkiya nikaḻccikaḷ allatu viḻākkaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟil naṭattappeṟukiṉṟa uṭal payiṟcikaḷi ceytu kāṭṭukiṉṟa māṭciyiyē ceyal: māṭci eṉṟu aḻikkiṟōm.

ceyal māṭciyiṉ ciṟappu nōkkaṅkaḷ

1. māṇavarkaḷukku ciṟappāṉa muṟiyil, palvēṟu vakiyāṉa uṭaṟ payiṟcikaḷip payiṟṟuvikkum vāyppu: kiṭikkiṟatu.

2. uṭaṟ kalviyil māṇavarkaḷukku uṇmiyāṉa viruppamum, uṟutiyāṉa ārvamum ēṟpaṭuttum, vaḻivaki kiṭikkiṟatu.

3. cemmiyāka ceyalpaṭavum, taṅkaḷ tiṟamikaḷi veḷippaṭutti maṟṟavarkaḷi makiḻvittu, tāṅkaḷum makiḻntuviṭukiṟa vāyppu, uṭaṟkalvi āciriyarkaḷukkum kiṭikkiṟatu.

4. paḷḷi nikaḻccikkup pārviyāḷarkaḷāka varuāai tarukiṉṟa peṟṟōrkaḷ, potumakkaḷukku, uṭaṟkalviyippaṟṟi terintu koḷḷavum, purintu koḷḷavum kūṭiya uṉṉata cūḻniliyi uṇṭākkit tarukiṟatu.

paḷḷiyil varum mukkiya nikaḻccikaḷ

1. tiruviḻā nāṭkaḷ.

2. paḷḷi niṟuvaṉar viḻā.

3. peṟṟōrkaḷ viḻā.

4. ciṟappu viruntiṉar varuki viḻā.

5. paḷḷi āṇṭu viḻā.

6. muṉṉāḷ māṇavar caṅka viḻā.

7. tēciya tiṉa viḻākkaḷ.

8. etir pārāmal varukiṟa mukkiya viḻākkaḷ.



naṭattukiṉṟa muṟikaḷ

1. kūṭṭup payiṟcikaḷukkuriya ceyal māṭcik kāṭcil kāka, muṉkūṭṭiyē tiṭṭamiṭṭuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum, ataṟku ētuvāka, nāḷ, nēram iṭam, kālam pōṉṟa vaṟṟiyum tīrmāṉittu viṭa vēṇṭum.

2. nikaḻcci naṭappati peṟṟōr, potumakkaḷ mutaliyavarkaḷum, muṭinta vaḻikaḷil ellām, viḷamparam ceytiṭa vēṇṭum.

3. eṉṉeṉṉa uṭaṟpayiṟcikaḷik kūṭṭuppayiṟci yil cērkka vēṇṭum eṉpatiyum tiṭṭamiṭṭu, tīrmāṉikka vēṇṭum.

4. ententak kuḻu, eṉṉeṉṉap payiṟcikaḷ ceyya vēṇṭum eṉpatiyum cariyāka, teḷivāka, uṟuti ceyya vēṇṭum.

5. taṉittaṉiyāka ovvoru kuḻuviyum, ataṟkuriya kayiṟcikaḷi aḷittu, piṟaku, ellā kuḻukkaḷiyum oṉṟu cērttu ceykiṟa ceyal māṭcikku, palamuṟi ottikikaḷ tēvi.

6. enta iṭattil, ceyal māṭci nikaḻcci naṭi peṟumō, anta iṭattilēyē ottiki pārppatum illatu.

7. viḻāvukkāṉa ciṟappu viruntiṉariyum mu ṉ kūṭṭiyē teḷivu ceytu, aḻippitaḻukkum ēṟpāṭu ceyya vēṇṭum.

8. nuḻivu vāyil, veḷiyē cellum vaḻi pōṉṟa vaṟṟi amittu, pārviyāḷarkaḷ vacatiyāka vantu, iruntu, pōka ēṟpāṭu ceyvatu nallatu.

ceyalmāṭci naṭipeṟum nāḷukku muṉṉatāka, mēlē kūṟiya vivaraṅkaḷik kaṭippiṭitta piṟaku, viḻā nikaḻccikkāka atē nāḷil ceyya vēṇṭiya viṣayaṅkaḷ.

1. pārviyāḷarkaḷukkāka irukkikaḷ. mēṭi alaṅkāraṅkaḷ.

2. nikaḻcci naṭipeṟum iṭattil kuṟiyīṭukaḷ.

3. nikaḻccikkut tēviyāṉa utavi cātaṉaṅkaḷit tayārāka cēkarittu vittiruttal.

4. paṅkēṟkum māṇavarkaḷukkuriya cīruṭikaḷ.

5. paṅkēṟkum māṇavarkaḷ taṅki, uṭiyi cīci ceyya iṭa vacatikaḷ.

6. oli, oḷi amippu.

7. kuṭi nīr vacati, kaḻivaṟi vacatikaḷ.

8. mutalutavi vacatikaḷ.

iviyellām, mukkiyamāka kavaṉikkappaṭavēṇṭiya viṣayaṅkaḷākum.

ceyalmāṭcikkuriya nikaḻcci niralit tayāritta piṟaku, avaṟṟip paṟṟiya virivuri viḷakkamum taral vēṇṭum.

ovvoru nikaḻcci paṟṟiya virivuri viḷakkamāṉatu. oru nikaḻcciyit toṭarntu maṟṟoru nikaḻcciyit toṭarntu koḷḷavum, pārviyāḷarkaḷukku nikaḻcci paṟṟi aṟintu koḷḷavum utavi ceykiṟatu.

ceyal māṭci nikaḻcciyi kuṟitta nērattiṟkuḷ, (6.5kima uṅkaḷukkuḷ) muṭittuk koḷḷavēṇṭum. atika nēram eṭuttuk koḷvatu, pārviyāḷarkaḷukku ericcali ēṟpaṭuttiṉālum ēṟpaṭuttum.

tēciya kītam icittu, viḻāvi muṭittiṭa vēṇṭum.

ceyal māṭcikkuriya ceyal muṟikaḷ

1. cīruṭaṟpayircikaḷ (Calisthenics)

2. utavi cātaṉaṅkaḷuṭaṉ ceykiṟa uṭalpayiṟcikaḷ.

3. aṇi naṭippayiṟcikaḷ

4. tāḷalayap payiṟcikaḷ (immi, kōlāṭṭam pōṉṟavi)

5. intiya nāṭṭut tēkappayiṟcikaḷ (taṇṭāl, paski)

6. kuṭṭikkaraṇap payiṟcikaḷ.

7. jimṉāsṭiks payiṟcikaḷ.

8. taṟkāppukkalip payiṟcikaḷ.

9. oṭukaḷappōṭṭikaḷ, viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷ paṟṟi cittarikkum cilikkāṭci amippu (Tableaux) kaṇkāṭci.

aṟivu viḷakkamum, ārvamum aḷikkak kūṭiya vakiyil, kaṇkāṭciyi amikka vēṇṭum.

viḷiyāṭṭu, uṭal nalam, uṭal payiṟcikaḷ pōṉṟa talippukkaḷil, paṭaṅkaḷ, oviyaṅkaḷ, mātiriccīlikaḷ, kalaroṭṭikaḷ mūlamāka kaṇkāṭciyi amikkalām.

pārviyāḷarkaḷukkut teḷivāka viḷakka, māṇavarkaḷi payaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷalām.

kaṇkāṭcik keṉṟu taṉi iṭam vēṇṭum. vantu pōkum vaḻikaḷum tārāḷamāka irukkavēṇṭum.

kaṇkāṭciyi naṭatta, poṟuppuḷḷa naṭuvari talimiyēṟkac ceytu, avar talimiyiṉ kīḻ, oru kuḻuvi amittu viṭukiṟapōtu, kaṇkāṭci ciṟappu mikkatāka, payaṉaḷippatāka amintu, perumi cērkkum.





﻿18. viḷiyāṭṭuc cuṟṟulā





(GAMES TOURS)





* * *



paḷḷikaḷil allatu kallūrikaḷil tāṉ, māṇavar kaḷukkāṉa viḷiyāṭṭuc cuṟṟulāvi, ēṟpāṭu ceytu, naṭattiṭa muṭiyum.

cuṟṟulāvukkāṉa paṇac celavu maṟṟum viṭumuṟi nāṭkaḷ paṟṟi, muṉkūṭṭiyē tiṭṭamiṭṭu naṭattukiṟa pōtu tāṉ, cuṟṟulā cukamākavum, cumukamākavum amiyum.

cuṟṟulā veṟṟikku, cila kuṟippukkaḷ

1. cuṟṟulāvukku enta iṭattiṟkup pōkiṟōm enta nāḷil puṟappaṭukiṟōm ? evvaḷavu nāṭkaḷ evvaḷavu paṇam celavākum eṉṟellām mutalil tiṭṭamiṭal vēṇṭum.

2. celavu muḻuvatiyum paḷḷi ēṟṟuk koḷkiṟatā ? pakuti celavi ēṟkiṟatā ? māṇavarkaḷē muḻumiyāka ēṟṟuk koḷkiṟārkaḷā eṉpatiyellām, muṉṉatākavē, tiṭṭamiṭṭut tīrmāṉikka vēṇṭum.



8. pas allatu rayil mūlam payaṇam ceyvatu eṉṟāl, ataṟkuriya calukik kaṭṭaṇam pōṉṟa vaṟṟiṟku maṉu ceytu, ēṟpāṭu ceytuviṭal vēṇṭum.

4. cuṟṟulā cellum iṭaṅkaḷukku, muṉ kūṭṭiyē uriyavarkaḷukku eḻuti, taṅkum vacati, uṇavu mutaliya vaṟṟiṟku ēṟpāṭukaḷi ceytuviṭa vēṇṭum.

5. puṟappaṭum nāḷ, nēram, cellum iṭaṅkaḷ. ovvoru iṭattiliruntum puṟappaṭum nēram. payaṇattiṟ kuriya pas allatu rayil.nēram mutaliyavaṟṟit tayārikkum tikaḻccik kuṟippup paṭṭiyaliyum tayārittu, ataṉ paṭiyē tāmatappaṭuttāmal, oḻuṅkāka naṭattiṭa vēṇṭum.

6. cuṟṟulāvukku paṇam tāṉ mukkiyam. kiyil tēvikku atikamākap paṇam vittiruppatu, putiya iṭaṅkaḷil ēṟpaṭṭu viṭukiṟa etirpārāta tontaravukaḷi camāḷikka utavum.

7. putiya iṭaṅkaḷukkup pōvatāl, paṇattip pattiramāka vittuk koḷvatu putticālittaṉamākum. atupōlavē, celavaḻikkum paṇattiṟkuriya racītukaḷiyum vāṅki vittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum. paṇa viṣayattil mikavum poṟuppu avaciyam.

8. cuṟṟulā nērattil, cukavīṉattiṟku palar āḷā vatu cakajamē. potuvāka, upayōkappaṭuttukiṟa cila mukkiya maruntukaḷiyum, mutalutavi tarukiṟa maruntukaḷiyum, vāṅki vittiruppatu nallatu.

9. cuṟṟulā celpavarkaḷ makiḻcciyāṉa cūḻniliyil iruppatu iyaṟkitāṉ. iruntālum avarkaḷ elli mīṟi naṭantu koḷḷāmal, oḻukkattuṭaṉum kaṭṭuppāṭṭuṭaṉum irukka, cuṟṟulā poṟuppēṟṟiruk kum āciriyar, ciramattuṭaṉ ceyalpaṭa vēṇṭiyirukkum.

eṉavē, makiḻcciyākavum, maṉōkaramākavum cuṟṟulā amiya, āciriyarkaḷ māṇavarkaḷ kūṭṭu ottuḻippu avaciyamākum.

ivi ellām tiṭṭamiṭṭu, tīrmāṉittu celkiṟa pōtu, tēvikku mēlē ṇavacati irukkiṟapōtu, ciṟappāka amiyum. atuvum poṟuppuṇarntu naṭantu koṉkiṟapōtu, pēriṉpamākavum amintuviṭum eṉpati uṟutiyākak kūṟalām.





﻿19. māṇavarkaḷiṉ iṉappirivum, paricum pārāṭṭum





(CLASSIFICATIONS & AWARDS)





* * *



iṉappirivu eṉpatu māṇavarkaḷi taram vāriyākap pirippatākum. vayatu, uṭal aḷavi (Size) amippu, viḷiyāṭṭut tiṟami mutaliyavaṟṟi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu, māṇavarkaḷik kuḻu kuḻuvākap pirippatākum.

ciṟuvar, iḷiyōr, mūttōr (Sub - Junior, Junior Senior, Super senior) eṉappirikkalām.

ivvāṟu pirippataṟku muṉ kavaṉikkavēṇṭiyatu.

1. nalla tēkattuṭaṉ, ellā viḷiyāṭṭilum kalantu koḷḷa muṭikiṟa māṇavarkaḷ.

2. uṭal kuṟiyuṭaṉ oru kuṟippiṭṭa viḷiyāṭ ṭukkaḷil maṭṭumē paṅku peṟa muṭiyum eṉkiṟa māṇavarkaḷ.

3. maruttuva āyviṉ aṭippaṭiyil viḷiyāṭṭil vilakkaḷikkappaṭṭa māṇavarkaḷ.

eṉṟu mutalil, 3 vakiyākap pirittuk koḷḷalām. taramum tiṟamum uḷḷa māṇavarkaḷi iṉavāriyākac pirikka pala muṟikaḷ kiyāḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

mutalmuṟi

vayatu + uyaram - eṭi

3

muṭivaṭinta vayatu, aṅkulattil uyaram, pavuṇṭil eṭi ākiya mūṉṟiyum kūṭṭi, 3-āl vakukkavēṇṭum.

ippaṭi varukiṉṟa eṇṇikkiyiṉ aṭippaṭiyil: nāṉku pirivākap pirikkalām.

pirivu kūṭṭil varum eṇṇikki

A 90kku mēl

B 85liruntu 9 vari

C 75 liruntu 85 vari

D 75kkuk kīḻāka

iraṇṭāvatu muṟi

ṭākṭar C. H. mekḷāyiṉ iṉappirivu muṟi

ivar oṭukaḷap pōṭṭikaḷil paṅku peṟṟa māṇavarkaḷ palari āyvu ceytu, oru muṭivukku vantār.

1. vayatu (Chronological age)

2. uṭaltiṟam (Physiological age)

3. uyaram (Height)

4. eṭi (Weight) inta nāṉkiyum payaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷum vaḻi muṟikaḷi vakuttu, oru cūttiratti (Formulla) aḷittār.

ivar koṭuttuḷḷa kaṇakkuppaṭi, 17 āṇṭukaḷ vari vayatiyum; 14 vayatukkup piṟaku uyarattiyum; ellā vayatu maṭṭaṅkaḷilum uḷḷa eṭiyiyum iṉappirivukkāka eṭuttuk koḷḷalām eṉpatu tāṉ.

(a) kūṭṭu=29 vayatu + 6 uyaram + eṭi

17 āṇṭukaḷukku mēṟpaṭṭa vayatiyum 17 ākavē, koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

(ā) kūṭṭu=6 uyaram + eṭi

27 āṇṭu vayatum, ataṟku mēṟpaṭṭa vayatum uṭiya kallūri māṇavarkaḷukku inta cūttirattip payaṉpaṭuttalām.

(i) kūṭṭu= 10 vayatu + eṭi

inta vāyppāṭṭi toṭakkap paḷḷi māṇavarkaḷukkup payaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷalām. intac ciṟārkaḷukku uyaram tēviyaṟṟatākat tōṉṟukiṟatu.

mūṉṟāvatu muṟi

cūttiram 11/2 uyaram (aṅkulattil) + eṭi (pavuṇṭil).

inta cūttirattiṉpaṭi, varukiṟa motta eṇṇikkiyik koṇṭu. nāṉku pirivākap pirikkalām.

1. mika mūttōr - 200kku mēl

2. mūttōr - 180kku mēl 200kku kīḻ

3. iḷiyōr - 160kkum 180kkum kīḻ

4. ciṟuvar - 180kkum kīḻāka

nākāṉkāvatu muṟi kūṭṭu = vayatu + uyaram + l/10 eṭi

naṭantu muṭinta āṇṭukaḷi vayatākavum aṅkulattil uyarattiyum pavuṇṭil eṭiyiyum eṭuttuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

appaṭi varukiṟa motta eṇṇikkiyik koṇṭu, kīḻvarum kaṇakkil, iṉappirivu ceyyalām.

mika mūttōr = 85 m ataṟku mēlum

mūttōr = 80 mutal 85 vari.

iḷiyōr = 75 mutal 80 vari.

ciṟuvar =70 mutal 75 vari.

ārampa vakuppiṉar =65 mutal 70 vari.

kuḻantikaḷ = 65kkum kīḻāka

utāraṇam

15 āṇṭukaḷ - 15

5 aṭi 2 aṅkulam - 6.2

80 pavuṇṭu eṭi - 8 (80 ÷ 10 = 8)

----

mottam 85

----

85 eṉṟu kūṭṭu varuvatāl, anta māṇavar mika mūttōr pirivil cērkiṟār.

peṇkaḷukkāṉa iṉappirivu

peṇkaḷ vaḷarcciyik koṇṭu, 15 vayatukku mēṟpaṭṭa peṇkaḷiyellām, orē pirivil cērttuk koḷḷalām.

15 vayatukkuṭpaṭṭavarkaḷi pirittu viṭukiṟa muṟi.

1. kūṭṭu = vayatu + uyaram + eṭi

2. kūṭṭu = vayatu + uyaram + eṭi

muṭinta āṇṭukaḷi vayatākavum, aṅkulattil uyarattiyum, pavuṇṭil eṭiyiyum kaṇakkil koḷḷa vēṇṭum.

ivvāṟu pirippataṉ nōkkam, māṇavarkaḷip pōṭṭiyil īṭupaṭutti, tiṟamiyi vaḷarppataṟkēyākum.

pōṭṭiyil paṅku koḷpavarkaḷ, taṅkaḷ tiṟamiyip paṟṟi terintu koḷvatuṭaṉ, maṟṟavarkaḷ tiṟamikkum mēlākap pōṭṭiyiṭṭu, pōrāṭi veṟṟi peṟukiṉṟārkaḷ.

veṟṟikkup paricum pārāṭṭum, matippum mariyātiyum kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa. pukaḻum perumita cātaṉiyum kiṭikkiṉṟatu.

paricukaḷiyum pārāṭṭukkaḷiyum vaḻaṅkuvatil oru marapi mēṟkoḷvatu avaciyamākiṉṟatallavā!

vili matippuḷḷa paricup poruṭkaḷit tarukiṟa poḻutu, avaṟṟi viṟṟuviṭukiṉṟa maṉitarkaḷ niṟiyatu pērkaṉ uḷḷaṉar.

ākavē, pariciṉ matippi paṇam koṇṭu aḷippatāl, appaṭippaṭṭa paricit tavirttu, piṟkālattil avi utavukiṉṟa vakikaḷil taruvatu tāṉ putti cālittaṉamāṉa ceyalākum.

1. naṟcāṉṟitaḻkaḷ vaḻaṅkutal.

2. atika viliyaṟṟa paricup poruṭkaṉi vaḻaṅkutal.

3. veṉṟa kuḻuvukku veṟṟik kēṭayaṅkaḷ aḷittal.

4. matippup palakiyil, veṟṟiyāḷarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷi, cātaṉiyuṭaṉ eḻuti vittal.

5. veṟṟiyāḷarkaḷiṉ pukip paṭattiyum, matippup palakiyil oṭṭi vittal.

uṭaṟkalviyāṉatu uṭali vaḷarkkavum, tiṟamiyi mikutippaṭuttavum, muyaṟcikkiṉṟa maṉappāṅki uyarttavum niṟiya pōṭṭikaḷil paṅku peṟavum, veṟṟi peṟavum kūṭiya vāyppukkaḷi vaḻaṅkukiṟatu.

anta vāyppukkaṉi atikappaṭutta, ārvamūṭṭa ippaṭippaṭṭa paricukaḷum pārāṭṭukkaḷum peritum uttukiṉṟaṉa. uṟcākam ūṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.





20. tērvu neṟikaḷum aḷavu muṟikaḷum

(TESTS AND MEASUREMENTS)



* * *



tērvu neṟikaḷum, aḷavu muṟikaḷum, māṇavarkaḷiṉ tiṟami, tērccipaṟṟi, teḷivākat terintu koḷḷa utavukiṉṟaṉa.

tērvukaḷ ellām māṇavarkaḷiṉ tiṟamikaḷ, valimi, nīṭittuḻikkum āṟṟal, viḷiyāṭṭu paṟṟiya aṟivu, naṭatti, paṇpāṟṟal paṟṟi terintu koḷḷa utavukiṉṟaṉa.

aḷavu muṟikaḷ ellām, māṇavarkaḷiṉ uṭal aḷavu, amippu, uyaram, eṭi, uṭal iyaṅku āṟṟal, cātaṉi paṟṟiyellām aṟintu koḷḷa utavukiṉṟaṉa.

tērvum aḷavum etaṟkāka?

1. māṇavarkaḷiṉ palattiyum palahīṉattiyum aṟintu koḷḷa.

2. māṇavarkaḷi ōriṉap pirivākappirittuk koḷḷa.

3. māṇavarkaḷ taṅkaḷ tiṟamiyit terintu koḷvatuṭaṉ, maṟṟavarkaḷ tiṟamiyuṭaṉ oppiṭṭup pārttu, uyāttikkoḷḷa.

4. māṇavarkaḷiṉ tiṟami vaḷarcciyi matippīṭu ceyya.

5. āciriyarkaḷ taṅkaḷ pōtaṉiyil, māṇavarkaḷ evvaḷavu tūram payaṉaṭintārkaḷ eṉṟu aṟintu koḷḷa.

6. mēlum, muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkāka āyvum cōtaṉiyum mēṟkoḷḷa.

nalla tērvu neṟikkāṉa aḷavu muṟi (Criteria)

1. ettaṉi muṟi tērvukaḷ naṭattiṉālum, orē viḷivi (Result) ēṟpaṭuttittarakkūṭiya, nampikkiyi (Reliability) ūṭṭuvatāka tērvu muṟi amintirukka vēṇṭum.

2. aimpulaṉkaḷālum aṟintu ceyalpaṭuttak kūṭiya tāka (Objectivity) tērvu amintiṭa vēṇṭum.

3. cikkal illāta eḷiya taṉmi (Simplicity) koṇṭatāka tērvumuṟi irukka vēṇṭum.

4. eṉṟeṉṟum māṟippōkāta niliyāṉa taṉmi (Standard) koṇṭatāka viḷaṅka vēṇṭum.

5. tērvukaḷi naṭatta oru cīrāṉa naṭi muṟikaḷiyum tiṭṭavaṭṭamāṉa takavalkaḷiyum tarak kūṭiyatāka (Standard directions) irukka vēṇṭum.

6. tērvu neṟiyāṉatu etaṉi aḷakkavēṇṭum eṉ patit teḷivāka cuṭṭikkāṭṭi, naṭattum kuṇam koṇ ṭatākavum (Validity) viḷaṅka vēṇṭum.

uṭal tiṟat tērvukaḷ (Physical Efficiency Tests)

uṭal tiṟam eṉpatu paṟṟi, pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāka āyvukaḷ ceyyappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. eṉṟālum, oru muṭinta muṭivukku yārālum vara muṭiyavilli .

amerikkāvic cērnta cārjeṉṭ (Sargent) eṉpavar, tacivalimiyāl tāṉ oruvar uṭal tiṟam vaḷarkiṟatu eṉṟu kaṇṭu piṭittuk kūṟiṉār.

taci valimiyāṉatu uṭaluṟuppukkaḷiṉ motta ceyal tiṟaṉāl vaḷarkiṟatu eṉṟu piṉṉāḷil kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭatu.

ākavē, oruvar uṭal tiṟam (Physical Fitness) uṭiyavar eṉpati, piṉvarum kūṟukaḷ koṇṭu, muṭivu ceyyalām eṉṟa oru marapu ēṟpaṭṭatu.

1. valimi (Strength)

2. vēkam (Speed)

3. nekiḻccit taṉmi (Agility)

4. nīṭittuḻikkum āṟṟal (Endurance)

5. naramput taci iṇinta ceyalāṟṟal (Neuro muscular Coordination)

ivaṟṟi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu, taṉiyāḷ uṭal tiṟat tērvu, taramāṉa potu uṭal tiṟat tērvu eṉṟu iru pirivukaḷākap pirittu, tērvu ceytu pārkkalām.

taṉiyāḷ uṭal tiṟat tērvu (Individual Physical Efficiency Test)

kīḻkkāṇum uṭaliyakka ceyalkaḷ mūlamāka, paḷḷi māṇavarkaḷiṉ uṭal tiṟaṉi tērvu ceytu pārkkalām.

1. 100 mīṭṭar ōṭṭam (vēkam).

2. uyaram tāṇṭal (nekiḻcciyum, uṟuppukkaḷ iṇinta ceyal āṟṟal)

3. nīḷam tāṇṭal (vēkam, nekiḻcci, uṟuppu ceyal āṟṟal)

4. kirikkeṭ panteṟi (valimi, iṇinta ceyalāṟṟal)

5. toṅku kampiyil ēṟi iṟaṅkal. (valimi, tacic cakti) (Pull ups)

6. 800 mīṭṭar ōṭṭam (nīṭittuḻikkum āṟṟal) ovvoru nikaḻccikkum, ivvaḷavu cātaṉi cey tāl, ittaṉi veṟṟi eṇ kiṭikkum eṉṟu kuṟip piṭṭu, tayārittuk koṇṭu ataṟkup piṟaku, tērvu ceytu, aṟiyalām.

oru mātirip paṭṭiyal

100 mīṭṭar uyaram tāṇṭal nīḷam tāṇṭal kirikkeṭ panteṟi toṅku kampiyil ēṟi iṟaṅkavum 800 mīṭṭar veṟṟi eṇ





2. taramāṉa potu uṭal tiṟat tērvukaḷ (Standard Physical Efficiency Tests)

15-vatu talippil uḷḷa oṭukaḷappōṭṭikaḷ eṉpatil, tiṟamaṟiyum pōṭṭikaḷ (Tabloid Sports) eṉṟa pakutiyil irukkiṉṟa kuṟippukaḷ, intat tērvuk kum poruntum.





inta miṉṉūlip paṟṟi


uṅkaḷukku immiṉṉūl, iṇiya nūlakamāṉa, vikkimūlattil iruntu kiṭittuḷḷatu[1].

inta iṇiya nūlakam taṉṉārvalarkaḷāl vaḷarukiṟatu. vikkimūlam patiya taṉṉārvalarkaḷi varavēṟkiṟatu. tāṅkaḷum vikkimūlattil iṇintu mēlum pala miṉṉūlkaḷi aṉivarum paṭikkumāṟu ceyyalām.

mikunta akkaṟiyuṭaṉ meyppu ceytālum, miṉṉūlil piḻi ētēṉum iruntāl tayakkam illāmal, vikkimūlattil immiṉṉūliṉ pēccu pakkattil terivikkalām allatu piḻikaḷi nīṅkaḷē kūṭa cari ceyyalām.

ippaṭippākkam, kaṭṭaṟṟa urimaṅkaḷōṭu (potukaḷa /kuṉu -Commons /GNU FDL )[2][3] ilavacamāka aḷikkappaṭukiṟatu. eṉavē, inta uriyi nīṅkaḷ maṟṟavarōṭu pakiralām; māṟṟi mēmpaṭuttalām; vaṇika nōkkattōṭum, vaṇika nōkkamiṉṟiyum payaṉpaṭuttalām

immiṉṉūl cāttiyamāvataṟku paṅkaḷittavarkaḷ piṉvarumāṟu:
